Crusader Camp
Glimpses of history can be seen in what remains of these ancient camps. The forefathers of civilization sheltered here during their Last Crusade, and these broken walls were their last holdout before their ultimate defeat.
1. Crusader camps are open for contending every 12 hours. During this period, all alliances may attack the crusader camp. Any alliance who can occupy and hold a crusader camp for 4 hours will gain control of it.
2. The first commander (captain of the rallied army) to occupy a crusader camp will become the defense captain responsible for repelling enemy attacks. If this commander is withdrawn, the commander with the highest power will automatically succeed the position.
3. Buffs from the same types of crusader camps do not stack.
4. Half of the units who are severely wounded during crusader camp battles will be sent to the hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with barbarians all severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital).

The Lost Kingdomx
The forefathers of civilization constructed eight imposing fortresses during their Last Crusade against the barbarians. Since their defeat, these once mighty structures have gradually been worn away by the passage of time.
1. There are 8 crusader fortresses in the Lost Kingdom, one in each of the eight starting provinces.
2. Governors must retake the fortresses from the barbarians and restore them to their former glory.
3. Reconstruction of crusader fortresses is divided into three stages. Upon completion of each stage, all governors from the fortress' home kingdom will receive a powerful buff. However, this buff only takes effect within the Lost Kingdom.
4. Once reconstruction is complete, the leader of the alliance controlling the fortress will be able to select a unified color for all Lost Kingdom territory controlled by alliances from their home kingdom. In addition, all governors from the fortress' home kingdom will be able to use a Territorial Teleport to teleport their city into territory belonging to any of their kingdom's alliances.
5. Once reconstruction is complete, the crusader fortress will begin providing honor for its home kingdom.
6. Half of the units who are severely wounded during crusader fortress battles will be sent to the hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with barbarians all severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital).
Hierons are where barbarian warriors go to express their devotion and demonstrate their faith. Warriors who reside in or near these structures become imbued with the mysterious power of the Lost Kingdom.
1. Hierons are open for contending every 2 days. During this period, all alliances may attack the hieron. Any alliance who can occupy and hold a hieron for 4 hours will gain control of it.
2. The first commander (captain of the rallied army) to occupy a hieron will become the defense captain responsible for repelling enemy attacks. If this commander is withdrawn, the commander with the highest power will automatically succeed the position.
3. Buffs from the same types of hierons do not stack.
4. Half of the units who are severely wounded during hieron battles will be sent to the hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with barbarians all severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital).
Only those warriors chosen by their peers for their surpassing devotion may set foot within sanctuaries. Years of solitude immersed in the unknown powers contained within have given these warriors unimaginable strength.
1. Sanctuaries are open for contending every 2 days. During this period, all alliances may attack the sanctuary. Any alliance who can occupy and hold a sanctuary for 4 hours will gain control of it.
2. The first commander (captain of the rallied army) to occupy a sanctuary will become the defense captain responsible for repelling enemy attacks. If this commander is withdrawn, the commander with the highest power will automatically succeed the position.
3. Buffs from the same types of sanctuaries do not stack.
4. Half of the units who are severely wounded during sanctuary battles will be sent to the hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with barbarians all severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital).
Circles were constructed in ancient times around sites of incredible power. They are laden with traps and guarded by dreadful wardens, but if captured will provide great powers to their captor.
1. Circles are open for contending every 2 days. During this period, all alliances may attack the circle. Any alliance who can occupy and hold a circle for 4 hours will gain control of it.
2. The first commander (captain of the rallied army) to occupy a circle will become the defense captain responsible for repelling enemy attacks. If this commander is withdrawn, the commander with the highest power will automatically succeed the position.
3. Buffs from the same types of circle do not stack.
4. Half of the units who are severely wounded during circle battles will be sent to the hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with barbarians all severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital).
Barbarian legends tell that the Great Ziggurat was once a stairway leading directly to the realm of the gods. The Ziggurat is the meeting place of the most powerful barbarian chieftains in the entire Lost Kingdom, and is guarded by their most elite warriors. For thousands of years, all those who have thought to conquer the Great Ziggurat have perished beneath its walls.
1. The Great Ziggurat is open for contending every 3 days. During this period, all alliances may attack the Ziggurat. Any alliance who can occupy and hold the Ziggurat for 8 hours will gain control of it.
2. The first commander (captain of the rallied army) to occupy the Great Ziggurat will become the defense captain responsible for repelling enemy attacks. If this commander is withdrawn, the commander with the highest power will automatically succeed the position.
3. All units who are severely wounded during Great Ziggurat battles will die immediately (during battles with barbarians all severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital).
4. The Great Ziggurat will be unsealed at the same time as its four citadels. If a citadel and the Great Ziggurat are captured by separate alliances, the citadel will launch an attack on the Great Ziggurat once every 5 minutes, dealing massive damage to the troops garrisoned within.V
Strange ruins left behind from times forgotten. Now used by the barbarians as places to perform their sacrificial rites. The barbarians encamped near here are unusually powerful.
1. Ancient ruins are open for contending once every 39 hours, and remain open for 1 hour.
2. New ruins stalkers will appear when the ruins open for contending. Defeat them to earn massive amounts of experience and abundant rewards.
3. Ancient ruins cannot be attacked directly. Instead, an alliance automatically gains control of a set of ruins once all barbarian forces and troops not belonging to the alliance have been removed from the vicinity of the ruins.
4. Once an alliance has gained control of a set of ancient ruins, commanders leading troops dispatched by alliance members will earn experience continuously for as long as they remain in the vicinity of the ruins, while their governors will earn personal honor continuously over time.
5. The barbarian defenders will regain control of the ancient ruins once the contention period has ended.
6. Each Governor can only have one troop benefit from the ancient ruins buff.N
A special type of ancient ruin reserved by the barbarians for only the most important rituals. The dark power flowing through this broken altar gives its defenders additional strength.
1. Altars of darkness open for contending every 84 hours and remain open for 2 hours.
2. New dark stalkers will appear when the altar opens for contending. Defeat them to earn massive amounts of experience and abundant rewards.
3. Altars of darkness cannot be attacked directly. Instead, an alliance automatically gains control of an altar once all barbarian forces and troops not belonging to the alliance have been removed from the altar's vicinity.
4. Once an alliance has gained control of an altar, commanders leading troops dispatched by alliance members will earn experience continuously for as long as they remain in the vicinity of the citadel, while their governors will earn personal honor continuously over time.
5. The barbarian defenders will regain control of the altar of darkness once the contention period has ended.
6. Each Governor can only have one troop benefit from the altar of darkness buff.
These heavily fortified structures stand at the four corners of the Great Ziggurat. They serve as the Ziggurat's first line of defense; if captured, they become a tremendous threat to the Ziggurat's occupants.
1. Citadels are sealed and unsealed together with the Great Ziggurat. 
2. Citadels cannot be attacked directly. Instead, an alliance automatically gains control of a citadel once all troops not belonging to the alliance have been removed from the citadel's vicinity.
3. If a citadel and the Great Ziggurat are held by separate alliances, the citadel will launch an attack on the Great Ziggurat once every 5 minutes, dealing massive damage to the troops garrisoned within. Severely wounded units will die instantly.
Passes are important strategic locations because of their key positions along interprovincial thoroughfares. They have been contested by opposing armies since the dawn of civilization.
1. Troops may not go through a pass that is not occupied by their own alliance.
2. Passes will not intercept scouts or troops that are unable to fight.
3. Level 4 passes can be "protected", "semi-protected", or "unsealed".
4. Semi-protected Level 4 passes cannot be attacked by governors from other kingdoms.
5. Unsealed Level 4 passes can be attacked by all governors, but only if their alliance's territory borders the pass.
6. You cannot teleport a city to within a certain radius of a Pass.Z
1. Alliance Crystals are a precious alliance resource used to build Crusader Flags within the Lost Kingdom.
2. Expand your alliance territory to an Alliance Crystal Field to occupy it. 
3. Alliance Crystal Fields will continuously produce Crystals for your alliance.
4. If you occupy multiple Alliance Crystal Fields, their production will stack.
Barbarians
Marauders
Marauder Encampment
Stalker
Dark Stalker
Exalted barbarians form the ruling class of barbarian society. For untold centuries they have guarded the Lost Kingdom from invasion, thriving on the mysterious power these lands possess.
1. You will only suffer minor losses from attacking barbarians. All slightly wounded units will recover once they return to the city.
2. Attacking barbarians will cost action points, but action points will recover over time.
3. After defeating barbarians, all troops that participated in the attack will receive rich rewards. Dispatching multiple troops or teaming up with your allies is a great way to maximize the rewards you receive from each barbarian.
No one knows where the marauders came from, but they are different than the enemies our kingdom has previously faced. 
1. Attacking marauders will cost action points, but action points will recover over time.
2. When marauders are defeated, all troops that participated in the attack will receive rich rewards. Dispatching multiple troops or teaming up with your allies is a great way to maximize the rewards you receive from each marauder.G
The marauders have constructed imposing strongholds all across the kingdom. Destroying them is the only way to gain entrance the Lost Kingdom.
1. You will only suffer minor losses from attacking marauder encampments. All slightly wounded units will recover once they return to the city.
2. Attacking marauder encampments will expend action points, but action points will recover over time.
3. When marauder encampments are destroyed, all troops that participated in the attack will receive rich rewards. Troops that dealt more damage to the encampment will receive greater rewards.
4. Marauder encampments are garrisoned by countless warriors, so their attack will not decrease during the course of the battle. 
5. Marauder encampments must be destroyed in a single attempt, or nearby reinforcements will immediately replenish any losses.
Stalkers belong to a secretive barbarian cult responsible for protecting each tribe's ancient ruins and altars of darkness. Cold, calculating warriors, they will take any means necessary to eliminate potential threats.
1. Stalkers will automatically attack all nearby enemy forces.
2. When stalkers are defeated, all troops that participated in the attack will receive rich rewards. Dispatching multiple troops or teaming up with your allies is a great way to maximize the rewards you receive from each stalker.
Eve of the Crusade
The Lost Secret
Prepare For Battlei
"A passage, it's a passage!" While exploring the Lost Temple, a scout stumbled upon a passage to a magnificent underground chamber, filled with the relics of our ancestors. After tireless study, our scholars came upon an exciting discovery. According to our ancestors' records, even more Holy Sites await us in the Lost Kingdom, ready to be restored. For the glory of our people and the memory of our ancestors, a massive campaign is about to begin!
1. The "Eve of the Crusade" event is divided into four stages. During each stage, you can perform certain in-game actions to earn individual crusade points and kingdom crusade points.
2. The four stages are: Kill Marauders, Train Troops, Attack Marauder Encampments, and the Announcement Stage
3. Multiple kingdoms will participate together in this event, and all event rankings will be cross-kingdom rankings.
4. Crusade points will determine each kingdom's ranking and which kingdom buffs each kingdom will receive in the Lost Kingdom.
5. If you join another kingdom's alliance, all the kingdom crusade points you've earned thus far will still belong to your original kingdom.I
Marauders, a mightier and more brutal tribe of barbarians, have begun to roam this land. Governors powerful enough to best these formidable foes have found them carrying scraps of paper with mysterious drawings on them.
1. Defeat Marauders to receive Worn Leather Satchels, which have a chance to contain Scraps of Parchment.
2. You can exchange a complete set of 7 different Scraps of Parchment for a Kingdom Supply Chest containing abundant rewards.
3. Each time you exchange a set of Scraps of Parchment for a Kingdom Supply Chest, you will receive individual crusade points and earn kingdom crusade points for your kingdom.
4. All unused items will be lost at the end of this stage, so don't forget to use them.
-------------------------------------------------------
Exchange a complete set of Scraps of Parchment: <b>7,000</b> pointsX
The scraps of parchment have revealed to us our ancient history. Our ancestors built countless Holy Sites in what was once our homeland. When the barbarians came with fire and sword, our ancestors were forced to flee, reducing our homeland to the ruins and rubble we now call the Lost Kingdom. As the heirs of their legacy, we will set out to reclaim and re-sanctify our lost homeland.
1. Train troops during the event to receive individual crusade points and earn kingdom crusade points for your kingdom.
2. Upgrading units will award points equivalent to the difference between the point rewards for each tier.
-------------------------------------------------------
Train a Tier 1 unit: <b>1</b> point  
Train a Tier 2 unit: <b>2</b> points
Train a Tier 3 unit: <b>4</b> points
Train a Tier 4 unit: <b>8</b> points
Train a Tier 5 unit: <b>20</b> points
The barbarian vanguard has arrived, and they've built massive encampments throughout the land. Our crusade must begin with their destruction.
1. Marauder Encampments will appear throughout the kingdom during this event.
2. Marauder Encampments can only be rally attacked. All governors who participate in the attack will receive the same number of individual crusade points.
3. Each Encampment destroyed will earn kingdom crusade points for your kingdom.
----------------------------------------------------------------
Destroy 1 Marauder Encampment       <b>10,000</b> points (individual)
Destroy 1 Marauder Encampment       <b>50,000</b> points (kingdom)
The Lost Kingdom
Honor Roll
Crusader Achievements
Past Glory
This magnificent kingdom has been overrun by barbarians for far too long. The ancient flames of the Last Crusade have been rekindled once more. Who will be master of the Lost Kingdom once all is said and done?
1. If your City Hall is Level 16 or higher, you can teleport into the Lost Kingdom province corresponding to your home kingdom.
2. Alliances can only build Alliance Fortresses within the Lost Kingdom. Center Fortresses cannot be built.
3. Alliances can build Crusader Flags within the Lost Kingdom. Building Crusader Flags is the only way to expand your territory within the Lost Kingdom.
4. A new type of alliance resource point will be available within the Lost Kingdom: Alliance Crystal Fields. Alliance Crystal Fields produce Crystals for your Alliance, which are an essential resource for constructing Crusader Flags.
5. After entering the Lost Kingdom, you will only be able to use Territorial Teleports or Targeted Teleports to teleport into your alliance's territory. You will be unable to teleport into unoccupied regions or ineffective territory.
6. Enemy cities cannot be attacked while located in enemy territory (excluding ineffective territory), but their troops on the map can be attacked.
7. After entering the Lost Kingdom, you can use a Random Teleport to return to your home kingdom at any time.
8. Only governors located within the Lost Kingdom can participate in the Lost Kingdom event series.
9. Barbarian troops in the Lost Kingdom will be Level 26 or higher.
In the Lost Kingdom, you, your alliance, and the rest of your kingdom will have a chance to compete directly with other kingdoms. This will be a long contest. The price of victory will be great, but the rewards will be greater still. Good luck!
<b><size=30>Past Glory</size></b>
 <color=#997336>The forefathers of civilization launched their Last Crusade in these lands, and were dealt a crushing defeat. Now, their descendants have returned to rebuild the ancient Crusader Fortresses and restore their past glory.</color>
 <b>Overview</b>
Work together to rebuild the Crusader Fortresses.
 <b>How to Play</b>
1. Past Glory is divided into 3 stages, each with different targets.
2. Complete the targets of all stages with the governors of your kingdom to rebuild your Crusader Fortress.
3. After your kingdom's Crusader Fortress is rebuilt, the territory of all your kingdom's alliances will share a unified color. In addition, you will be able to teleport your city to the territory of any alliance in your kingdom.
 <b>Rewards</b>
1. After completing each stage target, all governors in your kingdom will receive a powerful buff (these buffs will only take effect in the Lost Kingdom). 
2. Complete the quests of each stage to earn chests or item rewards.
 <b>Important Notes</b>
Even if you haven't achieved all the targets, this event will automatically end when its time elapses.
Stage 1: Ashes & Dust
Stage 2: Annihilation
Stage 3: Heroism4
The defeat of our forefathers echoes in these ruins..
Our enemies will pay for their overconfidence..
What we do here today will go down in history.
Soon a towering fortress will be constructed upon these dust-covered ruins, and we will begin our conquest of these forgotten lands.
1. Governors can donate resources to rebuild their kingdom's crusader fortress.
2. Governors will receive a Crusader's Supply Chest containing item rewards for each donation.
3. Once the current rebuilding stage is completed, the next stage will begin.
Our enemies are as powerful as ever, but our civilizations have grown much stronger. Now is the time to rewrite history!
1. Governors will be able to claim rewards for every 10 barbarian troops defeated and every 3 barbarian forts destroyed.
2. Claiming rewards will also speed up the reconstruction of your kingdom's crusader fortress. Once the current rebuilding stage is completed, the next stage will begin.
Every era has its heroes. Perhaps you will be the mighty champion of whom our descendants sing.
1. Governors can donate commander sculptures to receive Hero Medallions.
2. Hero Medallions can be exchanged for rare items.
3. Each donation will speed up the reconstruction of your kingdom's crusader fortress.
4. Once your kingdom's crusader fortress has been rebuilt, the leader of the alliance controlling the fortress will be able to select a unified color for all Lost Kingdom territory controlled by alliances from their home kingdom. In addition, the crusader fortress will begin providing honor for its home kingdom once reconstruction is complete.
5. At the end of the event, all unused Hero Medallions will be reclaimed.

At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), hierons throughout the Lost Kingdom will be activated, and {p1} hours later will be open for contending. Your alliance territory must adjoin a hieron to attack it.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), sanctuaries throughout the Lost Kingdom will be activated, and {p1} hours later will be open for contending. Your alliance territory must adjoin a sanctuaries to attack it.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), circles throughout the Lost Kingdom will be activated, and {p1} hours later will be open for contending. Your alliance territory must adjoin a circle to attack it.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), the Great Ziggurat will be activated, and {p1} hours later will be open for contending. Your alliance territory must adjoin the Ziggurat to attack it.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), Level 4 passes throughout the Lost Kingdom will be activated, and {p1} hours later will become <b>semi-protected</b>. Semi-protected passes can only be attacked by alliances from the home kingdom of the corresponding outer province. Governors from other kingdoms cannot attack semi-protected passes and will be unable to enter outer provinces corresponding to other kingdoms.Z
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), Level 5 Passes throughout the Lost Kingdom will be activated, and will be opened for contention {p1} hours later. Your alliance territory must be adjacent to a Pass to launch an attack. Alliances who successfully occupy a Pass will be able to travel to neighboring provinces.Z
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), Level 6 Passes throughout the Lost Kingdom will be activated, and will be opened for contention {p1} hours later. Your alliance territory must be adjacent to a Pass to launch an attack. Alliances who successfully occupy a Pass will be able to travel to neighboring provinces.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), Level 4 passes throughout the Lost Kingdom will be activated, and {p1} hours later will become <b>unsealed</b>. Unsealed passes can be attacked by alliances from any kingdom, and alliances who successfully occupy a pass will be able to travel into the corresponding outer province and launch a direct attack on that province's crusader fortress. (However, alliance territory must still adjoin the pass before an attack can be launched.)
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), ancient ruins throughout the Lost Kingdom will be activated, and {p1} hours later will be open for contending. Your alliance territory <b>does not</b> need to adjoin a set of ancient ruins to attack it. Expel all barbarians and troops that do not belong to your alliance from the vicinity of the ruins, and your alliance will automatically take control of the ruins.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), altars of darkness throughout the Lost Kingdom will be activated, and {p1} hours later will be open for contending. Your alliance territory <b>does not</b> need to adjoin an altar of darkness to attack it. Expel all barbarians and troops that do not belong to your alliance from the vicinity of the altar, and your alliance will automatically take control of the altar.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), Level 6 barbarian forts will be activated in the Lost Kingdom. They are garrisoned by 1,200,000 Tier 4 units and can only be attacked by rallied armies.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), Level 7 barbarian forts will be activated in the Lost Kingdom. They are garrisoned by 1,400,000 Tier 4 units and can only be attacked by rallied armies.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), Level 8 barbarian forts will be activated in the Lost Kingdom. They are garrisoned by 1,600,000 Tier 4 units and can only be attacked by rallied armies.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), Level 9 barbarian forts will be activated in the Lost Kingdom. They are garrisoned by 1,800,000 Tier 4 units and can only be attacked by rallied armies.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), Level 10 barbarian forts will be activated in the Lost Kingdom. They are garrisoned by 2,000,000 Tier 4 units and can only be attacked by rallied armies.

The Lost Kingdom will be closed some time after the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), and all governors will be transported back to their home kingdoms. The next time the Lost Kingdom opens, your kingdom will face a new set of opponents!

LIGHT AND DARKNESS
We returned to the Lost Kingdom once more, hoping to unveil more secrets within the scraps of parchment. Beneath a freshly excavated site we discovered a tunnel left behind by our forefathers. The dark passage pulsated with light as we ventured forth, and at the end of it stood a monolith inscribed with ancient runes: "When light and darkness intertwine, the gate to conflict and war shall open."
1. Each Kingdom must become a part of a team, which can hold a maximum of 4 Kingdoms.
2. Each Kingdom will be categorized into one of four types: Type A, B, C, or D. Only Kingdoms that match a team's required types may join that team.
3. After the teaming up phase ends, teams of identical types will be matched against each other based on Power, and then assigned randomly into Camp Solaria or Camp Lunaria.

When light and darkness intertwine, the gate to conflict and war shall open.
1. After the teaming up phase begins, Kingdoms can team up with each other. Each team can hold a maximum of 4 Kingdoms.
2. A King can either choose to create their own team, or join a team that has already been created.
3. Each Kingdom will be categorized into one of four types: Type A, B, C, or D. Only Kingdoms that match a team's required types are able to apply for that team.
4. A King can choose to switch teams as they see fit, or even create a team of their own until the teaming up phase ends.
5. After the teaming up phase ends, Kingdoms that do not yet belong to a team will be assigned randomly into teams, with less than 4 Kingdoms, that they meet requirements for.
6. After the teaming up phase ends, teams of identical types will be matched against each other based on their Power.
7. The two teams that are matched against each other will be assigned randomly into either Camp Solaria or Camp Lunaria.
8. You cannot migrate to a kingdom while the team it belongs to has started a matching process.

Camp Technology
1. All participating kingdoms will be randomly assigned to either Camp Solaria or Camp Lunaria. You cannot change your camp during the Lost Kingdom.
2. Governors in Camp Solaria will receive a buff during the day -- Solar Glory. Governors in Camp Lunaria will receive a buff during nighttime -- Lunar Glory. Inside the Lost Kingdom, each day lasts for 24 hours and each night lasts for 24 hours.
3. Aside from the Great Ziggurat in the center, all Holy Sites, Crusader Camps, and Crusader Fortresses can be destroyed by the enemy camp, and these structures cannot be rebuilt once destroyed. Destroying an enemy structure will award you 1 point.
4. At the end of the event, the camp with the most points will be declared the winner. If both camps have an equal number of points, then there will be no winner and both camps will be considered as defeated.
5. Alliance Fortresses cannot be built inside the center province. Alliance Flags and Alliance Fortresses cannot be built inside a province that has an Ancient Ruins or an Altar of Darkness in it.
6. Territorial Teleports and Targeted Teleports can only teleport you to your alliance's territory, or to the territory of an alliance that is from the same kingdom as your alliance.
7. Cities cannot be attacked if they are located within their alliance's effective territory.
8. Camp Solaria and Camp Lunaria can upgrade their Camp Technologies via the "Grace of Soluna" panel. Researching these technologies will grant useful bonuses to all governors within the camp.
Solar Gloryw
The day approaches. Increases the attack of Camp Solaria by 5%, and speeds up the Burning Speed of Camp Lunaria by 50%.
Lunar Gloryt
The night falls. Increases the attack of Camp Lunaria by 5%, and speeds up the Burning Speed of Camp Solaria by 50%.

Trading Rules for Scraps of Parchment
1. You can trade Scraps of Parchment you already own with other members in your alliance.
2. Trades are one for one and have no cooldown. You may trade only 1 Scrap of Parchment at a time.
3. Scraps of Parchment that are identical cannot be traded with each other.
4. Once a trade request has been started, the Scraps of Parchment being traded will be temporarily unavailable for use. You may cancel that trade request at any time

1. Your total troop power gained during this event will be tracked. Refer to the <color=#379f1f>Point Breakdown</color> for more details.
2. Your alliance will be ranked with the rest of your <color=#379f1f>Lost Kingdom</color> based on total troop power gained during the event.
 <b>Rewards</b>
1. Reach certain milestones to claim rewards from the event page.
2. Alliances will receive additional <color=#379f1f>rank rewards</color> based on their ranking at the end of the event.

<b><size=30>Now Or Never</size></b>
 <color=#997336>Time hurries along its course.</color>
 <b>Overview</b>
Use speedups to earn bountiful rewards.
 <b>Starting the Event</b>
1. Use any kind of speedups during this event to contribute to your total time saved.
2. Your alliance will be ranked with the rest of your <color=#379f1f>Lost Kingdom</color> based on the number of speedups your alliance used during the event.
 <b>Points</b>
You can only earn event points by using speedups <color=#379f1f>in your city</color>.
 <b>Rewards</b>
1. During this event, you can claim rewards from the event page once you've used speedups to save a certain amount of time.
2. Alliances will receive additional <color=#379f1f>rank rewards</color> based on their ranking at the end of the event.8
<b><size=30>Enemy Elimination</size></b>
 <color=#997336>War is coming. Take up arms or perish.</color>
 <b>Overview</b>
Complete event quests to earn bountiful rewards.
 <b>Starting the Event</b>
Earn points by severely wounding or killing enemy units during the event.
 <b>Points</b>
1. Whenever you kill or severely wound an enemy unit, you will earn points depending on the unit's tier. Refer to the <color=#379f1f>Point Breakdown</color> for more details.
2. You will be ranked with the rest of your Lost Kingdom based on the number of points you earned from killing or severely wounding enemy units in the Lost Kingdom during this event.
 <b>Rewards</b>
1. Reach certain milestones to claim rewards from the event page.
2. You will receive additional rank rewards based on your ranking at the end of the event.
 <b>Point Breakdown</b>
Severely Wound/Kill a Tier 1 Unit: 1 Point
Severely Wound/Kill a Tier 2 Unit: 2 Points
Severely Wound/Kill a Tier 3 Unit: 4 Points
Severely Wound/Kill a Tier 4 Unit: 8 Points
Severely Wound/Kill a Tier 5 Unit: 20 Points

Registered KingdomsW
Kingdoms that did not register last time will be made to do so automatically this time.
<b>Registration Rules</b>
1. Kings can choose their kingdom's story. Once the king of a kingdom has chosen a story and registered, all governors of that kingdom will be considered registered.
2. Once initial registration has ended, if your kingdom fails to find a match or the story you registered for does not meet the requirements to begin, then your kingdom will enter a by-election phase, ending the story and cancelling your kingdom's registration status. Your kingdom can register for another story during the by-election phase. By-elections will only happen once, so choose carefully!
3. There will be multiple camps in each story. Camps may be comprised of multiple kingdoms.
4. Kingdoms that do not register this time will be automatically registered for a random story during the next registration period.
5. All kingdoms entering the Season of Conquest for the first time will be automatically placed in the Heroic Anthem story. Once all kingdoms are ready, matchmaking for the story will begin.
Crusader Fortresses
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), all crusader fortresses throughout the Lost Kingdom will be activated, and {p1} hours later will be open for contending. Your coalition territory must adjoin a crusader fortress to attack it.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), all level 4 passes throughout the Lost Kingdom will be activated, and {p1} hours later will become <b>semi-protected</b>. Semi-protected passes can only be attacked by coalitions from the controlling camp. Governors from other camps cannot attack semi-protected passes, nor can they pass through to enter outer provinces corresponding to the controlling camp.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), all sanctuaries throughout the Lost Kingdom will be activated, and {p1} hours later will be open for contending. Your coalition territory must adjoin a sanctuary to attack it.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), all crusader camps throughout the Lost Kingdom will be activated, and {p1} hours later will be open for contending. Your coalition territory must adjoin a crusader camp to attack it.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), all level 5 passes throughout the Lost Kingdom will be activated, and {p1} hours later will become <b>semi-protected</b>. Semi-protected passes can only be attacked by coalitions from the controlling camp. Governors from other camps cannot attack semi-protected passes, nor can they pass through to enter outer provinces corresponding to the controlling camp.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), all level 6 passes throughout the Lost Kingdom will be activated, and {p1} hours later will become <b>semi-protected</b>. Semi-protected passes can only be attacked by coalitions from the controlling camp. Governors from other camps cannot attack semi-protected passes, nor can they pass through to enter outer provinces corresponding to the controlling camp.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), all altars of darkness throughout the Lost Kingdom will be activated, and {p1} hours later will be open for contending. Your coalition territory <b>does not</b> need to adjoin an altar of darkness to attack it. Expel all barbarians and troops that do not belong to your coalition from the area, and your coalition will automatically take control of the altar.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), all circles throughout the Lost Kingdom will be activated, and {p1} hours later will be open for contending. Your coalition territory must adjoin a circle to attack it.
7 Passes (Unsealed)
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), all level 7 passes throughout the Lost Kingdom will be activated, and {p1} hours later will become <b>unsealed</b>. Unsealed passes can be attacked by coalitions from any camp, and coalitions who successfully occupy a pass will be able to travel into the corresponding outer province and launch a direct attack on that province's circles. (However, coalition territory must still adjoin the pass before an attack can be launched.)
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), the Lost Kingdom's Great Ziggurat will be activated, and {p1} hours later will be open for contending. Your coalition territory must adjoin the Great Ziggurat to attack it.
5 Passes (Unsealed)
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), all level 5 passes throughout the Lost Kingdom will be activated, and {p1} hours later will become <b>unsealed</b>. Unsealed passes can be attacked by coalitions from any camp, and coalitions who successfully occupy a pass will be able to travel into the corresponding outer province and launch a direct attack on that province's crusader fortress. (However, coalition territory must still adjoin the pass before an attack can be launched.)!

<b><size=30>Past Glory</size></b>
 <color=#997336>The forefathers of civilization launched their Last Crusade in these lands, and were dealt a crushing defeat. Now their descendants have returned to rebuild the ancient Crusader Fortresses and restore their past glory.</color>
 <b>How to Play</b>
1. Past Glory is divided into 3 stages, each with different targets.
2. Complete the targets of all stages with the governors of your camp to rebuild your Crusader Fortress.
 <b>Rewards</b>
1. After completing each stage target, all governors in your camp will receive a powerful buff (these buffs will only take effect in the Lost Kingdom).
2. Complete the quests of each stage to earn chests or item rewards.
 <b>Important Notes</b>
Even if you haven't achieved all the targets, this event will automatically end when its time elapses.^
Upon completion of stage one, all governors from this camp will receive a special buff: Glory.^
Upon completion of stage two, all governors from this camp will receive a special buff: Mercy.e
Upon completion of stage three, all governors from this camp will receive a special buff: Discipline.B
Coalition members can teleport to any member alliance's territory.
Governor, your camp has already completed stage {p1} of rebuilding their crusader fortress. Thank you for your hard work! Please accept this small token of thanks from the entire camp.
Support SkillsL
Commanders cannot equip their own skills as support skills for their troops.
Unknown Skill
Source Commander: Unknowno
You do not yet have any information about this skill. If you send your scouts to explore, they might find some.
This journey is dangerous and unpredictable. Fortunately, the great commanders favor our cause. With their favor, we can work together to overcome obstacles and find greater glory.
1. When scouts explore the fog, they have a chance to find Commander Bastions.
2. Bastions cannot be occupied. When your coalition's territory adjoins a Bastion, governors can accept quests from the Bastion's commander.
3. Completing quests given by Bastion commanders will increase your Favor with them. When Favor with a commander reaches level 6, one of that commander's skills will be unlocked, and can be used by your troops as a support skill.
4. The level of support skills for each governor will be the same as the level of that skill on their own corresponding commander (if a governor has not yet recruited the commander in question, they will be unable to use that support skill).
5. Each troop can equip up to 2 support skills from Bastion commanders.
6. Each support skill can only be equipped by one troop at a time.
7. Every time a Story begins, available Bastion commanders and support skills will be completely different.
8. Support skills will trigger normally regardless of which commander's troops have equipped them, unless they are equipped by the troops of a commander who already has this skill.

After the story has begun, you will be able to construct a new building in your city: the Crystal Mine.d
You can "Work" the Crystal Mine to get Crystals. Working the Mine costs Food, Wood, Stone, and Gold.
Upgrading the Crystal Mine will increase the maximum amount of work you can order at a time. In addition, completing quests given by commanders will increase its Crystal output.e
The Crystals which can be mined here are a precious resource. You can use them to open up a whole new frontier of research.
You can get Crystals from: Bastion quests, your Crystal mine, the "War of Conquest" event, Lvl 41 or higher Barbarian Troops, Kahar the Hidden, Lvl 11 or higher Barbarian Forts, the Premium Season Crystal Supply, and certain bundles.L
During this story, Crystals cannot be plundered by attackers or sent as aid.
Crystals will be removed once the Season is over. Your Crystal Mine, Crystal Research Center, and any Crystal technologies you've researched will be reset and removed.J

In the "Heroic Anthem" Story, governors and their kingdoms will be grouped into different camps. Each camp must contain at least 1 kingdom. In order to guarantee balanced camps, the power of all kingdoms in each camp will be counted by the system, and these calculations will be used to arrange fair matchmaking.
Fire Camp
Warriors, honoring their ancestors' covenant, answer the king's call. Gathering at the flame altar, they swear allegiance to the ancestors and the flame. They shall be a raging, inextinguishable fire.
Earth Camp
Pious warriors rallied to their kings at the earth altar, kissing the sacred land in an oath to restore their ancestors' honor. Their knowledge of the land allows them to march effortlessly.
Water Camp
On the orders of their kings, the warriors gathered at the surge altar. Dreaming of huge waves each night, they are as relentless and unending as the sea itself.
Wind Camp
Kings lead their warriors toward the sanctum in the eye of the storm. Only those who pass the trials of the winds are worthy allies. For those that do, the blessings of the wind are well worth it.

Thank you, Governor. You have shown everyone that you are a friend and ally worthy of our trust. You have my eternal respect. May this bond last a thousand years!
1. Complete Bastion quests to earn Favor.
2. Increasing your Favor will increase the work speed of your Crystal Mine.
3. When your Favor with a Bastion commander reaches level 6, you will unlock one of their skills to use as a support skill.
4. The same commander may control more than one Bastion. Your Favor with a commander will be the same regardless of which Bastion you visit, but any resultant boosts will not stack.
5. You can accept quests from a Bastion commander only when your coalition's territory is connected to their Bastion.

Barbarian Patrol
Barbarian patrols wander around this part of the Lost Kingdom day and night. No one knows how long they have lived here. They are far more bloodthirsty and cruel than the barbarians we know. Should we encounter them, it will be a fight to the death.
1. You will only suffer minor losses from attacking barbarian patrols. All slightly wounded units will recover once they return to the city.
2. Attacking barbarian patrols will expend action points, but action points will recover over time.
3. When barbarian patrols are defeated, all troops that participated in the attack will receive rich rewards. Dispatching multiple troops or teaming up with your allies is a great way to maximize the rewards you receive from each barbarian patrol.

Unsealed passes can be attacked by coalitions from any camp, and coalitions who successfully occupy a pass will be able to travel into the corresponding province. Unit Deaths
Percentage Returned
Units Returned
Return Time: <b>{p1}</b>"
A percentage of your units killed during the Season of Conquest will be returned to you at the end of the Season.
1. During the Season of Conquest, your units killed in battles with other governors will be recorded in the Hall of Heroes. Units lost in battles outside of the Lost Kingdom or after the Season is tallied will not be recorded.
2. At the end of the Season, a portion of your dead units in the Hall of Heroes will be resurrected and returned to you. In addition, your kingdom will receive extra special immigration points based on how many Tier 4+ units it lost.
3. Units awaiting resurrection will still count towards your kingdom's power total.
4. The Hall of Heroes will record the type and level of all units killed. If you change civilizations before the end of the Season, any special units in the Hall of Heroes will be converted into your new civilization's special units when they are returned to you.
5. If you immigrate before the end of the Season, a portion of your dead units in the Hall of Heroes will be returned to you via mail.

Kahar the Hidden is a barbarian chieftain in the Lost Kingdom. His troops may not be great in number, but each one is a skilled hunter. Under Kahar's leadership, these hunters are extremely stealthy. Targets that survive long enough often think they are being attacked by ghosts.
1. You will only suffer minor losses from attacking Kahar the Hidden and his troops. All slightly wounded units will recover once they return to the city.
2. Attacking Kahar the Hidden and his troops will expend action points, but action points will recover over time.
3. After defeating Kahar the Hidden, you will get a bountiful set of trophies.
4. You must defeat Kahar the Hidden before time expires, or he and his troops will go back into hiding._

The forefathers of civilization constructed eight imposing fortresses during their Last Crusade against the barbarians. Since their defeat, these once mighty structures have gradually been worn away by the passage of time.
1. Governors must retake the fortresses from the barbarians and restore them to their former glory, turning ruined crusader fortresses into crusader fortresses.
2. Reconstruction of crusader fortresses is divided into three stages. Upon completion of each stage, all governors from the fortress' camp will receive a powerful buff. However, this buff only takes effect within the Lost Kingdom.
3. Half of the units who are severely wounded during crusader fortress battles will be sent to the hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with barbarians all severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital).
4. The coalition that occupies their crusader fortress will be able to call on one more alliance to join them.-

Map Overview%
Entering and Leaving the Lost Kingdom
Territory
Alliance Buildings
Alliance Crystal Field
Governors whose city hall is Lvl 16 or higher can go to the event page and tap the "TELEPORT" button to enter the Lost Kingdom. From there, you can tap the "HEAD HOME" button to use a Random Teleport item and teleport back to your home kingdom.
When you choose to teleport to a new location while already in the Lost Kingdom, you can only choose locations within your kingdom's territory. When your city is in alliance territory, it cannot be attacked (troops on the map but not in your city do not benefit from this protection).
In the Lost Kingdom, alliances can build only alliance fortresses and crusader flags to expand their territory. Alliance center fortresses cannot be built here.
A new type of alliance resource point will be available within the Lost Kingdom: Alliance Crystal Fields. Alliance Crystal Fields produce Crystals for your alliance, which are an essential resource for constructing Crusader Flags.
While exploring the Lost Temple, a dilapidated mural is exposed to daylight for the first time in generations. The faded drawings and text tell the tale of our ancestors being driven into exile by the barbarians. Over time, their ancient homeland has taken on a new name: "The Lost Kingdom".
Long ago, the barbarians drove out our ancestors. Now it's time to take our lands back!
Camp
Camp Power
Camp Technology
Camp Pointst
Participating kingdoms will be divided into Camp Solaria and Camp Lunaria. Camps cannot be changed during the story.
Governors in Camp Solaria receive a buff when daytime begins on the map: Solar Glory. Governors in Camp Lunaria receive a buff when nighttime begins on the map: Lunar Glory. In this season of the Lost Kingdom, day and night will last 24 hours each.
On the Camp Technology page, you can work together with other governors in your camp to upgrade your Camp Technologies. The benefits of Camp Technologies will apply to all governors in your camp.i
Aside from the Great Ziggurat, all holy sites, Crusader Camps, and Crusader Fortresses can be occupied and destroyed by the enemy camp. These structures cannot be rebuilt once destroyed. Destroying an enemy structure will award 1 Point. At the end of the event, the camp with more points wins. In the event of a tie, it will be considered a loss for both camps.;
In this story, Governors will experience brand-new content.

Resource Point
There are three types of honor points: individual honor, alliance honor, and kingdom honor. Gaining enough honor points can unlock crusader achievements and qualify you for ranking rewards in the Honor Roll page.
A mysterious building in the Lost Kingdom, which opens for contending once every 39 hours and remains open for 1 hour. Clear the surrounding area of barbarians and enemies, and your alliance will gain ownership, as well as a steady stream of honor points.
A site even more spectacular than the Ancient Ruins, which opens for contending once every 84 hours and remains open for 2 hours. Clear the surrounding area of barbarians and enemies, and your alliance will gain ownership, as well as a steady stream of honor points.
The barbarian tribes of the Lost Kingdom are stronger and more violent. The lowest barbarian troop level is 26, and even higher level barbarians lie in wait for unsuspecting invaders.
The barbarian fortresses in the Lost Kingdom are stronger and harder to attack. Governors will gradually discover Lvl 6-10 barbarian forts.f

The Great Ziggurat
An overgrown fortress which was once glorious. Occupy a crusader fortress to begin rebuilding it. Once rebuilt, Governors of that kingdom can teleport their cities to any alliance territory of the same kingdom.
The remnants of a crusader camp once held by our ancestors. Occupy it to generate honor points over time and gain buffs. Once Level 4 Passes are unsealed, you can attack other kingdoms' crusader fortresses.
The divine power one feels on these hallowed grounds cannot be described with words. Hierons will appear on the map in provinces outside Level 4 Passes, and can be occupied to acquire powerful buffs.
Even more awe-inspiring than hierons, sanctuaries are the birthplace and beating heart of faith in the divine. Occupy sanctuaries to acquire even more powerful buffs.
Such holy sites are extremely intimidating and only the truly faithful or the truly bold dare tread upon these hallowed grounds. Alliances that can occupy them will gain truly miraculous buffs.
The Great Ziggurat lies at the heart of the Lost Kingdom, and is the traditional seat of the sovereign in these lands. Once the Great Ziggurat is unlocked, control can be contested once every three days.
Kingdom Distribution
When the Lost Kingdom first opens, warriors from every kingdom will begin setting up their starting provinces. It's time to set forth for the Great Ziggurat, and victory!
The land beneath our feet is at long last free from barbarian oppression. As we reach the top of the mountain, a whole new world lies spread out before us...
We should not be confined by the borders of our continent. Soon, we shall set sail for new lands beyond the mountains.l
We journeyed to the Lost Kingdom in hopes of unveiling secrets hidden within the scraps of parchment. Beneath a freshly excavated site we discovered a tunnel. The dark passage pulsated with light as we ventured forth, and at the end of it stood a monolith inscribed with ancient runes: "When light and darkness intertwine, the gate to conflict and war shall open."

7 Passes
8 Passes
Foundation Stones can only be built near Ancient Pagodas controlled by your coalition, and allow you to teleport your city to its location once completed.
1. Foundation Stones can be built by Governors near Ancient Pagodas under their coalition's control.
2. It takes {p1} hours to build a Foundation Stone. Speedups cannot be used.
3. Governors can organize a city teleport to a Foundation Stone's location, consuming the Stone. It takes {p2} hours to teleport a city, and only 1 teleport can be carried out every {p3} days.
4. The teleport will consume the Foundation Stone. You'll need to rebuild that Foundation Stone in order to use it to teleport again.
5. Foundation Stones cannot be attacked unless they are disconnected from coalition territory.
6. Governors may only use 1 Foundation Stone at a time.f
Forward Camps may only be constructed within coalition territory, and allow troops to garrison within.
1. Forward Camps can be built by Governors within coalition territory.
2. It takes 2 hours to build a Forward Camp. Speedups cannot be used.
3. Governors can dispatch troops to garrison Forward Camps at the cost of a "Marching Order", one of which is provided every {p1} minutes.
4. Routed and returning troops will prioritize going to a Forward Camp if given the option.
5. Forward Camps can heal slightly wounded units directly, and will send heavily wounded units straight away to the hospitals.
6. Available Reserves at a Forward Camp recover over time and can be expended to deploy units from their city to reinforce Camp troops. 
7. Reserve Recovery Speed will depend on the distance between the Forward Camp and the city. The further distance means a lower speed.

Derelict Pass
Derelict Passes, which cannot be attacked or traversed, are scattered around the Lost Kingdom. Governors will need to find creative and strategic ways around wherever they appear.U
"Mighty" - the first thought that entered my head when I saw it. What ants people on the ground must appear to those who climb its heights. No doubt, imposing figures once stood at the top of this pagoda and looked upon hordes of pious multitudes, envoys of both faith and prayer conjuring up miracles with fire and shadow beyond their eyes.O
This ancient pagoda is even more impressive than previous ones we have encountered. It can't have been an easy thing to have preserved these ancient stones in such good condition, imbuing the building with a strange aura of power. Many of our warriors have described a sense of renewed vigor in the presence of this "solemn" structure.i
The first time we laid eyes on the pagoda, the enveloping green moss gave it a sort of mystical aura as if we had stumbled upon some hidden secret. I cannot help but respect its builders, and the stonemasonry required to polish rocks into smooth, precise stones. What seemed traces of human habitation within turned out to be the remnants of ancient sacrifices.
The heart of the grand central hallway features a single blade carved from bone, its immaculate condition a testament to the piety of past priests. Legend tells of ceremonies before war where warriors would come to grasp the handle and pray for victory.
The pagoda is surrounded by impressive monuments which seem rather to render the structure somewhat tragic and empty by comparison. What a shame the centuries have not left us a single soul capable of explaining what or who the monuments refer to. This knowledge is gone with the wind.

The forefathers of civilization constructed eight imposing fortresses during their Last Crusade against the barbarians. Since their defeat, these once mighty structures have gradually been worn away by the passage of time.
1. There are 8 crusader fortresses in the Lost Kingdom, one in each of the eight starting provinces.
2. Governors must retake the fortresses from the barbarians and restore them to their former glory.
3. Reconstruction of crusader fortresses is divided into three stages. Upon completion of each stage, all governors from the fortress' home kingdom will receive a powerful buff. However, this buff only takes effect within the Lost Kingdom.
4. Once reconstruction is complete, the leader of the alliance controlling the fortress will be able to select a unified color for all Lost Kingdom territory controlled by alliances from their home kingdom. In addition, all governors from the fortress' home kingdom will be able to use a Territorial Teleport to teleport their city into territory belonging to any of their kingdom's alliances.
5. Once reconstruction is complete, the crusader fortress will begin providing honor for its home kingdom.
6. Crusader fortresses are open for contending every 2 days. During this period, all alliances are able to attack the fortress. Any alliance who can occupy and hold the fortress for 6 hours will gain control of it.
7. Half of the units who are severely wounded during crusader fortress battles will be sent to the hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with barbarians all severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital).
The forefathers of civilization constructed eight imposing fortresses during their Last Crusade against the barbarians. Since their defeat, these once mighty structures have gradually been worn away by the passage of time.
1. Governors must retake the fortresses from the barbarians and restore them to their former glory, turning ruined crusader fortresses into crusader fortresses.
2. Reconstruction of crusader fortresses is divided into three stages. Upon completion of each stage, all governors from the fortress' camp will receive a powerful buff. However, this buff only takes effect within the Lost Kingdom.
3. Crusader fortresses are open for contending every 2 days. During this period, all alliances are able to attack the fortress. Any alliance who can occupy and hold the fortress for 6 hours will gain control of it.
4. Half of the units who are severely wounded during crusader fortress battles will be sent to the hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with barbarians all severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital).
5. The coalition that occupies their crusader fortress will be able to call on one more alliance to join them.
<b><size=30>Past Glory</size></b>
 <color=#997336>The forefathers of civilization launched their Last Crusade in these lands, and were dealt a crushing defeat. Now their descendants have returned to rebuild the ancient Crusader Fortresses and restore their past glory.</color>
 <b>Overview</b>
Work together to rebuild the Crusader Fortresses.
 <b>How to Play</b>
1. Past Glory is divided into 3 stages, each with different targets.
2. Complete the targets of all stages with the governors of your camp to rebuild your Crusader Fortress.
 <b>Rewards</b>
1. After completing each stage target, all governors in your camp will receive a powerful buff (these buffs will only take effect in the Lost Kingdom).
2. Complete the quests of each stage to earn chests or item rewards.
 <b>Important Notes</b>
Even if you haven't achieved all the targets, this event will automatically end when its time elapses. You will still benefit from any buffs you have already earned, and will gain the buff for the stage you were currently working towards.
<b><size=30>Past Glory</size></b>
 <color=#997336>The forefathers of civilization launched their Last Crusade in these lands, and were dealt a crushing defeat. Now, their descendants have returned to rebuild the ancient Crusader Fortresses and restore their past glory.</color>
 <b>Overview</b>
Work together to rebuild the Crusader Fortresses.
 <b>How to Play</b>
1. Past Glory is divided into 3 stages, each with different targets.
2. Complete the targets of all stages with the governors of your kingdom to rebuild your Crusader Fortress.
3. After your kingdom's Crusader Fortress is rebuilt, the territory of all your kingdom's alliances will share a unified color. In addition, you will be able to teleport your city to the territory of any alliance in your kingdom.
 <b>Rewards</b>
1. After completing each stage target, all governors in your kingdom will receive a powerful buff (these buffs will only take effect in the Lost Kingdom). 
2. Complete the quests of each stage to earn chests or item rewards.
 <b>Important Notes</b>
Even if you haven't achieved all the targets, this event will automatically end when its time elapses. You will still benefit from any buffs you have already earned, and will gain the buff for the stage you were currently working towards.

PIONEER OVERVIEW
Rules
Special Rewards
Welcome to our "Pioneer" system, where adventurous governors can sign up to obtain an early preview of future story content and gain special rewards.
Developer Q&A 
Why bring in a "Pioneer" system?<
What are the sorts of things we'll get to see in this story?
We've added a new special terrain type: the <b>Sea of Death</b>, new structures - <b>Sandswept Outposts</b> and <b>Ancient Outposts</b>, a new coalition role - the <b>General</b>, and a new item - the <b>Healing Potion</b> to the Desert Conquest story.
-The new <b>Sea of Death</b> terrain is a flat and barren contested region entirely lacking in resource points and Barbarian Forts. Furthermore, you cannot <b>teleport</b> into the Sea of Death nor build there.
-There are two types of Outposts in the Sea of Death: <b>Sandswept Outposts</b> and <b>Ancient Outposts</b>, both of which can be occupied by any camp. You must make good use of these two fortifications to progress strategically through the barren waste and occupy more Passes, Colossi and Holy Sites. Specifically, you have the option of advancing gradually through an adjoining network of Sandswept Outposts or getting straight to the point by attacking an Ancient Outpost from anywhere accessible on the map.
-We've added a new role within each coalition, the <b>General</b>, responsible for overseeing each Outpost. The coalition that occupies an Outpost can appoint any governor as the Outpost's General. Any coalition member can choose to assemble their troops at a Outpost, which will grant Outpost's General the right to garrison, rally, and dispatch these troops however they see fit.
-<b>Healing Potions</b> are a new resource type that can be given to you by your camp or won by battling enemy troops. You can use Healing Potions to <b>heal severely wounded units</b> in your Hospital.
We are looking forward to seeing the exciting strategic outcomes you will create by skillfully using these special terrain types and structures.
What sort of different or new rewards can governors earn in this story?
This story will have a <b>Prestige Point</b> leaderboard instead of the Honor Point leaderboard in other stories.
In this story, you can earn Prestige Points by severely wounding or killing enemy troops. Coalitions and camps can also earn continuous Prestige Points by occupying map structures such as Holy Sites and Colossi. You can earn significant rewards, including Soluna Coins, equipment materials, and commander sculptures based on your Prestige Point leaderboard ranking at the end of the Season.
We may have removed the Honor Point leaderboard for this story, but governors with enough Honor Points will still be able to claim Crusader Achievement rewards.)
Where can I give suggestions or feedback?
You can share any thoughts or issues with us by going to Settings -> Customer Support. We look carefully at all our governors' suggestions, and will be sure to issue an in-game reward afterwards.0
Will I receive a special reward for taking part?
Governors to take part in the new story will be eligible for a further basic Pioneer Reward and Participation Reward in addition to ordinary story rewards. Participating governors will also be entitled to a number of limited edition name plaques.
Base Rewards
Participation Rewards
Governors participating in Lost Kingdom events will be entitled to the following rewards, distributed after teleporting into the Lost Kingdom.
Governors with at least <b>10,000 Honor Points</b> by the end of the Lost Kingdom event will be entitled to the following rewards.*

<b><size=30>Lord of War</size></b> 
 <color=#997336>Defend peace with arms.</color>
 <b>Overview</b>
Complete event quests to earn bountiful rewards.
 <b>Starting the Event</b>
1. Your total troop power gained during this event will be tracked.
2. Your coalition will be ranked with the rest of your <color=#379f1f>Lost Kingdom</color> based on total troop power gained during the event.
 <b>Rewards</b>
1. Reach certain milestones to claim rewards from the event page.
2. Coalitions will receive additional <color=#379f1f>rank rewards</color> based on their ranking at the end of the event.

<b><size=30>Now Or Never</size></b>
 <color=#997336>Time hurries along its course.</color>
 <b>Overview</b>
Use speedups to earn bountiful rewards.
 <b>Starting the Event</b>
1. Use any kind of speedups during this event to contribute to your total time saved.
2. Your coalition will be ranked with the rest of your <color=#379f1f>Lost Kingdom</color> based on the number of speedups your coalition used during the event.
 <b>Points</b>
You can only earn event points by using speedups <color=#379f1f>in your city</color>.
 <b>Rewards</b>
1. During this event, you can claim rewards from the event page once you've used speedups to save a certain amount of time.
2. Coalitions will receive additional <color=#379f1f>rank rewards</color> based on their ranking at the end of the event.
This Season of Conquest story features an unusually large amount of new content. Please see the "Pioneer Overview" for more details. Register?   
I understand_
Although this Season of Conquest story's development has finished, some flaws are to be expected owing to the overall newness of the scenario. This mechanism seems to strike a good balance between providing an enjoyable scenario while facilitating feedback as we continue to iron out issues. This "Pioneer experience" is planned to be a one-off event.

The forefathers of civilization constructed eight imposing fortresses during their Last Crusade against the barbarians. Since their defeat, these once mighty structures have gradually been worn away by the passage of time.
1. Governors must retake the fortresses from the barbarians and restore them to their former glory, turning ruined crusader fortresses into crusader fortresses.
2. Reconstruction of crusader fortresses is divided into three stages. Upon completion of each stage, all governors from the fortress' camp will receive a powerful buff. However, this buff only takes effect within the Lost Kingdom.
3. Half of the units who are severely wounded during crusader fortress battles will be sent to the hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with barbarians all severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital).
4. The coalition that occupies their crusader fortress will be able to call on five more alliances to join them.
After the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), all coalitions in the Lost Kingdom will be able to recruit <b>5</b> more new alliances from their camp.
This Season's results have <color=#c88800>already been tallied</color>. You will not be able to earn any more Honor Points or complete any more quests. In addition, units killed in battle after this point will not be recorded in the Hall of Heroes.

WORLD OF ALLIANCES
Rules
Others
Registration Rules
The <color=#bd5400>alliance leader</color> holds the power to pick a Story and register at the start of this event, which will sign up all alliance members to that Story.
The leader must have a level <color=#bd5400>25</color> City Hall and the alliance must possess no fewer than <color=#bd5400>10</color> members to register for a Story.
The leader can withdraw and <color=#bd5400>re-register</color> for another Story during the registration phase, but can no longer do this once the registration phase ends.
Registration Phase
Alliances will have two Season of Conquest registration phases in which to express interest during the event period. Alliances to unsuccessfully register on the first occasion can be included in the <color=#bd5400>second registration phase</color>.
If an alliance unsuccessfully attempts registration for both phases, then that alliance must wait until all other alliances in the kingdom have <color=#bd5400>finished their Lost Kingdom events</color> before this alliance can register for subsequent events.
Matchmaking Modes
Normal Mode: If you select the same Story as the King, then you will certainly be placed in the <color=#bd5400>same Lost Kingdom</color>.
Random Mode: Even if you select the same Story as the King, you may still be placed in <color=#bd5400>different Lost Kingdoms</color>.
Regardless of the matchmaking mode selected by the alliance leader, all governors that come from the same kingdom and are matched to the same Lost Kingdom will certainly be allocated to the one faction.
Alliance Management
Governors <color=#bd5400>cannot join</color> an alliance which has been matched to a different Lost Kingdom. However, those in their home kingdom can still join alliances that have not yet registered for a Story.
Governors who have already embarked on a Lost Kingdom event <color=#bd5400>cannot join</color> an alliance currently involved in registering for a Story or in the matchmaking process.
Alliance Events
Lost Kingdom
Coalition Capacity
Subsequent SeasonsK
Once the matchmaking process has completed, governors will then be <color=#bd5400>unable to join an alliance matched to a different Lost Kingdom</color> than you.
Governors should be aware of the potential for alliance events to clash with Story registrations, and strategically plan Ark of Osiris, Osiris League, and other events.P
During the event period, alliance members who have successfully finished the matchmaking process will only be able to take part in KvK events with other governors also <color=#bd5400>matched to the same Lost Kingdom</color>. Furthermore, Camp Rankings will be displayed instead of Kingdom Rankings during the "Eve of the Crusade" event.
A coalition's upper limit of alliance members will be expanded, increasing gradually from <color=#bd5400>5</color> to <color=#bd5400>20</color>, during the event.
Subsequent Season of Conquests after two registration phases have concluded will return to the "Kingdom Registration System". If there are still alliances within a kingdom that have been successfully matched to a Lost Kingdom event that is still ongoing, then the next round of Season of Conquest can only begin once <color=#bd5400>all alliances in this kingdom have ended their current Season of Conquest</color> and returned to their home kingdom.6

Kingdoms will have one Season of Conquest registration phase in which to express interest during the event period.

After the registration phase has concluded, subsequent Season of Conquests will return to the "Kingdom Registration System". In the "{p1}" Story, governors and their alliances will be grouped into different camps. Each camp must contain at least 1 alliance. In order to guarantee fair matchmaking, the power of all alliances in each camp will be automatically balanced by the system.
During the registration phase, joining a new alliance will <color=#bd5400>automatically register</color> governors to the Story the alliance has matched to and leaving the alliance will <color=#bd5400>automatically withdraw your Story registration</color>.

Strange ruins left behind from times forgotten. Now used by the barbarians as places to perform their sacrificial rites. The barbarians encamped near here are unusually powerful.
1. Ancient ruins are open for contending once every 39 hours, and remain open for 1 hour.
2. New ruins stalkers will appear when the ruins open for contending. Defeat them to earn massive amounts of experience and abundant rewards.
3. Ancient ruins cannot be attacked directly. Instead, a coalition automatically gains control of a set of ruins once all barbarian forces and troops not belonging to the coalition have been removed from the vicinity of the ruins.
4. Once a coalition has gained control of a set of ancient ruins, commanders leading troops dispatched by coalition members will earn experience continuously for as long as they remain in the vicinity of the ruins, while their governors will earn personal honor continuously over time.
5. The barbarian defenders will regain control of the ancient ruins once the contention period has ended.
6. Each Governor can only have one troop benefit from the ancient ruins buff.P
A special type of ancient ruin reserved by the barbarians for only the most important rituals. The dark power flowing through this broken altar gives its defenders additional strength.
1. Altars of darkness open for contending every 84 hours and remain open for 2 hours.
2. New dark stalkers will appear when the altar opens for contending. Defeat them to earn massive amounts of experience and abundant rewards.
3. Altars of darkness cannot be attacked directly. Instead, a coalition automatically gains control of an altar once all barbarian forces and troops not belonging to the coalition have been removed from the altar's vicinity.
4. Once a coalition has gained control of an altar, commanders leading troops dispatched by coalition members will earn experience continuously for as long as they remain in the vicinity of the citadel, while their governors will earn personal honor continuously over time.
5. The barbarian defenders will regain control of the altar of darkness once the contention period has ended.
6. Each Governor can only have one troop benefit from the altar of darkness buff.9

These mighty Outposts dot the desert landscape like beacons of rest for weary troops. But where will these silent guardians and signposts lead us?
1. Wrest control of a Sandswept Outpost from the hands of the barbarians to gain access to all of its features.
2. If your <color=#ffA800ff>coalition</color> controls a Sandswept Outpost, all governors in your coalition can <color=#ffA800ff>assemble</color> troops there.
3. The <color=#ffA800ff>alliance leaders and titled officers</color> in your coalition can assign Generals to oversee each Sandswept Outpost or dismiss them.
4. The General in charge of a Sandswept Outpost can <color=#ffA800ff>garrison, rally, or dispatch</color> all troops assembled there.
5. Sandswept Outposts can <color=#ffA800ff>only be attacked by nearby enemies</color>. To attack a Sandswept Outpost, you <color=#ffA800ff>must first occupy another nearby Outpost</color>.
6. Rallies launched from a Sandswept Outpost have a limited range. You cannot launch a rally against a <color=#ffA800ff>Barbarian Troop or Barbarian Fort</color> from a Sandswept Outpost.
7. <color=#ffA800ff>Half</color> of the units who are severely wounded during Sandswept Outpost battles will be sent to the Hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with barbarians, all severely wounded units will be sent to the Hospital).
1. Alliance leaders and titled officers can <color=#ffA800ff>appoint</color> a member of the same coalition to the position of General of this Outpost, or <color=#ffA800ff>dismiss</color> them.
2. Any appointed governor has the right to <color=#ffA800ff>transfer</color> their General position to another governor in the same coalition.
3. Within each coalition, up to <color=#ffA800ff>{p1}</color> governors can be appointed as Generals.
4. Each governor can be appointed as General of up to <color=#ffA800ff>{p2}</color> Outposts.
Outpost
Ancient Outposts appear far older than their contemporaries, and are far easier to locate thanks to the careful records maintained by our predecessors.
1. Wrest control of an Ancient Outpost from the hands of the barbarians to gain access to all of its features.
2. If your <color=#ffA800ff>coalition</color> controls an Ancient Outpost, all governors in your coalition can <color=#ffA800ff>assemble</color> troops there.
3. The <color=#ffA800ff>alliance leaders and titled officers</color> in your coalition can assign Generals to oversee each Ancient Outpost or dismiss them.
4. The General in charge of an Ancient Outpost can <color=#ffA800ff>garrison, rally, or dispatch</color> all troops assembled there.
5. You can attack an Ancient Outpost from <color=#ffA800ff>anywhere on the map</color>, so long as there are no barriers in the way.
6. Rallies launched from an Ancient Outpost have a limited range. You cannot launch a rally against a <color=#ffA800ff>Barbarian Troop or Barbarian Fort</color> from an Ancient Outpost.
7. <color=#ffA800ff>Half</color> of the units who are severely wounded during Ancient Outpost battles will be sent to the Hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with barbarians, all severely wounded units will be sent to the Hospital).
We have followed the trail of our predecessors in search of incalculable treasure buried deep underground. This search has led us to a barren wasteland filled with abandoned structures and towering monuments to long-lost glories. The very landscape is a challenge as our troops struggle in vain against bitter desert storms that frustrate our progress. We have no doubt that any victory here must be won by gradual steps.
