The Desert Conquest story incorporates the "Sea of Death" terrain, a barren wasteland entirely devoid of resource points and Barbarian Forts. Governors cannot teleport to or build in the Sea of Death.
The Sea of Death is dotted with Sandswept Outposts. Controlling one allows your coalition to assemble troops there, providing a launchpad for attacks on nearby Holy Sites or
other Outposts.
Generals are a new governor role in the Desert Conquest story, responsible for commanding Outposts. Generals have the right to garrison, rally, dispatch, or otherwise control troops belonging to other governors within the Outpost they command.b
The colossal statues around the Sea of Death will grant bonuses and Prestige Points when occupied.
These special structures on the Desert Conquest story map can be attacked from anywhere on the map, so long as there are no barriers in the way.k
Your Prestige Point score is a composite of your Individual Prestige Points, Coalition Prestige Points, and Camp Prestige Points. Prestige Points can be earned by killing/severely wounding enemy troops or occupying Colossi and Holy Sites. Earning enough Prestige Points will allow you to claim rewards from Crusader Achievements and the Prestige List leaderboard.r
A camp reward distributed to governors that can be used to directly heal severely wounded units in your Hospitals.t
A good war horse is far more than a steed for traversing desert battlefields, but a great mark of wealth and power. The impressive warhorse statues scattered around these wastes are a testament to that fact.
1. Colossi will become vulnerable for 1 hour every 35 hours, during which any alliance will be able to attack the colossus if they so choose.
2. The first commander (captain of the rallied army) to occupy a colossus will become the defense captain responsible for repelling enemy attacks. If this commander is withdrawn, the commander with the highest power will automatically succeed the position.
3. Buffs from the same types of colossi can stack.
4. Half of the units who are severely wounded during colossus battles will be sent to the hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with barbarians all severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital)./

Lost Kingdom ends in: <b>{p1}</b>N
The forefathers of civilization constructed eight imposing fortresses during their Last Crusade against the barbarians. Since their defeat, these once mighty structures have gradually been worn away by the passage of time.
1. There are 4 Crusader Fortresses in the Lost Kingdom, one in each of the four starting provinces.
2. Governors must retake the fortresses from the barbarians and restore them to their former glory.
3. Reconstruction of crusader fortresses is divided into three stages. Upon completion of each stage, all governors from the fortress' home kingdom will receive a powerful buff. However, this buff only takes effect within the Lost Kingdom.
4. Once reconstruction is complete, the leader of the alliance controlling the fortress will be able to select a unified color for all Lost Kingdom territory controlled by alliances from their home kingdom. In addition, all governors from the fortress' home kingdom will be able to use a Territorial Teleport to teleport their city into territory belonging to any of their kingdom's alliances.
5. Once reconstruction is complete, the crusader fortress will begin providing honor for its home kingdom.
6. Crusader fortresses are open for contending every 2 days. During this period, all alliances are able to attack the fortress. Any alliance who can occupy and hold the fortress for 6 hours will gain control of it.
7. Half of the units who are severely wounded during crusader fortress battles will be sent to the hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with barbarians all severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital).
You can complete the following tasks within the Lost Kingdom to earn Honor Points and Tech Tokens (neither can be earned after the Lost Kingdom ends).

Power Up 
Why bring in a "Pioneer" system?j
Although development has largely finished on this "Heroic Anthem - Power Up" event, we are still expecting some issues with the new ranged combat. The current mechanism seems to strike a good balance between providing an enjoyable scenario while facilitating feedback as we continue to iron out issues. This "Pioneer experience" is planned to be a one-off event.<
What are the sorts of things we'll get to see in this Story?
This Story offers brand new combat and diplomacy options.
New ranged strategies: Participating governors will gain access to two Story-exclusive Ceroli Chieftains. Siege Units under these two Chieftains can be directed to build Arrow Towers on the map to conduct long-range bombardments on enemy troops - an effective suppression technique.
Blockades: Your troops and other friendly troops can Blockade non-allied troops on the map while allowing free passage to allies. Blockading troops who encounter enemies can be directed to either stand their ground or launch an attack.
Coalition Diplomacy: Coalitions can ally with each other. Troops belonging to an allied Coalition will be viewed as "friendly" for the duration of the relationship. Also, any alliances under the same Coalition Union will be "allied" by default.
Alliance Arrow Towers: Alliance Leaders and Officers have the option of converting Crusader Flags into Alliance Arrow Towers, which can effectively take out nearby non-allied cities.)
Where can I give suggestions or feedback?
You can share thoughts or issues with us by going to Settings -> Customer Support. We look carefully at all our governors' suggestions, and will be sure to issue a reward afterwards.;
Will I receive a special reward for taking part in Pioneer?/
Participating governors in our Pioneer story will be eligible for Base and Participation Rewards in addition to the original rewards associated with the Story. Limited commanders included with the rewards, Wafura and Torgny, can only be deployed on this Story map and must be returned at its conclusion.
Ranged CombatF
By selecting Ranged Tactics in the new Combat Tactics system, all commanders will be able to direct siege units to build Arrow Towers on the map. Arrow Towers can conduct long-range bombardments of enemy troops
an effective suppression technique. We have also added two Story-exclusive Ceroli chieftains who will fight alongside you during this Story. Note: damage from Arrow Towers can only leave enemy troops slightly wounded. Alliance Flags can also be converted into Arrow Towers; they will reduce enemy cities' Wall Durability, but cannot inflict casualties to troops within.
Troop Blockadesy
Your troops and other friendly troops (governors in the same camp as you, or in a different camp within a coalition that has allied with your coalition) can Blockade non-allied troops on the map while allowing free passage to allies. If your troops block non-allied troops, your troops can be directed either to stand their ground with the Peaceful Mode or launch an attack with the Aggressive Mode. You can select your preferred Blockade Mode from the Armies panel.
Rallied armies will Blockade in Peaceful Mode by default, regardless of which Blockade Mode you have chosen. When Blockading, rallied armies will not actively attack.
Coalition Diplomacy
Coalitions are free to ally with each other and, once allied, the troops of both sides will view each other's troops as friendly and allow free passage. Alliances under the same Coalition will be considered allies by default.!

Alliance Arrow Tower
Alliance Leaders and Officers have the option of converting Crusader Flags into Alliance Arrow Towers, which can and will automatically attack nearby cities and reduce their wall durability regardless of Peace Shields. Targeted cities will automatically teleport back to their starting province upon reaching 0 wall durability. Note: Alliance Arrow Towers cannot attack or damage troops on the map or stationed within cities.V
When you choose to teleport to a new location while already in the Lost Kingdom, you can only choose locations within your kingdom's territory. When your city is in alliance territory, it cannot be attacked (troops on the map but not in your city do not benefit from this protection). Cities located in territory taken over by another kingdom will be forcibly teleported to their starting province. This will not affect you if you are under the effect of the Imprison king skill, and actively teleporting to another kingdom's territory (e.g. through a Random Teleport) will not trigger this effect.'

Coalitions with the same strategic objectives can now establish a friendlier and more binding union.
1. Diplomacy options are reserved for the <color=#ffA800ff>coalition leader</color>.
2. Any alliances within a coalition union will be "allied" by default.
3. Allied coalitions/alliances will view each other's troops as friendly and <color=#ffA800ff>will not block each other</color> in the Heroic Anthem - Power Up event.
4. A single coalition can form a union with up to <color=#ffA800ff>3 other coalitions</color>.
5. Creating or dissolving unions between coalitions can only be done after the waiting period ends.

Immigration Application Rulesa
During the Immigration Period, Governors can apply to immigrate to their desired kingdoms through the Lost Kingdom Immigration system, and immigrate to their new kingdom at a cost of fewer Passport Pages compared to regular immigration.
- During the Immigration Period, Governors can <color=#EE7600><b>apply to immigrate</b></color> to <color=#EE7600><b>any other kingdom within the same Lost Kingdom</b></color>. You will be notified when the king of your desired kingdom has approved your application, at which point a marker will be added to that kingdom on the <color=#EE7600><b>Immigration Application page</b></color>. Tap it to continue the immigration process. Once the Immigration Period ends, <color=#EE7600><b>all immigration applications you have submitted will be voided</b></color>.
- After approving an immigration application, the destination kingdom still has the right to revoke their approval. Please complete the immigration process as quickly as possible once your application has been approved.
- Each kingdom has an <color=#EE7600><b>immigration limit</b></color> which can be seen on the Immigration Application page. Once the limit is reached, governors can no longer apply to immigrate to that kingdom. Until a kingdom's limit has been reached, there is <color=#EE7600><b>no limit</b></color> on the number of applications you can submit to that kingdom, and no limit to the number of applications you make that can be accepted. You have the right to withdraw your application before the king has processed it.
- Governors may only carry out the Lost Kingdom Immigration process <color=#EE7600><b>once</b></color>, but this immigration process is not subject to the usual 30-day immigration cooldown period. Thus, a governor could go through the Lost Kingdom Immigration process, then go through the regular immigration process without having to wait 30 days.

A Cut Above 
Why bring in a "Pioneer" system?
Although development has largely finished on this "Heroic Anthem - Power Up" event, we are still expecting some issues with the new ranged combat, blockade, and Combat Tactics features. The current mechanism seems to strike a good balance between providing an enjoyable scenario while facilitating feedback as we continue to iron out issues. This "Pioneer experience" is planned to be a one-off event.<
What are the sorts of things we'll get to see in this story?
This story offers brand new combat and diplomacy options.
Combat Tactics: When creating troops, you can set your troop's Combat Tactics in the troop creation page. You can only choose one set of Combat Tactics at a time, and they cannot be changed after your troop has left the city. Different Combat Tactics will grant different buffs to your commanders.
Ranged Combat: By setting their Combat Tactics to Ranged Tactics, all commanders will be able to direct siege units to build Arrow Towers on the map. Arrow Towers can conduct long-range bombardments of enemy troops
an effective suppression technique. We have also added two story-exclusive Ceroli chieftains who will fight alongside you during this story.
Blockades: Your troops and other friendly troops can blockade non-allied troops on the map while allowing free passage to allies. Blockading troops who encounter enemies can be directed to either stand their ground or launch an attack. You can set blockade modes in the Armies tab.
Coalition Diplomacy: Coalitions can ally with each other. Troops belonging to an allied coalition will be viewed as "friendly" for the duration of the relationship. Also, any alliances within the same coalition will be "allied" by default.
Aggressive Rallies: When your rally target is an enemy Alliance Fortress, Crusader Flag, Pass, or Holy Site bordering your own alliance's territory, your rally will be designated as an aggressive rally, and its blockade mode will default to aggressive mode. The rally initiator must select an Alliance Flag or territory building adjacent to the rally target as the location to build their War Camp. Troops will depart for aggressive rallies from the War Camp, and automatically attack any enemy troops that blockade their march route.
Alliance Arrow Towers: Alliance Leaders and Officers have the option of converting Crusader Flags into Alliance Arrow Towers, which can quickly take out nearby non-allied cities.)
Where can I give suggestions or feedback?
You can feedback any thoughts or issues to us by going to Settings -> Customer Service. We look carefully at all our governors' suggestions, and will be sure to issue a reward afterwards.0
Will I receive a special reward for taking part?/
Participating governors in our Pioneer story will be eligible for Base and Participation Rewards in addition to the original rewards associated with the Story. Limited commanders included with the rewards, Wafura and Torgny, can only be deployed on this Story map and must be returned at its conclusion.
Aggressive Rallies
When your rally target is an enemy Alliance Fortress, Crusader Flag, Pass, or Holy Site bordering your own alliance's territory, your rally will be designated as an aggressive rally, and its blockade mode will default to aggressive mode. The rally initiator must select an Alliance Flag or territory building adjacent to the rally target as the location to build their War Camp.
Troops will depart for aggressive rallies from the War Camp, and automatically attack any enemy troops that blockade their march route.
Combat Tactics
When you form a new troop, you can select their Combat Tactics on the troop creation page. You can only choose one set of Combat Tactics at a time, and they cannot be changed after your troop has left the city. Different Combat Tactics confer different buffs to your Commanders.
Power Up
IMMIGRATION
Your troops and other friendly troops (governors in the same camp as you, or in a different camp within a coalition that has allied with your coalition) can Blockade non-allied troops on the map while allowing free passage to allies. If your troops block non-allied troops, your troops can be directed either to stand their ground with the Peaceful Mode or launch an attack with the Aggressive Mode (unless you are being blockaded by a rallied army). You can select your preferred Blockade Mode from the Armies panel.X

Lost Kingdom Immigration Management#
Kings can manage Lost Kingdom Immigration as follows:
1. You can see all applicants and manage their applications on the "Applicants" page. You can approve or reject applications, or revoke your approval of previously-approved applications.
2. After approving an application, the applicant will be notified, and may complete their immigration process any time before the Immigration Period ends.
3. After rejecting an application, the applicant will be removed from the list of applicants. This cannot be undone. However, the applicant may reapply to your kingdom.
4. After an application has been approved, but before the applicant has immigrated to your kingdom, it is possible to revoke your approval. After revoking approval, the application will remain on your applications list. You will still be able to re-approve or reject the application.
5. You can see which governors have entered your kingdom through Lost Kingdom Immigration on the Migrated Members page.
6. Each kingdom has an immigration limit. Approving an application takes up one place on the quota; revoking your approval will free up one place on the quota.
7. Once the Lost Kingdom Immigration Period ends, all immigration applications, whether approved or not, will end. Your migration quota will be reset at the beginning of the next Season.

Greetings Governor,
The Lost Kingdom Story migration feature is now active. You can apply to migrate to any kingdom within your current Lost Kingdom. Once your application is approved, you can then migrate to that kingdom at a cost of fewer Passport Pages than regular immigration. The number of Passport Pages required is determined by your Power.
Sometimes it's a good idea to visit other kingdoms and meet new people. Submit your immigration application to the kingdom of your choice! See the Lost Kingdom Immigration application page for more details.

KING OF THE NILE
King Of The Nile
Although development has largely finished on this "Heroic Anthem - Legion of Heroes" event, we are still expecting some issues with the new social features such as cross-kingdom alliance membership and Lost Kingdom immigration. We are hoping to use this as an opportunity to gather more suggestions and provide more enjoyable social gameplay. This Pioneer experience is planned to be a one-off event.
This Story offers brand new features for cooperation and interaction.
Cross-Kingdom Alliances: You can join an alliance in any kingdom in the same camp as you, and fight side-by-side with your fellow camp members whenever you like. After joining an alliance as a cross-kingdom member, you can participate in all alliance events (except Ark of Osiris and Osiris League), and enjoy the same rank privileges as other alliance members.
Alliance Crusader Achievements: Alliance-based Crusader Achievements are only available to members of that alliance. Complete their requirements to gain greater rewards.
Lost Kingdom Immigration: once Lost Kingdom ends, a 3-day Immigration Period will begin. During the Immigration Period, you may apply to immigrate to any kingdom in the same Lost Kingdom as you. Once your application is approved, you may immigrate at a cost of fewer Passport Pages than regular immigration. This immigration is not subject to the 30-day immigration cooldown.
You can share any thoughts or issues with us by going to Settings -> Customer Support. We look carefully at all our governors' suggestions, and will be sure to issue a reward afterwards.
Governors who take part in Pioneer Stories will be eligible for a further basic Pioneer Reward and Participation Reward in addition to ordinary Story rewards.E

STRATEGY EFFECTS
1. Each Strategy Tree has 4 levels: <b>Basic</b>, <b>Intermediate</b>, <b>Advanced</b>, and <b>Ultimate</b>. Before you can adopt more advanced Strategies, you must adopt a Strategy from the level before it.
2. You can only choose <b>one Strategy</b> from each level of a Strategy Tree.
3. Different Strategies cost different amounts of Strategy Points to adopt. Basic Strategies cost <b>1</b>, Intermediate Strategies cost <b>2</b>, Advanced Strategies cost <b>3</b>, and Ultimate Strategies cost <b>4</b> Strategy Points.
4. You <b>cannot undo</b> a Seasonal Strategy selection once developed, so choose wisely!
5. You can earn up to <b>17</b> Strategy Points: 12 from the Lost Kingdom Chronicles and 5 from Crusader Achievements and the "Enemy Elimination" event.

Story Overview
With the death of King Osiris, the Holy Scepter will soon fall into the hands of the mighty King Seth.
But Horus, God of the Sky and son of Osiris, leads his brave warriors into battle to thwart Seth's designs. Reiterating his right to succession, Horus hopes to carry on the glorious rule of the Age of Osiris. Seth's followers stand in his way, vowing to defend Seth's claim to the throne.
Who will become King of Ancient Egypt and take their place in history?
Camp Clash
This Story is a climactic battle between Horus and Seth, with both Camps aiming to destroy the other Camp's buildings to earn points and claim victory.
Fight alongside your allies to bring victory to your Camp!
Free Team-up
During this story's Matchmaking Phase, we will be introducing the new Kingdom Classes and Free Team-up features. Your kingdom can create or join a team as it pleases during the Team-up Phase. At the end of the Team-up Phase, kingdoms that have not teamed up will be automatically matched by the system.
Find like-minded allies, devise your strategy, and take to the battlefield to test your mettle in a fair fight!
Seasonal Strategies
This Story introduces the new Seasonal Strategies system. Governors will be able to make choices along three Strategy Trees to decide the best path for their development.
Find the best combination of Strategies that works for you to become indispensable on the battlefield!
Camp System
During the Registration and Matchmaking Phases of this Story, we will be introducing the new Kingdom Classes and Free Team-up features. Band together with your allies to defend against the enemy and strike out toward the Great Ziggurat to claim victory!
Camp Compositiong
During the Registration Phase, all registered Kingdoms will be classified from A to D, which will determine the size of their teams. A- and B-class Kingdoms will form teams of 4, while C- and D-class Kingdoms will form teams of 6.
Finally, teams with similar Power will be matched together as opponents and randomly assigned to either Camp Horus or Camp Seth.
Camp Points
The Sanctuaries and Hierons across the map have become points to be contested by both Camps, with each point belonging to one of the two Camps. Destroy the enemy Camp's Sanctuaries and Hierons to gain Camp Points.
Special Content
This Story introduces the all-new Seasonal Strategies system.
You can also find a number of holy structures on the map
occupy them to bring your Camp one step closer to victory.
Strategies
Seasonal Strategies are divided into three distinct Strategy Trees composed of 4 levels: Basic, Intermediate, Advanced, and Ultimate. Only one Strategy can be adopted from each level of a Strategy Tree.
Throughout the Season, you will gain Strategy Points. Seasonal Strategies confer powerful buffs over the course of a Season, whether it's to gathering, peacekeeping, or warfare
find the combination of Strategies that works for you!
Seth/Horus Hierons
Hierons blessed by either Seth or Horus, belonging to one of the two Camps. Occupy your own Camp's Hierons to gain persistent buffs.
Occupy an enemy Camp's Hieron for 12 hours to destroy it and gain 1 Camp Point.
Seth/Horus Sanctuaries
Sanctuaries blessed by either Seth or Horus, belonging to one of the two Camps. Occupy your own Camp's Sanctuaries to gain powerful persistent buffs.
Occupy an enemy Camp's Sanctuary for 12 hours to destroy it and gain 1 Camp Point.
The Great Ziggurat
A holy structure located in the center of the map. Only the faithful or truly bold dare tread upon these hallowed grounds. Occupy the Great Ziggurat to gain extremely powerful buffs.
Spring of Rah
Mysterious ruins blessed by Ra, God of the Sun. These ruins are open for contention once every 39 hours, and remain open for 1 hour.
When there are no Barbarians or non-friendly Coalition troops near a building, your Coalition will automatically capture it. Coalition members will continuously receive both personal and Camp Honor Points while in the vicinity.
Font of Rai
Mysterious ruins blessed by Ra, God of the Sun. These ruins are open for contention once every 84 hours, and remain open for 2 hours.
When there are no Barbarians or non-friendly Coalition troops near a building, your Coalition will automatically capture it. Coalition members will continuously receive both personal and Camp Honor Points while in the vicinity.
Crusader Camp
A temporary camp left behind by the ancestors during their expeditions here. Occupy it to gain persistent buffs and Honor Points.
Crusader Fortress"
A mighty fortress built by the ancestors during their expeditions here. Occupy all Crusader Fortresses belonging to the same Camp to start rebuilding it. Once rebuilding is complete, Governors in the same Coalition may relocate their City to the territory of any Alliance in that Coalition.C

Both Camps will receive corresponding rewards based on the final outcome of the Camp Clash event. The rankings of both Camps will be calculated separately.

Unsealed Passes can be attacked by Coalitions from any Camp, and Coalitions who successfully occupy a Pass will be able to travel into the corresponding Province. (However, Coalition territory must still adjoin the Pass before an attack can be launched.)

Matchups have been confirmed. Lost Kingdom is about to begin.
A hieron blessed by the god Seth, crowned in divine glory. From here, the God of Strength issues his proclamations. The epic war has begun.
1. Hierons are open for contention every day. During this period, all alliances may attack the hieron. Any alliance who can occupy and hold a hieron for 4 hours will gain control of it.
2. Seth hierons may be occupied and destroyed by Camp Horus, and cannot be rebuilt after being destroyed. Destroying an enemy structure will award 1 point.
3. The first commander (captain of the rallied army) to occupy a hieron will become the defense captain responsible for repelling enemy attacks. If this commander is withdrawn, the commander with the highest power will automatically become captain.
4. Buffs from the same types of hierons do not stack.
5. Half of the units who are severely wounded during hieron battles will be sent to the hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with barbarians all severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital).0
A sanctuary blessed by Seth. Only the strongest and most pious of souls may enter. The God of Strength's calls to the faithful have also summoned those who would vie for control.
1. Sanctuaries are open for contention every day. During this period, all alliances may attack the sanctuary. Any alliance who can occupy and hold a sanctuary for 4 hours will gain control of it.
2. Seth sanctuaries may be occupied and destroyed by Camp Horus, and cannot be rebuilt after being destroyed. Destroying an enemy structure will award 1 point.
3. The first commander (captain of the rallied army) to occupy a sanctuary will become the defense captain responsible for repelling enemy attacks. If this commander is withdrawn, the commander with the highest power will automatically become captain.
4. Buffs from the same types of sanctuaries do not stack.
5. Half of the units who are severely wounded during sanctuary battles will be sent to the hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with barbarians all severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital).0
A sanctuary blessed by Horus. Only the strongest and most pious of souls may enter. The God of the Sky's calls to the faithful have also summoned those who would vie for control.
1. Sanctuaries are open for contention every day. During this period, all alliances may attack the sanctuary. Any alliance who can occupy and hold a sanctuary for 4 hours will gain control of it.
2. Horus sanctuaries may be occupied and destroyed by Camp Seth, and cannot be rebuilt after being destroyed. Destroying an enemy structure will award 1 point.
3. The first commander (captain of the rallied army) to occupy a sanctuary will become the defense captain responsible for repelling enemy attacks. If this commander is withdrawn, the commander with the highest power will automatically become captain.
4. Buffs from the same types of sanctuaries do not stack.
5. Half of the units who are severely wounded during sanctuary battles will be sent to the hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with barbarians all severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital).
A hieron blessed by the god Horus, crowned in divine glory. From here, the God of the Sky issues his proclamations. The epic war has begun.
1. Hierons are open for contention every day. During this period, all alliances may attack the hieron. Any alliance who can occupy and hold a hieron for 4 hours will gain control of it.
2. Horus hierons may be occupied and destroyed by Camp Seth, and cannot be rebuilt after being destroyed. Destroying an enemy structure will award 1 point.
3. The first commander (captain of the rallied army) to occupy a hieron will become the defense captain responsible for repelling enemy attacks. If this commander is withdrawn, the commander with the highest power will automatically become captain.
4. Buffs from the same types of hierons do not stack.
5. Half of the units who are severely wounded during hieron battles will be sent to the hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with barbarians all severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital).L
This majestic Great Ziggurat is as imposing as the sun itself, and is a symbol of eternity. Heeding the call of the gods, onlookers rush towards it, but only the strongest will gain their favor.
1. The Great Ziggurat is open for contending every 3 days. During this period, all alliances may attack the Ziggurat. Any alliance who can occupy and hold the Ziggurat for 8 hours will gain control of it.
2. The first commander (captain of the rallied army) to occupy the Great Ziggurat will become the defense captain responsible for repelling enemy attacks. If this commander is withdrawn, the commander with the highest power will automatically succeed the position.
3. All units who are severely wounded during Great Ziggurat battles will die immediately (during battles with barbarians all severely wounded units will be sent to the hospital).7
This holy structure resounds with the sound of rushing water, carrying the blessings of the King of the Gods. But greedy and barbarous tribes have come to claim these blessings for their own.
1. Springs of Ra are open for contention every 39 hours and remain open for 1 hour.
2. New Stalkers will appear when the Spring opens for contending. Defeat them to earn massive amounts of experience and abundant rewards.
3. Springs of Ra cannot be attacked directly. Instead, an Alliance automatically gains control of a Spring of Ra once all Barbarian forces and troops not belonging to the Alliance have been removed from its vicinity.
4. Once an Alliance has gained control of a Spring, Commanders leading troops dispatched by Alliance members will earn experience continuously for as long as they remain in the vicinity of the Spring, while their Governors will earn personal Honor Points continuously over time.
5. Barbarians will regain control of the Spring of Ra once the contention period has ended.
6. Each Governor can only have one troop benefit from the Spring of Ra buff.
This ancient structure is enveloped in an aura of glorious splendor, and once bathed the faithful in divine blessings. Now, barbarous raiders have their sights set on it.
1. Fonts of Ra open for contention every 84 hours and remain open for 2 hours.
2. New Stalkers will appear when the Font opens for contention. Defeat them to earn massive amounts of experience and abundant rewards.
3. Fonts of Ra cannot be attacked directly. Instead, an Alliance automatically gains control of a Font once all Barbarian forces and troops not belonging to the Alliance have been removed from the Font's vicinity.
4. Once an Alliance has gained control of a Font, Commanders leading troops dispatched by Alliance members will earn experience continuously for as long as they remain in the vicinity of the Font, while their Governors will earn personal Honor Points continuously over time.
5. Barbarians will regain control of the Font of Ra once the contention period has ended.
6. Each Governor can only have one troop benefit from the Font of Ra buff. 
Why bring in a "Pioneer" system?<
What are the sorts of things we'll get to see in this Story?'
What is the schedule of this new story?*
Where can I give suggestions or feedback? 0
Will I receive a special reward for taking part?H
Though the core content of this Story is now complete, though some areas may require urgent improvement as part of a new Season. We are hoping to use this as an opportunity for Governors to get a sneak peek, to gather more suggestions and provide a more enjoyable Story. This Pioneer experience is planned to be a one-off event.[
In the "King of the Nile" story, the forces of Horus and Seth will fight for dominion over the world's greatest river. We've revamped matchmaking with new <b>kingdom classes</b> and the <b>free team-up</b> feature. In addition, you'll have access to special <b>camp buildings</b> and the new <b>Seasonal Strategies</b> system.
- During registration, kingdoms will be <b>divided into A, B, C, and D-class</b>. Kingdoms will be grouped into teams based on kingdom class. The size of each team will be based on class. A and B-class kingdoms will be formed into teams of 6, while C and D-class kingdoms will be formed into teams of 9.
Your kingdom can choose to either create or join a team, forming your own camp. Teams of approximately equal strength will be matched up as opponents, and randomly designated as either Camp Horus or Camp Seth.
- In the new Lost Kingdom, <b>Sanctuaries</b> and <b>Hierons</b> scattered across the map will become points of contention, with each building belonging to <b>one of the two camps</b>. Enemy camp Hierons and Sanctuaries can be <b>destroyed</b>, earning Camp Points for your camp.
- In addition, you'll be able to utilize the all-new <b>Seasonal Strategies</b> system. Seasonal Strategies are divided into 3 unique Strategy Trees. At each level of a Strategy Tree, you can select <b>one</b> Strategy to adopt. Over the course of the Season, you will gain <b>Strategy Points</b>. Seasonal Strategies will grant you with powerful buffs for the duration of the Season, whether in gathering, peacekeeping, or warfare. Explore and discover the combination of Strategies that works for you.
In the King of the Nile story, the battle schedule will be roughly as follows (the specific days are subject to change and are only for reference):
On day 8, governors from the opposing camps will be able to encounter each other for the first time. On day 12, both camps will move into a new combat area to do battle. On day 23, another new combat area will be opened for governors to explore. On day 32, all Passes on the map will be opened. On day 34, the final battle for the King's Land will begin.
You can share any thoughts or issues with us by going to Settings -> Customer Support. We look carefully at all our governors' suggestions, and will be sure to issue an in-game reward afterwards. 
Governors who take part in the newest story will be eligible for basic Pioneer rewards and participation rewards, in addition to ordinary story rewards.

Adopting this Strategy will strengthen the effects of your rallies and garrisons. When friendly troops are near your rallied or garrisoned troops, their Movement Speed is significantly increased, allowing your rallies and garrisons to more easily replenish themselves and providing safety for friendly troops.)

Stalkers belong to a secretive Barbarian cult, obsessed with the Springs and Fonts of Ra. They have tasked themselves with "protecting" these places, and will take any means necessary to eliminate potential threats.
1. Stalkers will automatically attack all nearby enemy forces.
2. When Stalkers are defeated, all troops that participated in the attack will receive plentiful rewards. Dispatching multiple armies or teaming up with your allies is a great way to maximize the rewards you receive from each Stalker.
These Stalker cult leaders lurk in the shadows, appearing only when the sanctity of the Springs and Fonts of Ra has been seriously threatened.

2. When Stalker Warlords are defeated, all troops that participated in the attack will receive rewards. Dispatching multiple armies or teaming up with your allies is a great way to maximize the rewards you receive.A

Governor, a new round of Seasonal Strategies has begun! Allow me to provide a few basic pointers on how Seasonal Strategies work.
This area shows the <color=#ffa800>number of Strategy Points</color> you currently have. <color=#ffa800>Strategy Points</color> are used to develop Strategies.
You can select from three different Strategy classes, each with its own advantages and a powerful Ultimate. Strategies can either boost <color=#ffa800>your battlefield performance</color>, <color=#ffa800>map army strength</color>, or <color=#ffa800>support logistics</color>.
You can only develop <color=#ffa800>one</color> Strategy per level within each category. You <color=#ffa800>cannot change</color> it later, so choose wisely!
Adopt a category to gain access to <color=#ffa800>higher-level Strategies</color>. Your end goal is the category's "Ultimate Strategy".
You <color=#ffa800>cannot reset</color> your Seasonal Strategy choices within a given Season, <color=#ffa800>so choose wisely</color>!

Governors can join a coalition along with their alliance (coalitions are created by alliance leaders). Alliances must be of the same starting province to join the same coalition.

SIEGE OF ORL
In this story, you can join alliances belonging to any kingdom that is in the same Lost Kingdom camp as you, fighting shoulder to shoulder with members of that alliance. You can reach up to Rank 4 in another kingdom's alliance, and have the same rights as other members of the same rank except for restocking the alliance shop. You can also participate in all alliance events normally (except for the Ark of Osiris and the Osiris League).

Hieron of Thorns
1. For <b>3</b> days from 00:00 UTC the day after the end of the Battle Phase in the Lost Kingdom, the Lost Kingdom will enter a immigration period. You can only migrate within the Lost Kingdom <b>once</b> during the season.
2. During the immigration period, you can apply to immigrate to any kingdom <b>in the same Lost Kingdom</b>. After the king approves the application, you can move to the target kingdom.
3. Immigration requires spending Passport Pages. Compared to regular immigration outside of the season, immigrating within the same Lost Kingdom requires <b>fewer</b> Passport Pages. The reduction in the number of Passport Pages varies depending on the power of the governor, with a smaller discount for governors with higher power and a larger discount for governors with lower power.
4. In addition, all kingdoms in the Lost Kingdom will be able to accept immigrants during the season, but there is a limit to the number of immigrants they can receive. The limit of the number of immigrants a kingdom can receive is related to the overall strength of the kingdom. The higher the overall strength of a kingdom, the lower the number of immigrants it can receive. Conversely, the lower the overall strength of a kingdom, the more immigrants it can receive. For example, an Imperium Kingdom can receive only 3 immigrants, while a kingdom with lower overall strength can receive up to 100 or more immigrants. The number of immigrants a kingdom can accept will be announced at the beginning of the immigration period.
5. Lost Kingdom immigration <b>will not affect</b> out-of-season regular immigration. That is, you can immigrate within the same Lost Kingdom and then immigrate out-of-season without being limited by the regular immigration cooldown.g

Ancient Ruins
Many English soldiers have gathered here. Driving them off will be an important step towards victory.
1. Ancient Ruins will be opened for contention every 39 hours, and will remain open for 1 hour.
2. New English Guards will appear when the Ruins are opened for contention. Defeat them for massive rewards and EXP.
3. Ancient Ruins cannot be attacked directly. Instead, your coalition will automatically gain control of a Ruins when all English Guards and troops not belonging to your coalition have been cleared from the vicinity of the Ruins.
4. Once your coalition has gained control of a Ruins, so long as you have a troop in the vicinity of the Ruins, the commanders of that troop will continually gain EXP and you will continually earn Honor Points.
5. Once the contention period has ended, the English Guards will regain control of the Ancient Ruins.
6. Each governor can only have one troop benefit from an Ancient Ruins' effect at a time.

Auxiliaries
In the {p1} story, you can assign a commander as another commander's auxiliary. Each commander can have up to 3 auxiliaries. Auxiliaries will provide 1 of their skills to the commander they're supporting. More skill slots will be unlocked as the Lost Kingdom Chronicles progress.
Auxiliary Skills
Each auxiliary commander can provide 1 skill. A commander can equip up to 3 auxiliary skills. Auxiliary skills are provided at their current level. A skill must first be unlocked before it can be used as an auxiliary skill. Auxiliary skills can be used throughout the Lost Kingdom.
Writs
You must spend Writs to equip or replace a commander's auxiliary skills. You will gain more Writs over time, and can complete bastion quests to earn more. There is a limit to how many Writs you can store.1

In the {p1} Season of Conquest story, commanders can equip skills from auxiliary commanders, allowing for all-new combinations.
1. Each commander can equip up to 3 extra auxiliary skills from 3 separate auxiliary commanders.
2. Each commander has 3 auxiliary skill slots. The skills that can be equipped to each slot depend on which number slot it is:
<b>The 2-Slot can be equipped with another commander's 2nd skill</b>
<b>The 3-Slot can be equipped with another commander's 3rd skill</b>
<b>The 4-Slot can be equipped with another commander's 4th skill</b>
3. When dispatched with a troop, only primary commanders can use auxiliary skills. Secondary commanders will not be able to use their equipped auxiliary skills.
4. Auxiliary commanders cannot be dispatched. They must stop providing their skills to another commander in order to be dispatched again.
5. The 2-Slot will be available from the start. The 3-Slot and 4-Slot will be unlocked with the Lost Kingdom Chronicles.
6. The selection of available auxiliary commanders will change with the addition of new commanders and in subsequent seasons.

English Soldiers
Among the English soldiery, longbowmen were feared for their immense range and lethality. Even the most well-trained and well-armored soldiers are prone to panicking at the sight of a sky darkened by their bodkin arrows.
1. You will only suffer minor losses when attacking English Soldiers. All slightly wounded units will recover once they return to your city.
2. Attacking English Soldiers will cost action points, but action points will recover over time.

<color=#ffa800>Mottes</color> serve as lodging for the English Army, as well as storehouses and military centers. Only the most powerful <color=#ffa800>alliances</color> can hope to take on these fortifications.
1. You will only suffer minor losses when attacking Mottes. All slightly wounded units will recover once they return to your city.
2. Attacking Mottes will cost action points, but your action points will recover over time.
3. When a Motte is destroyed, all troops involved in the attack will receive rewards. Troops that dealt more damage will receive greater rewards.
4. Mottes are garrisoned by countless English soldiers, so their Attack will not decrease during the battle.
5. Mottes must be destroyed in a single attempt, or nearby English forces will immediately replenish any losses.+

2. The first commander (captain of the rallied army) to occupy a Crusader Camp will become the defense captain responsible for repelling enemy attacks. If this commander is withdrawn, the commander with the highest power will automatically succeed the position.
3. Buffs from the same types of Crusader Camps do not stack.
4. Half of the units who are severely wounded during Crusader Camp battles will be sent to the Hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with the English Army, all severely wounded units will be sent to the Hospital).[

The people of Brittany treasure their ancient land, give thanks to the vast sea, and sing praises to their fearless Celtic forefathers. These values have made the Bretons a brave and free people. When their homeland comes under threat, they do not hesitate to shed invader blood in the name of their ancestors.
Poitou
Located in western France, the fertile marshes, gentle rivers, and lush forests of Poitou have nurtured a humble but hardy people. The Poitevins have pledged to defend their oft-coveted homeland, no matter who comes knocking.
La Marche
Historically, La Marche has served to protect Poitou and the southern reaches of France from northern aggression. With new invaders marching down from the north, the Marchers are prepared to once again perform their ancient duty.

Bourbon, or Bourbonnais, is known for its gorgeous chateaus, lovely wines, and romantic atmosphere. This fertile land imparts a mellow bouquet upon its wines and a brave, resolute character upon its people. Its people are among the most pious of France, and they are ready to fight for its freedom.
Auvergne
Auvergne is named for the Arverni, a mighty Gallic tribe that once lived in the region. Valor runs in the blood of Vercingetorix, and their fearlessness forms an impregnable defense. Whether Romans or Englishmen, the Auvergnats show no kindness to invaders.

Crusader Camp
As reinforcements arrive from across France, figuring out where they can encamp has become an important consideration.
1. Crusader Camps will be opened for contention every 12 hours. During this period, all alliances may attack the Crusader Camp. Any alliance that can occupy and hold a Crusader Camp for 4 hours will gain control of it.
2. The first commander (captain of the rallied army) to occupy a Crusader Camp will become the defense captain responsible for repelling enemy attacks. If this commander is withdrawn, the commander with the highest power will automatically succeed the position.
3. Buffs from the same types of Crusader Camps do not stack.
4. Half of the units who are severely wounded during Crusader Camp battles will be sent to the Hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with the English Army, all severely wounded units will be sent to the Hospital).-
Spread across the heart of France, these fortresses house large contingents of English soldiers. Capturing them would greatly weaken the English cause.
1. After retaking a Crusader Fortress from the English Army, it must be rebuilt.
2. Reconstruction of Crusader Fortresses is divided into three stages. Upon completion of each stage, all governors from the Fortress' camp will receive a powerful buff. This buff will only take affect within the Lost Kingdom.
3. Half of the units who are severely wounded during Crusader Fortress battles will be sent to the Hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with the English Army, all severely wounded units will be sent to the Hospital).
4. The coalition that occupies their Crusader Fortress will be able to call on one more alliance to join them.
The captain of a contingent of English Guards, they represent the might and honor of England. When an Ancient Ruins or Altar of Darkness comes under serious threat, they will personally enter the fray.
1. Guard Captains will automatically attack any enemies that come near them.
2. When a Guard Captain is defeated, all troops that participated in the attack will receive rewards. Dispatching multiple troops or teaming up with your allies is a great way to maximize the rewards you receive.

Engineering Equipment
When the Lost Kingdom Season 2 event Eve of the Crusade starts, you will gain access to engineering equipment. Engineering equipment blueprints can be obtained by opening Equipment Chests in the Tavern, among other methods.y
This troop is a defender of Orl
ans. If it is disbanded, it will not be able to return to the city during this campaign.
1. For <b>3</b> days from 00:00 UTC the day after the end of the Battle Phase in the Lost Kingdom, the Lost Kingdom will enter a immigration period. You can only migrate within the Lost Kingdom <b>once</b> during the season.
2. <b>The immigration system in the Lost Kingdom</b> will be displayed on the main season page 7 days before the immigration period starts, at which time you can go view the details. Once the immigration period begins, you can apply for immigration and other immigration-related operations in the immigration system of the Lost Kingdom.
3. During the immigration period, you can apply to immigrate to any kingdom <b>in the same Lost Kingdom</b>. After the King approves the application, you can move to the target kingdom.
4. Immigration requires spending Passport Pages. Compared to regular immigration outside of the season, immigrating within the same Lost Kingdom requires <b>fewer</b> Passport Pages. The reduction in the number of Passport Pages varies depending on the power of the governor, with a smaller discount for governors with higher power and a larger discount for governors with lower power. You can check the number of Passport Pages required for immigration for different power levels on the rules page of the <b>immigration system</b>.
5. In addition, all kingdoms in the Lost Kingdom will be able to accept immigrants during the season, but there is a <b>limit</b> to the number of immigrants they can receive. The limit of the number of immigrants a kingdom can receive is related to the overall strength of the kingdom. The higher the overall strength of a kingdom, the lower the number of immigrants it can receive. Conversely, the lower the overall strength of a kingdom, the more immigrants it can receive. The number of immigrants a kingdom can receive will be calculated at 00:00 UTC on the day the immigration period starts. After it is calculated, the number will be <b>fixed</b> for the rest of the current Lost Kingdom.
6. Lost Kingdom immigration <b>will not affect</b> out-of-season regular immigration. That is, you can immigrate within the same Lost Kingdom and then immigrate out-of-season without being limited by the regular immigration cooldown.
Kings can manage Lost Kingdom Immigration as follows:
1. The number of immigrants a kingdom can receive is related to the <b>overall strength</b> of the kingdom. The final number will be calculated at 00:00 UTC on the day the immigration period starts. After it is calculated, the number will be <b>fixed</b> for the rest of the current Lost Kingdom.
2. You can see all applicants and manage their applications on the <b>"Applicants" page</b>. You can approve or reject applications, or revoke your approval of previously-approved applications.
3. <b>After approving an application</b>, the applicant and the King will be notified, and may complete the immigration process any time <b>before</b> the Lost Kingdom immigration period ends.
4. <b>If an application is rejected</b>, the applicant will be removed from the list of applicants. This cannot be <b>undone</b>. However, the applicant may reapply to your kingdom.
5. After an application has been approved, but <b>before the applicant has immigrated to your kingdom</b>, it is possible to <b>revoke</b> your approval. After revoking approval, the application will remain on your applications list. You will still be able to re-approve or reject the application.
6. You can see which governors have entered your kingdom through Lost Kingdom immigration on the <b>Migrated Members page</b>.
7. Each kingdom has an <b>immigration limit</b>. Approving an application takes up one place on the quota; revoking your approval will free up one place on the quota.
8. Once the Lost Kingdom immigration period ends, all immigration applications, whether approved or not, will <b>end</b>. Your migration quota will be reset at the beginning of the next season.

War waits for no one. Once the casus belli has been issued, swords are soon to cross.
A dim light emerged from beneath the Lost Temple. The relics within seemed to hum with potent energy, and the troves of precious metals and gems had not lost any of their luster.

Artifacts
In the {p1} story, you can unlock "artifacts", a new type of legendary equipment. When a commander equipped with an artifact is assigned as their troop's secondary commander, any skills they have that only apply to a single unit type will instead apply to a different unit type, depending on the artifact. Artifacts will only take effect in the Lost Kingdom, Sunset Canyon, and Lost Canyon events.

Why bring in a "Pioneer" system?<
What are the sorts of things we'll get to see in this story?)
Where can I give suggestions or feedback?0
Will I receive a special reward for taking part?'
What is the schedule of this new story?I
Though the core content of this story is now complete, as part of a new season there may be some areas that require urgent improvement. We are hoping to use this as an opportunity for governors to get a sneak peek, to collect feedback, and provide a more enjoyable story. This Pioneer experience is planned to be a one-off event.
In the Warriors Unbound story, you can experiment with new commander combinations. 
Artifacts: In this new story, each governor can claim a new type of legendary equipment, "artifacts". Artifacts allow you more freedom to experiment with multi-unit troops, rather than building all your troops around a single unit type. When a troop's secondary commander is equipped with an artifact, any skills or Museum Relic bonuses they have that only apply to a single unit type will instead apply to a different unit type, depending on the artifact. For example, if a commander has a skill or Museum Relic bonus that only applies to cavalry units, if they equip an infantry artifact that skill or Relic bonus will apply to infantry units instead. Note: Artifacts will only work in this story's Lost Kingdom, Sunset Canyon, and Lost Canyon. 
Building Restrictions: In this new story, Alliance Fortresses built outside of your starting province can only be built in areas bordering a Holy Site or Pass (you do not need to capture the Holy Site/Pass).
You can share any thoughts or issues with us by going to Settings -> Customer Support. We look carefully at all our governors' suggestions, and will be sure to issue an in-game reward afterwards.
Governors who take part in the newest story will be eligible for a further basic Pioneer rewards and participation rewards, in addition to ordinary story rewards.@
In the Warriors Unbound story, governors from different camps will first encounter each other on day 10. On day 21, governors will enter a new combat zone to fight governors from other camps. On day 34, the conflict will spread to the rest of the map. The final, decisive battle for the King's Land will begin on day 39.

During the immigration period, you can apply to immigrate to your desired kingdom through the Lost Kingdom immigration system using fewer Passport Pages than regular immigration.
- During the immigration period, you can <color=#EE7600><b>apply to immigrate</b></color> to <color=#EE7600><b>any other kingdom within the same Lost Kingdom</b></color>. You will be notified by mail when the king of your desired kingdom has approved your application, at which point a marker will be added to that kingdom on the <color=#EE7600><b>Immigration Application page</b></color>. Tap it to continue the immigration process. Once the immigration period ends, <color=#EE7600><b>all immigration applications you have submitted will be voided</b></color>.
- After approving an immigration application, the destination kingdom still has the right to revoke their approval. Please complete the immigration process as quickly as possible once your application has been approved.
- Each kingdom has an <color=#EE7600><b>immigration limit</b></color> which can be seen on the Immigration Application page. Once the limit is reached, governors can no longer apply to immigrate to that kingdom. Until a kingdom's limit has been reached, there is <color=#EE7600><b>no limit</b></color> on the number of applications you can submit to that kingdom, and no limit to the number of applications you make that can be accepted. You have the right to withdraw your application before the king has processed it.
- You can only carry out the Lost Kingdom immigration process <color=#EE7600><b>once</b></color>, but this immigration process is not subject to the usual 30-day immigration cooldown period. In other words, you can immigrate through the Lost Kingdom immigration process, then immigrate again through the regular immigration process without having to wait 30 days.$

Passes are important strategic locations because of their key positions along interprovincial thoroughfares. They have been contested by opposing armies since the dawn of civilization.
1. Troops may not go through a pass that is not occupied by their own coalition.
2. Passes will not intercept scouts or troops that are unable to fight.

See What Treasure They Left BehindX
Congratulations governor! You defeated the English Guards and found a hoard of treasure.
Governor, you've received an "artifact", a new type of <color=#ffa800>legendary equipment</color>. Allow me to explain how they work.
Artifacts allow you to experiment with <color=#ffa800>multi-unit troops</color>, rather than always building your troops around a single unit type.
When a <color=#ffa800>troop's secondary commander</color> is equipped with an artifact, all their skills and Museum Relic bonuses that only apply to a single unit type will instead affect a different unit type (depending on the artifact).
For example, if a <color=#ffa800>cavalry commander</color> deployed as a secondary commander is equipped with the <color=#ffa800>Artifact - Berserker's Brooch</color>, any skills or Museum Relics they have that apply solely to cavalry units will instead apply solely to infantry units.
Please note that you can only redeem <color=#ffa800>one artifact</color> per season, and it <color=#ffa800>cannot be swapped</color> afterwards, so please choose carefully.
Artifacts can only be used during the Warriors Unbound story in the <color=#ffa800>Lost Kingdom, Sunset Canyon, and Lost Canyon</color> events. I know you will make great use of this new boon!3
No auxiliary skill has been added to the {p1}-Slot.
Protected By Faith
Takes {s1}% less ranged damage.
In the {p1} story, you can unlock "artifacts", a new type of legendary equipment. When a troop's secondary commander is equipped with an artifact, any skills or Museum Relic bonuses they have that only apply to a single unit type will instead apply to a different unit type, depending on the artifact. For example, if a commander has a skill or Museum Relic bonus that only applies to cavalry units, if they equip an infantry artifact that skill or Relic bonus will apply to infantry units instead.
Artifact effects will only work in the Lost Kingdom, Sunset Canyon, and Lost Canyon. You can only claim one artifact per season, which will be lost at the end of the season.

Royal Regiments are formed of a mix of different units, allowing them to face any opponent. Their strength is matched only by their courage, and they will not hesitate to charge into the fray.&
The Royal Cavalry have sworn oaths of lifelong fealty to their king. Once they've drawn their swords and couched their lances, they won't lay them down until they've won either glory or death.
1. Royal Cavalry troops will charge at the garrison of Orl
ans. Royal Cavalry troops will gain a shield at the start of battle.
2. While their shield is active, Royal Cavalry will deal much more damage to garrisoned troops but much less counterattack damage to troops on the map.
3. Royal Cavalry counter archer units, but are countered by infantry units.C
Clad in sturdy armor, England's Royal Infantry form an impregnable defensive bulwark. The sound of their marching is not unlike the roar of a lion.
1. Royal Infantry troops are comprised of infantry units. They deal extra damage while surrounded.
2. Royal Infantry counter cavalry units, but are countered by archer units.
The longbows of England and Wales have a terrifying, and justified, reputation. The Royal Archers are the deadliest of all, able to rain down piercing bodkin arrows with pinpoint accuracy.
1. If a Royal Archers troop has most of its units remaining, it will deal direct damage to all nearby targets and deal extra counterattack damage.
2. If it has few units remaining, it will deal high direct damage to a single target troop instead of extra counterattack damage.
3. Royal Archers counter infantry units, but are countered by cavalry units.
The Royal Vanguard believe that waiting for the enemy to attack spells certain defeat. They are the first into the fray, believing that every charge brings them one step closer to victory.
1. The Royal Vanguard will lead the way for the other English forces, When attacking your walls, they will deal extra damage to garrisoned troops.
2. The more Royal Vanguard troops attacking your walls, the more extra damage your garrisoned troops will take.
The Royal Saboteurs are a secretive bunch, able to detect an enemy's weak points and exploit them. Their devotion to their king is such that they would gladly sacrifice their own lives to inflict as much damage as possible behind enemy lines.
1. Royal Saboteurs know the terrain around Orl
ans and can thus move very quickly.
2. Royal Saboteurs will appear right outside or even inside of Orl
ans. They are able to go right through our Gates and wreak havoc on the Orl
ans Guildhall.
The Royal Besiegers are armed with the most lethal siege weapons in the English Army's employ, and anyone foolish enough to approach them tends to be swiftly destroyed. They've yet to meet a city gate capable of withstanding their barrages.
1. Royal Besiegers are comprised of melee siege units. They have an aura that deals high, continuous damage to all hostile troops around them.
2. Royal Besiegers are susceptible to ranged attacks, and can be easily destroyed by Arrow Towers.
Soldiers win battles, but logistics wins wars. A lack of supplies leads to a lack of soldiers and damages the morale of the soldiers you do have. As such, attacking an enemy's supply lines is a tactic as old as warfare itself.
ans has been under siege for many moons. The arrival of our soldiers has brought not only hope, but also much-needed supplies. That said, getting those supplies to the city will not be easy.
1. Orl
ans Supply Convoys will bring supplies from outside Orl
ans to the city's defenders. They will be attacked by Royal Reivers on their way to the city. If a Supply Convoy is destroyed, its supplies will be lost.
2. If a Supply Convoy reaches the Orl
ans Guildhall, all troops defending Orl
ans will recover some of their lost units.

Why bring in a "Pioneer" system?<
What are the sorts of things we'll get to see in this story?)
Where can I give suggestions or feedback?'
What is the schedule of this new story?0
Will I receive a special reward for taking part?I
Though the core content of this story is now complete, as part of a new season there may be some areas that require urgent improvement. We are hoping to use this as an opportunity for governors to get a sneak peek, to collect feedback, and provide a more enjoyable story. This Pioneer experience is planned to be a one-off event.
In the Siege of Orl
ans story, you can use the new auxiliary commander feature to customize your commanders' skillsets.
Auxiliary Commanders: In this story, each commander can equip up to 3 extra auxiliary skills from 3 separate "auxiliary commanders". The skills that can be equipped to each of a commander's auxiliary skill slots depend on which skill slot it is:
The 2-Slot can be equipped with another commander's 2nd skill
The 3-Slot can be equipped with another commander's 3rd skill
The 4-Slot can be equipped with another commander's 4th skill
Please note that auxiliary skills can only be used in the Lost Kingdom.
Siege of Orl
ans Campaign: In this story, if your alliance or coalition is occupying the Orl
ans Guildhall and it is not currently open to contention, you can choose to start the Siege of Orl
ans campaign. In this campaign, you must defend Orl
ans by defeating waves of incoming English troops. If all defending troops are defeated or the Orl
ans Guildhall is captured, the campaign will be failed.
1. In the Siege of Orl
ans story, governors from different camps will first encounter each other around day 10. After level 4 Passes are opened, every camp can begin sending troops to their two neighboring provinces.
2. Around day 21, all camps will be able to enter the two provinces neighboring Orl
ans and fight for control of them.
3. Around day 31, preparations will begin for the battle for Orl
ans.
4. Around day 36, the level 7 Passes to Orl
ans will be opened, and the battle for the city will begin.
You can share any thoughts or issues with us by going to Settings -> Customer Support. We look carefully at all our governors' suggestions, and will be sure to issue an in-game reward afterwards.
Governors who take part in the newest story will be eligible for a further basic Pioneer rewards and participation rewards, in addition to ordinary story rewards.O

Poorly defended land? That's free real estate. The Strategist's Folio contains extra slots for leadership Stratagems, for those who want to go on the offensive.
A well-fed army is a victorious army. The Provisioner's Folio allows you to equip extra support Stratagems, ensuring you have the resources needed to face any challenge.

Wars are won by logistics, not valor. The Guardian's Folio has tons of support Stratagem slots, making it perfect for navigating complex late-Season battles.

STORM OF STRATAGEMS'
1. All participating kingdoms and domains will be classified into classes based on their overall strength.
2. S-class kingdoms and domains are so powerful that they will not have any teammates.
3. A and B-class kingdoms and domains can form teams of <b>2</b>, which must consist of 1 A and 1 B-class kingdom or domain. C and D-class kingdoms and domains can form teams of <b>4</b>, which must consist of 2 C and 2 D-class kingdoms or domains.
4. For the sake of fairness, each Lost Kingdom will consist of either only S, A, and B-class kingdoms/domains or only C and D-class kingdoms/domains.
5. Once the team-up phase ends, teams that do not have enough members will be automatically combined with each other.
6. When combining teams, the system will adhere to the team composition requirements mentioned above. For example, if you have a team of 2 C-class kingdoms/domains, you'll be combined with a team of 2 D-class kingdoms/domains.
7. Before entering the Lost Kingdom, 6 teams of similar strength will be matched against each other and divided into 6 camps.F
1. Kings and domain regents can either choose to create their own team, or join an existing team.
2. Kingdoms and domains will be divided into S, A, B, C, or D-class. Teams must consist of either 1 S-class; 1 A-class + 1 B-class; or 2 C-class + 2 D-class. Each Lost Kingdom will consist of either S, A, and B-class kingdoms/domains, or C and D-class kingdoms/domains.
3. Until the end of the team-up phase, kings and domain regents will be free to create teams, invite other kingdoms/domains to join their team, approve applications from other kingdoms/domains to join their team, remove team members, edit the team announcement, or disband their team.
4. After the team-up phase ends, the team list will be locked and the above actions will be unavailable. Teams that lack the required members will be automatically combined. The system will adhere to the team composition requirements mentioned above. For example, if your team only has 2 C-class kingdoms/domains, you will be combined with a team that consists of 2 D-class kingdoms/domains.
5. After the team-up phase, teams of the same type will be matched against each other based on overall strength.
6. In each Lost Kingdom, 6 teams of similar strength will be matched against each other and divided into 6 camps.
7. After registration, kingdoms will not be able to accept any new immigrants.
Enter a team announcement...

During registration, kingdoms and domains will be divided into A, B, C, and D-class. Kingdoms and domains will be grouped into teams based on class. A and B-class kingdoms/domains will be formed into teams of 6, while C and D-class kingdoms/domains will be formed into teams of 9.
Teams of similar power will be matched up against each other and randomly assigned to either Camp Horus or Camp Seth.

Step foot on a distant continent, where rising plumes of smoke herald a battle to come.
We have worked tirelessly to expand our territory, but in doing so have planted the seeds of war deep in this land.
Amidst a storm of conflict and calumny, we have learned to be flexible in our tactics, make new allies, and repel our enemies.
On this new battlefield, who will emerge the victor?
Stratagems
In this story, you can choose from 5 Stratagem Folios and 3 categories of Stratagems. Mix and match Stratagems to suit your personal playstyle.
Stratagem Pointsh
Stratagem Points are used to change your equipped Stratagems. They can be earned through Bastion Quests.
Counter Rallies_
Whenever one of your coalition's strongholds (e.g. Alliance Fortresses, Crusader Flags, Passes, Holy Sites, etc) or cities is targeted by an enemy rallied army, you can launch a "counter rally" against their rallied army. A counter rallied army will immediately set out once it has reached its troop capacity and all participating troops have arrived.I
Though the core content of this story is now complete, as part of a new season there may be some areas that require urgent improvement. We are hoping to use this as an opportunity for governors to get a sneak peek, to collect feedback, and provide a more enjoyable story. This Pioneer experience is planned to be a one-off event. 
Why bring in a "Pioneer" system?_
In the Storm of Stratagems story, you'll be able to experience the new team system, more in-depth strategic gameplay, and new ways to fight your enemies.
Teaming Up: Kingdoms will be divided into S, A, B, C, or D-class. Each Lost Kingdom will consist of either only S, A, and B-class kingdoms, or only C and D-class kingdoms. The king of each kingdom will be able to create or join teams with other kingdoms. Teams can consist of either 1 S-class; 1 A-class + 1 B-class; or 2 C-class + 2 D-class. In each Lost Kingdom, 6 teams of similar strength will be divided into 6 camps. You will always begin in the same starting province as the rest of your team.
Stratagems: "Stratagems" are permanent buffs you can equip to your "Stratagem Folio". There are 3 types of Stratagems and 5 Stratagem Folios to choose from. Stratagems of a certain type must be equipped to a corresponding slot in your Folio. Different Stratagem Folios have different slot setups, and new Folio types will be unlocked as the Chronicles progress. Different Stratagems have different effects, so feel free to experiment and find the combination that matches your playstyle.
Counter Rallies: Whenever one of your coalition's strongholds (e.g. Alliance Fortresses, Crusader Flags, Passes, Holy Sites, etc) or cities is targeted by an enemy rallied army, you can launch a "counter rally" against their rallied army. A counter rallied army will immediately set out once it has reached its troop capacity and all participating troops have arrived.
Alliance Arrow Towers: In this story, you will be able to turn your Alliance Flags into "Alliance Arrow Towers". Arrow Towers will automatically attack non-allied cities within range. When it reduces a city's Wall Durability to 0, the city will be automatically returned to its starting province. They will not inflict any casualties on the troops garrisoned in the city.
In the Storm of Stratagems story, the battle schedule will be roughly as follows (the specific days are subject to change and are only for reference):
On day 10, governors from the opposing camps will be able to encounter each other for the first time. On day 24, all camps will move into a new combat area to do battle. On day 35, all Passes level 8 and below will be opened. On day 38, the final battle for the King's Land will begin.
You can share any thoughts or issues with us by going to Settings -> Customer Support. We look carefully at all our governors' suggestions, and will be sure to issue an in-game reward afterwards.
Governors who take part in the newest story will be eligible for basic Pioneer rewards and participation rewards, in addition to ordinary story rewards.)

At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), all Level 6 Passes throughout the Lost Kingdom will enter a countdown, and will become <b>semi-protected</b> {p1} hours later. Semi-protected Passes can only be attacked by coalitions from the camp that the Pass belongs to. Coalitions from other camps cannot attack the Pass or use it to enter its camp's province.r
Governor, the all-new <color=#ffa800>Stratagem system</color> is now available! Allow me to show you how it works.
Stratagems provide persistent buffs to you or your troops. You can <color=#ffa800>mix and match</color> Stratagems to suit your unique playstyle.
There are <color=#ffa800>3 types of Stratagems</color> to choose from. A Stratagem can only be equipped to a slot that matches its type. <color=#ffa800>Universal slots</color> can be equipped with any type of Stratagem.B
Tap here to switch between <color=#ffa800>Stratagem types</color>.\
Tap on a Stratagem to see its detailed effects, or to equip it to a suitable Stratagem slot.
You'll periodically be able to change your equipped Stratagems. If you need to change them in a hurry, you can spend <color=#ffa800>Stratagem Points</color> to do so. Stratagem Points can be earned through <color=#ffa800>Bastion Quests</color>.
New <color=#ffa800>Stratagem Folios</color> will be unlocked as you progress through the Chronicles. Once you've unlocked a new Stratagem Folio, you can equip it here. Different Stratagem Folios have different slot setups.

In the Storm of Stratagems story, kingdoms will be able to team up with one another before entering the Lost Kingdom. Each team will be assigned to a camp. In order to ensure a balanced battlefield, teams will be automatically assigned to a camp by the system. When attacking another governor in your camp, you cannot plunder their resources.

Every era has its heroes. Perhaps you will be the mighty champion of whom our descendants sing.
1. In this event, you can donate commander sculptures to receive Hero Medallions.
2. You can obtain up to {p1} Hero Medallions. After you've obtained the maximum number of Hero Medallions, you will not receive Hero Medallions when donating commander sculptures.
3. Each donation will speed up the reconstruction of your kingdom's Crusader Fortress. You can continue donating commander sculptures to help rebuild your kingdom's Crusader Fortress even after you've obtained the maximum number of Hero Medallions.
4. Hero Medallions can be exchanged for rare items.
5. Once your kingdom's Crusader Fortress has been rebuilt, the leader of the alliance controlling the Fortress will be able to select a unified color for all Lost Kingdom territory controlled by alliances from their home kingdom. In addition, the Crusader Fortress will begin providing Honor Points for its home kingdom once it has been rebuilt.
6. At the end of the event, all unused Hero Medallions will be reclaimed.

Rules for Trading Scraps of Parchment
1. You can trade Scraps of Parchment you already own with other governors in your kingdom.
2. Trades are one for one and have no cooldown. You can only trade 1 Scrap of Parchment at a time.
3. You cannot trade a Scrap of Parchment for an identical Scrap of Parchment.
4. Once you've initiated a trade request, the Scrap of Parchment being traded will be temporarily unavailable for use. You may cancel a trade request at any time.7
You can trade Scraps of Parchment with other governors!

Play It Safe
Personal DirectivesQ
1. During each Tide, you can choose one of 4 Personal Directives to issue.
2. Directives you issue take effect immediately and last until the end of the Tide.
3. After issuing a Directive, you cannot change it for the rest of the Tide.
4. At the start of the next Tide, you will be given a new list of Personal Directives to choose from.
Overview
1. During each Tide, <b>alliance leaders and titled officers</b> can issue Alliance Directives to help direct alliance strategy.
2. Alliance Directives affect <b>all current alliance members in the Lost Kingdom</b>. Even if an alliance member benefitting from an Alliance Directive's effect leaves the alliance, they will continue to benefit from the effect, so long as they are in the Lost Kingdom.
3. Alliance Directives can be issued multiple times, but they have a cooldown and can only be used a limited number of times.
4. Whenever a new Tide begins, the available Alliance Directives will change, and alliance leaders or titled officers can choose to issue new Directives.
5. Alliance Directive cooldown times also apply to the governors that benefit from it. If you receive the effect of an Alliance Directive, you cannot receive that effect again from another alliance until the original directive's cooldown time has ended.

REDEEMG
1. In this Season, you can stock your Alliance Merit Shop with alliance supplies by gathering resources in the Lost Kingdom or by defeating Barbarian Patrols during certain Tides.
2. Alliance supplies can be redeemed using Merits, which you can earn by severely wounding/killing units belonging to other camps.
3. There is a limit on how many alliance supplies you can redeem per Tide (each Tide lasts 4 days). This limit will reset at the start of each new Tide.
4. The Alliance Merit Shop will remain open throughout the Season. Unused Merits will be lost at the end of the Season.
