<color=#000000>Tides of War</color>
TIDES OF WAR
Tides of War
Though its waters may ebb and flow, war is the ocean that rests beneath all of history.
In this world, a cause for war is not hard to come by. An enemy left unvanquished, a slight left unavenged... or even just the beckoning light of the Great Ziggurat.
Yet throughout the long, violent saga of humanity, the dream of peace has never died. It is a time for wounds to heal, a chance for civilization to flourish, and the last bastion of what makes us human.
Even the time between war and victory is not all fire and blood. Beyond killing, there are always other duties to be done, summits to surmount, and burdens to bear.
Governor, will you brave the tides of war?
Tidesy
In the Lost Kingdom story "Tides of War", the Lost Kingdom will periodically cycle through a series of phases called "Tides". You will have to navigate an ever-changing battlefield as the Tides ebb and flow.
Each Tide has its own unique mechanics and effects. As new Tides begin, you and your alliance will be able to issue unique "Directives" tailored to different playstyles.
Tide Effects
The Tides each have distinct mechanics and effects. Each Tide encourages certain playstyles and tactics.
To emerge victorious, you will need to adjust your strategy to the current Tide and plan ahead for the next.
Personal Directives
At the start of each new Tide, you will be able to issue a Personal Directive that will provide you with unique buffs. The Personal Directives you can choose from will depend on the current Tide.
Alliance Directives
Your alliance leader or officers will also be able to issue an Alliance Directive, which will affect the entire alliance. Like Personal Directives, the selection of Alliance Directives depends on the current Tide.
Bastions are spread throughout the Lost Kingdom, each of them led by a commander and their units. There are 14 commanders in total for you to find. 
Why bring in a "Pioneer" system?B
What sorts of things will we get to see in the Tides of War story?'
What is the schedule of this new story?)
Where can I give suggestions or feedback?0
Will I receive a special reward for taking part?M
Though the core content of this story is now complete, as part of a new Season there may be some areas that require urgent improvement. We are hoping to use this as an opportunity for governors to get a sneak peek, to collect feedback, and provide a more enjoyable story. This Pioneer experience is planned to be an infrequent event.
In the Tides of War story, you will get to experience the new "Tide" feature and a battlefield with ever-changing mechanics. To adapt to the changing Tides, you will be able to issue new "Personal Directives" and "Alliance Directives". 
Tides: In Tides of War, the Lost Kingdom will periodically cycle through phases called "Tides", each with their own unique mechanics, Personal Directives, and Alliance Directives.
Tide Effects: Different Tides have different effects, each encouraging certain playstyles. You'll need to adapt your strategy to the current Tide and plan ahead for the next one.
Personal Directives: At the start of each Tide, you can issue a Personal Directive for unique buffs. The Directives you can choose from will depend on the current Tide.
Alliance Directives: Your alliance leader or officers will also be able to issue Alliance Directives, which will affect the entire alliance. Like Personal Directives, the selection of Alliance Directives depend on the current Tide.
In the Tides of War story, the battle schedule will be roughly as follows (specific days are subject to change and are only for reference):
On Day 10, governors from different camps will meet for the first time. On Day 22, the camps will be able to fight for control over the Altars of Darkness. On Day 30, all camps will be able to battle all other camps. On Day 33, the final battle for the King's Land will begin.
You can share any thoughts or issues with us by going to Settings -> Customer Support. We pay close attention to all feedback we receive and will send you an in-game reward.
Governors who take part in the newest story will be eligible for basic Pioneer rewards and participation rewards, in addition to regular story rewards.

<b><size=30>Go Down In History</size></b>
A kingdom's glory goes to its governors, but who will be the most glorious of them all?
<b><size=24>Event Rules</size></b>
1. How to Trigger This Event
Non-Conquest Seasons: If your kingdom occupied the Great Ziggurat in the Lost Kingdom at any point, this event will open at the end of the season.
Season of Conquest:
- In the King of the Nile story, if your camp occupied the Great Ziggurat at the end of the Lost Kingdom, the event will open at the end of the season.
- In other stories, camps will be ranked by how many Autarch Testimonies they earned. Camps that rank in the top half will have this event at the end of the season. If two camps have the same number of Autarch Testimonies, the camp with more Honor Points takes precedence. Camps that received 0 Autarch Testimonies will not have this event.
2. Hall of Fame Seat Allocation
Non-Conquest Seasons:
The kingdom that occupies the Great Ziggurat at the end of the Lost Kingdom will receive 3 Hall of Fame seats. All other participating kingdoms will receive 1 Hall of Fame seat.
Season of Conquest:
- In the King of the Nile story, Hall of Fame seats will be given based on which coalition occupied the Great Ziggurat at the end of the Lost Kingdom. Kingdoms from the same starting province as the coalition will receive 3 Hall of Fame seats. Kingdoms in the same camp as the coalition but with different starting provinces will receive 1 Hall of Fame seat.
- In other stories, all kingdoms in the camp that controlled the Great Ziggurat at the end of the Lost Kingdom will receive 3 Hall of Fame seats. All other participating kingdoms will receive 1 Hall of Fame seat.
Season of Conquest (Domains):
If your kingdom has domains, the Go Down In History event will open for all governors in your kingdom, <b>but only governors in the most powerful domain will be eligible as candidates</b>.
3. Event Process
This event will begin on day 1 of a season's settlement period. It will be split into 3 phases: the voting phase, speech phase, and announcement phase. Each phase will last for 3 days.
4. Hall of Fame Candidates
When the season settlement period begins, all governors in the Lost Kingdom will receive a "candidate score" based on their total kills and deaths. The top 20 governors in your kingdom by candidate score will be added to the list of candidates. (If your kingdom has domains, only governors in the most powerful domain will be eligible as candidates.)
5. Voting Eligibility
When the event beings, your kingdom's top 300 governors by power are eligible to vote for Hall of Fame candidates. The top 100 governors can cast 3 votes each. All other eligible voters can cast 1 vote each.
6. Speech and Announcement Phases
After the voting phase ends, the governors (the specific number depends on how many Hall of Fame seats your kingdom received) with the most votes will be inducted into the Hall of Fame. At this time, inductees can choose to give a (written) speech. Once the speech phase ends, the inductees and their speeches will be announced and permanently added to the Hall of Fame.

VIEW
Hall of Fame Rules
1. Hall of Fame Requirements
1) Non-Conquest Seasons: If your kingdom captured the Great Ziggurat in the Lost Kingdom at any point, the Go Down In History event will open at the end of the season. You will be able to elect the governors who made the greatest contributions to your kingdom to be inducted into the Hall of Fame.
2) Season of Conquest:
- In the King of the Nile story, if your camp occupied the Great Ziggurat at the end of the Lost Kingdom, the Go Down In History event will open at the end of the season.
- In other stories, camps will be ranked by how many Autarch Testimonies they earned. Kingdoms in camps that rank in the top half will have the Go Down In History event at the end of the season. If two camps have the same number of Autarch Testimonies, the camp with more Honor Points takes precedence. Camps that received 0 Autarch Testimonies will not have the event.
2. Hall of Fame Seat Allocation
1) Non-Conquest Seasons: The kingdom that occupied the Great Ziggurat at the end of the Lost Kingdom will receive 3 Hall of Fame seats. All other participating kingdoms will receive 1 Hall of Fame seat.
2) Season of Conquest:
- In the King of the Nile story, Hall of Fame seats will be given based on which coalition occupied the Great Ziggurat at the end of the Lost Kingdom. Kingdoms from the same starting province as the coalition will receive 3 Hall of Fame seats. Kingdoms in the same camp as the coalition but with different starting provinces will receive 1 Hall of Fame seat.
- In other stories, all kingdoms in the camp that controlled the Great Ziggurat at the end of the Lost Kingdom will receive 3 Hall of Fame seats. All other participating kingdoms will receive 1 Hall of Fame seat.
3) Season of Conquest (Domains):
If your kingdom has domains, the Go Down In History event will open for all governors in your kingdom, <b>but only governors in the most powerful domain will be eligible as candidates</b>.
3. Selection Process
In the Go Down In History event, all governors that took part in the Lost Kingdom will receive a "candidate score" based on their total kills and deaths. The top 20 governors in your kingdom by candidate score will be added to the Hall of Fame candidate list. (If your kingdom has domains, only governors in the most powerful domain will be eligible as candidates.)
The top 300 governors in your kingdom by power will be able to cast votes for Hall of Fame candidates. The candidates (the specific number depends on how many Hall of Fame seats your kingdom received) with the most votes will be inducted into the Hall of Fame. Inductees can submit a written speech, which will be permanently added to the Hall of Fame along with their name.
4. Hall of Fame Information
When a governor is inducted, their nickname, avatar, alliance, and speech will be permanently recorded in the Hall of Fame. This information cannot be changed later. Other governors can like or share the inductee's Hall of Fame display.

This auxiliary skill library is for the {p1}-Slot.
1. This skill library contains the number {p1} skill of all commanders you have recruited (excluding commanders already assigned as an auxiliary commander). These skills can be equipped as auxiliary skills.
2. Equipping an auxiliary skill requires <b>Writs</b>. You will automatically gain more Writs over time, and can earn more by completing bastion quests.
3. Each commander can only provide one auxiliary skill to one other commander at a time. A commander with auxiliary skills equipped cannot provide an auxiliary skill to another commander.
4. Auxiliary skills are provided at their current level. If an auxiliary skill is modified after its commander's Expertise is awakened, it will be replaced with the Expertise version of the skill.
5. Some commanders will be unlocked as the Lost Kingdom Chronicles progress. You will only be able to assign them as auxiliary commanders after they've been unlocked.
6. The order in which commanders are unlocked will differ between different Lost Kingdoms./

VOTING RULES
1. Candidate Selection
Kingdoms With No Domains: When the season settlement period begins, all governors in the Lost Kingdom will receive a "candidate score" based on their total kills and deaths. The top 20 governors in your kingdom by candidate score will be added to the list of candidates.
Kingdoms With Domains: When the season settlement period begins, only governors in the most powerful domain will receive a "candidate score" based on their total kills and deaths. The top 20 governors in that domain by candidate score will be added to the list of candidates.
2. Voting Eligibility
The top 300 governors in your kingdom by power when this event begins will be eligible to vote for Hall of Fame candidates. The top 100 governors can cast 3 votes each. All other eligible voters can cast 1 vote each.
3. Voting Rules
When selecting candidates, if two governors have the same candidate score, the one with more deaths will take precedence. If both governors have the same number of deaths, the one with more power will take precedence.
At the end of the voting phase, if two candidates have the same number of votes, the tie breaker will be their candidate scores, followed by total deaths and power.
4. Other Rules
Candidates who emigrate out of their kingdom during the Go Down In History can still be elected to the Hall of Fame. If an emigrant governor is elected, they will still be added to the Hall of Fame, but they won't be able to submit a speech.
SPEECH RULESL
1. Speech Eligibility
During the speech phase, any governor inducted into the Hall of Fame can choose to give (or rather write) a speech to commemorate their achievement. Their speech will be permanently displayed alongside them in the Hall of Fame.
2. Speech Rules
During the speech phase, inductees can edit their speech at any time. After the speech phase ends, they will no longer be able to edit it. If an inductee does not submit a speech before time runs out, they will be entered into the Hall of Fame with no speech.
In order to maintain a safe and healthy gaming environment, speeches will be subject to review. Speeches containing sensitive or illegal content cannot be submitted. Speeches containing such content that have already been submitted will be removed.
You can also manually report speeches you believe to contain sensitive or illegal content. We will investigate the matter promptly.
3. Other Rules
At the end of the speech phase, every inductee's nickname, avatar, alliance, and speech will be permanently added to the Hall of Fame. This information cannot be changed later.

ALLIANCE BATTLE ROSTER
1. Your kingdom's king must appoint regents to manage each Lost Kingdom domain. Regents will help the king create a battle roster for their respective domains, which includes all the governors and alliances that will be allowed to fully take part in the Lost Kingdom.
2. <b>The maximum governors that can be assigned to each domain is {p1}</b>. Slots can be allocated directly by the king or via a signup period:
- Direct Allocation: The king or their regents must first allocate slots to alliances. Alliance leaders can then assign these slots to their individual members.
- Signup Allocation: The king can initiate a signup period, during which time governors can pick a domain to sign up for. Once the signup period has ended, the king can apply the signup results to the Lost Kingdom domain rosters.
3. At the end of the story registration phase, if there are any open slots left, they will be automatically assigned to the <b>governors with the highest activity points and power</b> in the kingdom that do not already have a slot. When the system automatically assigns slots, <b>no kingdom can have more than {p2} slots in a single Lost Kingdom</b>.,
You've assigned the maximum number of slots.
No alliances to show
No governors to show
Lost Kingdom Domain Rules
Story Registration Rules
Domain Restrictions
Other Rules 
1. At the start of the Season of Conquest story registration period, kingdoms will divided into classes based on activity level: S+, S, A, B, C, and D. If <b>your kingdom is S or S+</b>, it will be automatically split into multiple Lost Kingdom "domains". Each kingdom can be split into up to {p2} domains.
2. Each domain will take part in the upcoming Lost Kingdom independently, including matchmaking and actual combat. <b>Domains from the same kingdom will not be placed in the same Lost Kingdom</b>.
3. Your kingdom's king must appoint regents to manage each Lost Kingdom domain. Regents will help the king create a battle roster for their respective domains, which includes all the governors and alliances that will be allowed to fully take part in the Lost Kingdom.
4. <b>The maximum governors that can be assigned to each domain is {p3}</b>. Slots can be allocated directly by the king or via a signup period:
- Direct Allocation: The king or their regents must first allocate slots to alliances. Alliance leaders can then assign these slots to their individual members. The king and regents cannot revoke a slot from an alliance if the slot has already been assigned to an alliance member. The alliance must first unassign the slot before it can be revoked.
- Signup Allocation: The king can initiate a signup period, during which time governors can pick a domain to sign up for. Once the signup period has ended, the king can apply the signup results to the Lost Kingdom domain rosters.
5. At the end of the story registration period, a battle roster of alliances and governors will be automatically created for each domain.
- Any unassigned slots will be automatically given to governors in the kingdom who do not already have a slot, starting with the governors with the highest activity points and power.
- Alliances that have not been assigned to a domain will be automatically assigned to one based on the activity points of each domain. The highest-power alliances will be assigned first.
6. <b>No kingdom can have more than {p4} slots in a single Lost Kingdom</b>.\
1. Kings can <b>choose their kingdom's story</b>. Once the king of a kingdom has chosen a story and registered for it, all governors in that kingdom will be considered registered.
2. Once initial registration has ended, if your kingdom fails to find a match or the story you registered for does not meet the requirements to begin, then your kingdom will enter a by-election phase, closing the story and cancelling your kingdom's registration status. Your kingdom can register for another story during the by-election phase. <b>You will not be able to adjust the battle rosters of your Lost Kingdom domains during the by-election</b>. By-elections will only happen once, so choose carefully!
3. There will be multiple camps in each story. Camps may be comprised of multiple kingdoms or domains.
4. Kingdoms that do not register this time will be automatically registered for a random story during the next registration period.
5. All kingdoms entering the Season of Conquest for the first time will be automatically placed in the Heroic Anthem story. Once all kingdoms are ready, matchmaking for the story will begin.
Regents help manage the domain they are assigned to, and have the following abilities and restrictions:
1. In the Lost Kingdom, they can appoint kingdom titles to governors in their domain, with the exception of the Sinner title. <b>The limit on how many times a kingdom can appoint each title is shared across all domains</b>.
2. They can use their kingdom's Lost Temple to send mail to all governors in their domain.
3. They can use their kingdom's king skills on their own domain (except for Tax and Banish). <b>Each domain's king skills have separate cooldowns</b>.
Governors that have been assigned to a domain are subject to the following restrictions:
1. They cannot join an alliance in a different domain.
2. They can only build alliance buildings on their own domain's Lost Kingdom map, and they can only take part in their own domain's events.
Governors that did not receive a slot will still be added to a domain. They will be notified of which domain they are in via mail. However, they will be subject to additional restrictions:
1. They can enter their domain's Lost Kingdom map, but they <b>cannot move their troops or city outside of their starting province</b>.
2. They cannot launch or join rallies that target cities, strongholds, or troops belonging to other governors, nor can they directly attack other governors' strongholds. They can still rally against/attack NPC targets.
3. They cannot build or reinforce alliance buildings (except for alliance resource centers). They also cannot reinforce Passes or Holy Sites.
1. After entering the Lost Kingdom, you can use the Domain Overview page to view the status of other domains and claim certain camp rewards they've earned. <b>You can only claim each camp reward once</b>.
2. <b>Only governors in the highest-power domain will be considered for Hall of Fame eligibility</b>. If your kingdom meets the requirements, the Go Down In History event will be triggered for all governors in your kingdom, but only governors in the most powerful domain will be eligible as candidates.
3. <b>All domains in your kingdom will share one Lost Kingdom immigration quota</b>, and the king is still the only person who can approve immigrant applications.

Domain Overview
Preparation Phase: {p1}^
At the end of the preparation phase, story registration for the Season of Conquest will begin.
Registration Phase: {p1}
During this phase, your king can select a story to sign up for. They can change their choice or cancel their registration at any time until the end of this phase.
Team-Up Phase: {p1}
In this phase, kingdoms that chose a story that requires teaming up with other kingdoms will be able to form teams. If your kingdom did not choose one of these stories, you do not have to do anything in this phase.
Pre-Matchmaking Phase: {p1}
In this phase, kingdoms that signed up for the same story will be matched for the first time. If your kingdom fails to find a match by the end of this phase, or the story you registered for does not meet the requirements to begin, you will move on to the by-election phase.
By-Election Phase: {p1}
If your kingdom fails to find a match or the story you registered for cannot begin, your kingdom's registration will be canceled and you will enter into a by-election. During this phase, your kingdom can register for a different story. If you fail to register for this story, you will not get another by-election. If your kingdom does not require a by-election, you do not have to do anything during this phase.
Matchmaking Phase: {p1}
In this phase, all kingdoms that successfully registered for a story will be matched up with their final opponents. Matchmaking results will not be shown until the end of the phase.
Announcement Phase: {p1}7

ALLIANCE INVICTUS
Alliance Invictus9
Hoofbeats pound like falling rain, as our foe throws more steel, blood, and bodies against our mighty walls. Do not ask how long you will have to fight, how many you will need to slay, nor how much you will have to sacrifice. So long as this bastion holds, so too shall our alliance. When dawn breaks once more, you will see our flags still standing proud, and you will know that you are a true warrior.
No matter who you were before, we all must serve where we are needed. Trade your lance for arrows, your ballista for a sword. Glory can be won no matter your weapon!
Alliance Outposts
Alliance Flags can be upgraded into Alliance Outposts. Alliance Outposts have all the functionality of an Alliance Flag, and can also be used to loan troops to your alliance. Your alliance leader and officers can use these loan troops to garrison buildings or launch rallies.
Alliance Flag Shields
Alliance Flags can activate a shield, which will temporarily render them invincible and unable to be attacked. Shielded Flags cannot be used to build Flags further afield, nor can they be used to attack adjacent Alliance Flags/Fortresses.
Curios+
If a troop's secondary commander equips a curio, all units in their troop will be converted into a certain unit type (depending on the curio). Curios will not affect units garrisoned in your city. You will be able to redeem a curio after a certain Lost Kingdom Chronicles chapter has been completed.
Support Skills(
Each troop can equip up to 2 support skills. The level of a support skill will be based on the level of that skill in your own commander roster. If you do not own a commander, you cannot use their skills as support skills. Support skills will not take effect if a troop is in a rally or garrison.
Bastions are spread throughout the Lost Kingdom, each of them led by a commander and their units. There are 18 commanders in total for you to find. 
Why bring in a "Pioneer" system?<
What are the sorts of things we'll get to see in this story?'
What is the schedule of this new story?)
Where can I give suggestions or feedback?0
Will I receive a special reward for taking part?M
Though the core content of this story is now complete, as part of a new Season there may be some areas that require urgent improvement. We are hoping to use this as an opportunity for governors to get a sneak peek, to collect feedback, and provide a more enjoyable story. This Pioneer experience is planned to be an infrequent event.
In the Alliance Invictus story, you'll be able to experience new combat systems, including more powerful alliance strongholds, new Alliance Flag capabilities, and more varied unit and commander tactics. 
Alliance Outposts: In this new story, Alliance Flags can be upgraded into Alliance Outposts. Alliance members can use Outposts to loan troops to their alliance. Alliance leaders and officers can use these loan troops to garrison buildings or launch rallies.
Alliance Flag Shields: In this Season, Alliance Flags can activate a temporary shield, rendering them invincible and unable to be attacked.
Curios: In this story, each governor can claim one "curio". If a troop's secondary commander equips a curio, all units in that troop will be converted into a certain unit type (depending on the curio). For example, if a secondary commander equips "Curio - Footman's Pledge", all cavalry, archer, and siege units in their troop will be treated as infantry units. Note: Curios will only take effect in this story's Lost Kingdom. 
Support Skills: In this story, you can complete quests from Bastion commanders to unlock their skills as support skills. In certain situations, your troops can equip up to 2 support skills.
In the Alliance Invictus story, the battle schedule will be roughly as follows (the specific days are subject to change and are only for reference):
On Day 10, governors from different camps will meet for the first time. On Day 21, the camps will be able to fight for control over the Altar of Darkness. On Day 28, a new area will open up, drawing combatants into a larger, more intense conflict. On Day 31, the King's Land will be opened. On Day 37, all Passes will be opened, setting the stage for a climactic battle!
You can share any thoughts or issues with us by going to Settings -> Help. We pay close attention to all feedback we receive and may send you an in-game reward.
Governors who take part in the newest story will be eligible for basic Pioneer rewards and participation rewards, in addition to ordinary story rewards.

Curios
1. Curios will be unlocked after the end of the Lost Kingdom Chronicle chapter "{p1}".
2. You can redeem one curio per Season. After the Lost Kingdom Chronicles chapter "{p2}", you will have a chance to swap to a different curio. Redeeming or swapping curios costs {p3} Crystals.
3. If a troop's secondary commander equips a curio, all units in that troop will be converted into a certain unit type (which unit type depends on the curio equipped). Curios do not affect units garrisoned in your city.
4. Units converted to a different unit type will be treated as said unit type when calculating commander skill and talent effects.
5. Curios will only take effect in the Lost Kingdom map.
6. Units converted by a curio will count as their original unit type if killed or severely wounded.

Greetings, Governor. You can now redeem a new type of Legendary equipment: <color=#ffa800>Curios</color>. Allow me to give you a quick overview of what they can do.
If a commander is equipped with a curio, all units in their troop will be <color=#ffa800>converted into a single unit type</color>.M
Curios will only work when equipped to a troop's <color=#ffa800>secondary commander</color>. All units in said commander's troop will be treated as the same unit type when calculating skill and talent effects, depending on the curio. Curios will only take effect in the Lost Kingdom and will not affect units garrisoned in your city.
For example, if a secondary commander is equipped with <color=#ffa800>Curio - Riding Crop</color>, all units in their troop will be treated as <color=#ffa800>cavalry units</color>. This means that any skills they have that affect cavalry units will affect all units in their troop.
Remember, you can only redeem <color=#ffa800>one curio</color> per Season, and you will only have <color=#ffa800>one opportunity to swap to a different curio</color>.
Greetings Governor. In this story, you'll be able to make use of the <color=#ffa800>support skill</color> system. Allow me to show you how it works.
In this Season, you can complete quests issued by Bastion commanders to increase your Favor with them. At <color=#ffa800>Favor level 6</color>, you'll unlock one of the commander's skills as a <color=#ffa800>support skill</color>.
Each troop can equip up to two <color=#ffa800>support skills</color>. The level of a support skill will be based on the level of that skill in your own commander roster. Each support skill can only be equipped by one troop at a time.g
Do note, however, that support skills do not affect <color=#ffa800>garrisons or rallied armies</color>.

A special type of ancient ruin reserved by the barbarians for only the most important rituals. The dark power flowing through this broken altar gives its defenders additional strength.
1. The Altar of Darkness will be opened for contention every 84 hours, and will remain open for 2 hours.
2. New Dark Stalkers will spawn when the Altar opens. Defeat them to earn massive amounts of EXP and other rewards.
3. While the Altar is open, you can dispatch a troop to the province the Altar is located in to earn Honor Points.
4. The Altar of Darkness cannot be attacked directly. Instead, your coalition will automatically gain control of the Altar once all barbarians and troops from other coalitions have been removed from the Altar's vicinity.
5. If your coalition controls the Altar and you have a troop in the vicinity of the Altar, the commanders of that troop will continually gain EXP and you will continually earn Honor Points.
6. The barbarians will regain control of the Altar of Darkness once the contention period has ended.
7. Each governor can only have one troop benefit from the Altar of Darkness' effect at a time.l

Regents help manage the domain they are assigned to, and have the following abilities and restrictions:
1. In the Lost Kingdom, they can appoint kingdom titles to governors in their domain, with the exception of the Sinner title. <b>Each domain has its own titles, and regents cannot grant their domain's titles to a governor in a different domain. The titles will only take effect in the Lost Kingdom</b>.
2. They can use their kingdom's Lost Temple to send mail to all governors in their domain.
3. They can use their kingdom's king skills on their own domain (except for Tax and Banish). <b>Each domain's king skills have separate cooldowns</b>.
Additional Kingdom Title Rules:
1. At the start of the matchmaking phase, the system will record each kingdom's current king and their domain. If a governor in the king's recorded domain receives a kingdom title, the title will take effect in both their home kingdom and the Lost Kingdom.
2. Even if a kingdom changes kings, the recorded domain will remain the same. If a king grants a title in their original kingdom to a governor that isn't in the recorded domain, the title will only take effect in the original kingdom.
Governors that have been assigned to a domain are subject to the following restrictions:
1. They can only enter their own domain's Lost Kingdom. While in the Lost Kingdom, they cannot join alliances in other domains. Upon returning to their home kingdom, they can once again join any alliance they wish to.
2. They can only build alliance buildings on their own domain's Lost Kingdom map, and they can only take part in their own domain's events.
3. While in their <b>home kingdom</b>, if they join an alliance in a different domain from them, they won't be able to receive that alliance's Lost Kingdom rewards (including Autarch Testimonies), besides camp rewards from the Domain Overview page. In addition, their actions will not earn Lost Kingdom event points for their alliance, coalition, and camp. However, they will still count towards earning individual rewards for Crusader Achievements and the following events:
Give and Receive, Strategic Reserve, Lord of War, Barbarian Busters, and Now Or Never.
Governors that did not receive a slot will still be added to a domain. They will be notified of which domain they are in via mail. However, they will be subject to additional restrictions:
1. They can enter their domain's Lost Kingdom map, but they <b>cannot move their troops or city outside of their starting province</b>.
2. They cannot launch or join rallies that target cities, strongholds, or troops belonging to other governors, nor can they directly attack other governors' strongholds. They can still rally against/attack NPC targets.
3. They cannot build or reinforce alliance buildings (except for alliance resource centers). They also cannot reinforce Passes or Holy Sites.I

COALITION TECHNOLOGY
Weekly Donations: {p1}
Recommended$
<b>Contribution Points</b>
1. You can only earn Tech Tokens after joining a coalition. Tech Tokens you earn while in a coalition can only be donated to that coalition's techs. If you leave a coalition, your Tech Tokens will not be lost, and can still be used if you later rejoin that coalition.
2. Donating Tech Tokens to help research coalition techs will earn you Contribution Points.
3. If you reach {p1} Contribution Points in a week, you will be added to your coalition's contribution rankings, giving you a chance to earn substantial rewards.

<b>Occupation Rules</b>
Levels Up In: {p1}
1. Rather than opening with the Lost Kingdom Chronicles, Arenas will be opened at the very start of the Lost Kingdom. Arenas will alternate between phases of contention and protection.
2. After your coalition occupies an Arena for a period of time, it will gain an initial occupation level for the Arena, which will increase the longer your coalition occupies it. Your coalition's occupation level for an Arena will persist even if you lose control of it. If an alliance without a coalition occupies an Arena, their occupation level will remain at level 1 until they join a coalition.
3. All members of your coalition will receive bonuses from an Arena based on your coalition's occupation level. You will continue receiving those bonuses even if your coalition is not currently occupying the Arena.
4. The contention phase for Arenas will last for {p1} hours, while the protection phase will last {p2} hours. You cannot attack Arenas during the protection phase, but your coalition can still increase its occupation level for Arenas it is already occupying.
5. There are 4 types of Arenas in this Lost Kingdom, each with a different buff. If your coalition has occupied multiple Arenas of the same type, you will only receive a buff from the Arena with the highest occupation level.)
No coalition has occupied this Arena yet.

Coalition Member Elite Points
1. Mastery skills for a given unit type can only be used by troops with 200,000+ Tier 4 or 5 units of that type.
2. The effects of a mastery skill will end if the troop that used the skill enters a city, stronghold, or resource point.
3. Using mastery skills does not cost resources, but each mastery skill has a cooldown time. Mastery skills will need to cool down after being used a total of 5 times.R

OCCUPATION OVERVIEW
Bastion Quest Tech Tokens
After the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), you will be able to earn Tech Tokens by completing Bastion Quests in the Lost Kingdom. Tech Tokens can be donated to help research coalition techs.
Combat Tech Tokens
After the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), you will be able to earn Tech Tokens by defeating units from enemy camps. Tech Tokens can be donated to help research coalition techs.
Keener Blades
In this turbulent era, the beacon of civilization can only be forged in the crucible of war.
Once more on the march, our soldiers have been honed into sharper swords and stronger shields. Emboldened by their newfound power, they charge eagerly into battle, determined to prove themselves masters of the battlefield.
Amidst resounding battle cries, enemies flood the battlefield, met by our steadfast soldiers. In the haze of war, a new saga of conflict unfolds...
Coalition Technology
To develop the new coalition technology tree, you'll need to earn Tech Tokens by gathering resources, completing Bastion Quests, and defeating enemy camp units in the Lost Kingdom. You can donate Tech Tokens via the coalition technology interface to research coalition techs, which will provide benefits to all governors in your coalition. Certain techs can only be researched after their corresponding Lost Kingdom Chronicles chapters have ended.
Elite Unit Enhancement
In this Season, you can upgrade your existing Tier 4 and 5 units into new elite units. These new units can also be equipped with unique "mastery skills". You can earn Elite Points by researching coalition techs, which can be used to unlock and upgrade mastery skills.
Arenas
The new Arena buildings in the Lost Kingdom will alternate between contention and protection phases. As your coalition continues to occupy an Arena, your occupation level for that Arena will increase. All members of your coalition will receive buffs from the Arena based on your occupation level.
Your occupation level for an Arena and the buffs you receive from it will remain even if your coalition loses control of the Arena. 
Why bring in a "Pioneer" system?<
What are the sorts of things we'll get to see in this story?'

Will I receive a special reward for taking part?K
Though the core content of this story is now complete, as part of a new Season there may be some areas that require urgent improvement. We are hoping to use this as an opportunity to give our governors a sneak peek, to collect feedback, and to craft a more enjoyable story. This Pioneer experience is planned to be a one-off event.
In the Keener Blades story, you will get to experience the new Arena map building, the coalition technology system, new elite units, and their unique mastery skills.
Arenas: The new Arena buildings in the Lost Kingdom will alternate between contention and protection phases. As your coalition continues to occupy an Arena, your occupation level for that Arena will increase. All members of your coalition will receive buffs from the Arena based on your occupation level. Your occupation level for an Arena and the buffs you receive from it will remain even if your coalition loses control of the Arena.
Coalition Technology: To develop the new coalition technology tree, you'll need to earn Tech Tokens by gathering resources, completing Bastion Quests, and defeating enemy camp units in the Lost Kingdom. You can donate Tech Tokens via the coalition technology interface to research coalition techs, which will provide benefits to all governors in your coalition. Certain techs can only be researched after their corresponding Lost Kingdom Chronicles chapters have ended.
Elite Unit Enhancement and Mastery Skills: This new system allows you to promote your existing Tier 4 and 5 units into all-new elite units, which can be equipped with new "mastery skills". Mastery skills can be unlocked and upgraded using Elite Points, which can be earned by researching certain coalition techs. Mastery skills provide a powerful edge for your troops, but can only be used a certain number of times before they need to cool down.^
In the Keener Blades story, the battle schedule will be roughly as follows (the specific days are subject to change and are only for reference): 
On day 9, governors from different camps will be able to encounter each other for the first time. On day 16, governors will begin battling over the Ancient Ruins. On day 29, the camps will be able to enter a new combat area to do battle. On day 35, even more areas will be unlocked, expanding the scope of the battle. On day 38, the final battle for the King's Land will begin, and most of the Passes on the map will be opened to help you redeploy your forces.
You can share any thoughts or issues with us by going to Settings -> Customer Support. We pay close attention to all feedback we receive and will send you an in-game reward.
Governors who take part in the newest story will be eligible for basic Pioneer rewards and participation rewards, in addition to ordinary story rewards.
Class Rating Phase: {p1}
In this phase, kingdoms and domains will be assigned classes based on their power and other criteria. You do not need to do anything during this phase. At the end of the countdown, all kingdoms/domains will be assigned a class and the team-up phase will begin.

SHIFTING GEARS%
<color=#000000>Shifting Gears</color>
Shifting Gears
Ballistae. These mighty war machines were once the pride of empires and a symbol of martial might. Their powerful bolts soared across enemy lines and changed the outcome of countless wars.
Now, a new kind of ballista emerges from the kingdom's workshops, with abilities unlike any before it. "Shifters". This battlefield will be their proving ground, where our brave governors will use them to skirmish and harry our foes or rain death from vast distances. In this war, victory will come at the end of a bolt.
Shifters
If a revamped commander is the primary commander of a troop on the map, all of the troop's units will be converted into a new unit type: shifters.
Revamping Commanders
Revamping a commander allows them to lead skirmisher/artillery troops and select a new active/expertise skill. Revamped engineering commanders will gain an extra {s1}% bonus to Shifter Attack.
Unit Bonus Conversion
If a revamped commander is the primary commander of a troop on the map, certain bonuses from equipment, talents, formations, armaments, technologies, the museum, and city themes will be converted into bonuses for shifter units, depending on the commander's talents. For example, if a revamped commander has the "infantry" tag, all infantry bonuses from the above sources will be converted into shifter bonuses. In addition, melee damage bonuses will be converted into ranged damage bonuses.
Skirmisher Troops
Skirmisher Troop Features
1. Can launch ranged attacks without needing to switch to ranged mode.
2. If there are no enemy troops belonging to other governors near a skirmisher troop, it gains a 20% boost to damage dealt.
3. Damage received by skirmisher troops is boosted by 10%.
4. Only revamped commanders or commanders with the engineering tag can use their active skills while leading skirmisher troops.
5. If you order a skirmisher troop to attack while it is occupying the same space on the map as an Arrow Tower, artillery troop, or another skirmisher troop that is currently attacking, it will automatically move to an open space before attacking.
6. Skirmisher troops cannot launch basic attacks or use active skills for {p1} seconds after leaving a city or stronghold.
Artillery Troops-
Artillery Troop Features
1. Can switch to ranged mode to launch ranged attacks.
2. Extremely long attack range, but narrow firing angle.
3. Only revamped commanders or commanders with the engineering tag can use their active skills while leading artillery troops.
4. Gains a 15% boost to damage dealt.
Revamp Coins
You will need to spend Revamp Coins each time you revamp a commander. You will automatically gain Revamp Coins every hour, and can also earn them by completing Bastion Quests.
King Skills~
In this story, you'll have access to the new "Inspire" king skill: Choose one of your kingdom's troops to act as an "Inspiring Troop". All of your kingdom's melee troops that are within a certain range of the Inspiring Troop when this skill is cast will take {s1}% less ranged damage and deal {s2}% more ranged counterattack damage for the next {p3} minutes. Cooldown: {p4} minutes.
Counterfire
All your melee troops in this story will gain the "Counterfire" buff: Whenever one of your melee troops is hit with a ranged basic attack, it gains a {s1}% reduction to ranged damage received and a {s2}% bonus to ranged counterattack damage dealt. This effect can stack up to {p3} times, and each stack lasts for {p4} seconds. Each enemy troop can cause this effect to trigger once every {p5} seconds.

Ranged2
To use a unit's mastery skill, tap the skill icon.
Built by the ancients and imbued with divine power, the <color=#ffa800>Arenas</color> of the Lost Kingdom will provide powerful boons to the coalitions that occupy them.
While your coalition occupies an Arena, its "occupation level" will increase over time. The higher your coalition's occupation level for an Arena, the more bonuses it will provide.
After capturing an Arena, you can use the Occupation Overview page to view each coalition's occupation level for the Arena, the current bonuses it is providing, and how long it will take to level up.
MINI
Greetings, Governor. In this Season, you will have access to the Coalition Technology system, which will provide bonuses to all members of your coalition.
After researching certain coalition techs, you will be able to earn Tech Tokens by completing Bastion Quests and killing enemy units. In turn, you can use these Tech Tokens to research more coalition techs.
In addition, certain coalition techs will grant you <b>Elite Points</b>, which can be used for the elite unit enhancement feature.]
Through elite unit enhancement, you can promote your existing units into new, mightier units.
You can also select a "mastery skill" for your new elite units, though unlocking and upgrading mastery skills will also require <b>Elite Points</b>.
After using a mastery skill a certain number of times, you will need to wait for the skill to cool down. Choosing the right mastery skills
and the right time to use them
will be your key to victory.
<b>Registration:</b>
- Each time registration begins, a number of kingdoms will be allowed to participate in this mode, based on kingdom population, power, kills, and other factors.
<b>Season Info:</b>
How Mini-Seasons Differ From Regular Seasons:
- There will be fewer total governors in the Lost Kingdom and fewer governors in each camp.
- The objectives of certain Lost Kingdom Chronicle chapters will be easier to achieve.
- Requirements for certain Crusader Achievements will be lowered.
- Certain neutral buildings will be easier to capture.
- The number of Autarch Testimonies will be reduced to match the fewer governors in the Lost Kingdom.

In the "Shifting Gears" story, you'll be able to experience all-new ranged combat and a new way to customize your commanders: revamping. If a revamped commander is the primary commander of a troop on the map, all of that troop's units will be converted into the new "shifter" unit type. In addition, they'll be able to convert their troops into "skirmisher troops" or "artillery troops", which have the following features:
Artillery Troop Features
1. Can switch to ranged mode to launch ranged attacks.
2. Extremely long attack range, but narrow firing angle.
3. Gains a 15% boost to damage dealt.
Skirmisher Troop Features
1. Can launch ranged attacks without needing to switch to ranged mode.
2. If there are no enemy troops belonging to other governors near a skirmisher troop, it gains a 20% boost to damage dealt.
3. Damage received by skirmisher troops is boosted by 10%.
4. If you order a skirmisher troop to attack while it is occupying the same space on the map as an Arrow Tower, artillery troop, or another skirmisher troop that is currently attacking, it will automatically move to an open space before attacking.
In the Shifting Gears story, the battle schedule will be roughly as follows (the specific days are subject to change and are only for reference):
 On Day 10, governors from different camps will meet for the first time. On Day 22, the camps will be able to fight for control over the Altars of Darkness. On Day 31, all the camps will be connected to one another. On Day 34, the final battle for the King's Land will begin.
You can share any thoughts or issues with us by going to Settings -> Customer Support. We pay close attention to all feedback we receive and will send you an in-game reward.
Governors who take part in the newest story will be eligible for basic Pioneer rewards and participation rewards, in addition to ordinary story rewards.
Commander Revamp
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), you will unlock the ability to revamp your commanders. Revamped commanders will be able to lead skirmisher troops. You will be able to have up to 2 revamped commanders at this stage.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), revamped commanders will unlock the ability to lead artillery troops. You will be able to have up to 4 revamped commanders at this stage.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), the limit on how many revamped commanders you can have will be raised to 20.

Class Rating Phase
Revamping Commanders.
Defensive. Will not attack the Great Ziggurat.
These heavily fortified structures stand at the four corners of the Great Ziggurat. They serve as the Ziggurat's first line of defense; if captured, they become a tremendous threat to the Ziggurat's occupants.
1. Citadels will be opened for contention at the same time as the Great Ziggurat.
2. Citadels cannot be attacked directly. Instead, a coalition will automatically gain control of a Citadel once all troops not belonging to the coalition have been removed from the Citadel's vicinity.
3. If a Citadel and the Great Ziggurat are occupied by different coalitions, the troops garrisoned in the Great Ziggurat will take massive damage every 5 minutes. Units severely wounded by this damage will die instantly.

The Ziggurat is the meeting place of the most powerful barbarian chieftains in the entire Lost Kingdom, and is guarded by their most elite warriors. For thousands of years, all those who have thought to conquer the Great Ziggurat have perished beneath its walls.
1. The Great Ziggurat will open for contention every 3 days. During this period, all coalitions can attack the Ziggurat. Any coalition that can capture and hold the Ziggurat for 8 hours will gain control of it.
2. The first commander (captain of the rallied army) to occupy the Great Ziggurat will become the defense captain responsible for repelling enemy attacks. If this commander is withdrawn, the commander with the highest power will automatically succeed the position.
3. All units who are severely wounded during Great Ziggurat battles will die immediately (during battles with barbarians, all severely wounded units will be sent to the Hospital).

Greetings, Governor. On this page, you can "revamp" your existing commanders, allowing them to learn new skills and utilize this story's unique mechanics. Allow me to give you a brief overview of how it works.C
There are two new troop types in this story: skirmisher troops and artillery troops. Skirmisher troops are highly mobile, capable of launching ranged attacks without switching to ranged mode. Artillery troops will need to switch to ranged mode to launch ranged attacks, but they make up for it with greatly-increased range.x
You can convert a troop into a skirmisher or artillery troop by assigning a revamped commander as its primary commander.
Revamping a commander will disable their active and expertise skills, though you'll be able to select a new active and expertise skill for them.W
Tap the info icon in the upper-right corner of the Commander Revamp page to learn more.
Governor, you've selected a skirmisher troop commander as this troop's primary commander. While it's on the map, this troop will be able to launch ranged attacks without having to switch to ranged mode.
If this commander is the primary commander of a troop on the map, all units in their troop will be converted into shifter units.
Based on the units they specialize in, certain unit-specific bonuses will be converted into shifter unit bonuses. Additionally, all of their melee bonuses will be converted into ranged bonuses.
Only revamped commanders or commanders with the "engineering" tag can use their active skills while leading a skirmisher troop.
Units in a skirmisher troop will revert to their original forms if the troop joins a garrison or rally.7
Tap my icon on the Commander Revamp page to learn more.
Governor, you've selected an artillery troop commander as this troop's primary commander. This troop will be able to attack farther and hit harder while in ranged mode.
If this commander is the primary commander of a troop on the map, all units in their troop will be converted into shifter units.
Based on the units they specialize in, certain unit-specific bonuses will be converted into shifter unit bonuses. Additionally, all of their melee bonuses will be converted into ranged bonuses.
Only revamped commanders or commanders with the "engineering" tag can use their active skills while leading an artillery troop.
Units in an artillery troop will revert to their original forms if the troop joins a garrison or rally.7
Tap my icon on the Commander Revamp page to learn more.+

<b>Domain Signup Guide</b>
1. After setting a regent for each domain, your kingdom's king can set signup requirements for each domain and a deadline for signing up via this page. The signup deadline cannot be within 12 hours of the story registration deadline.
2. After confirming the settings, your king can begin a signup period for the kingdom, allowing all governors in your kingdom to choose a domain to sign up for (provided they meet the requirements). Once the signup period has begun, signup requirements and other related settings cannot be changed.
3. After the signup period ends, your king can choose to directly apply the results to your kingdom's Lost Kingdom domain rosters. Applying signup results will overwrite any manually-set domain rosters.
After the deadline, signup results will be published. You can view the results and choose to directly apply them to your Lost Kingdom domain rosters.

Activate
A percentage of your units killed during the Season of Conquest will be returned to you at the end of the Season.
1. During the Season of Conquest, your units killed in battles with other governors will be recorded in the Hall of Heroes. Units lost in battles outside of the Lost Kingdom or after the Season is tallied will not be recorded.
2. At the end of the Season, a portion of your dead units in the Hall of Heroes will be resurrected and returned to you.
3. Units awaiting resurrection will still count towards your kingdom's power total.
4. The Hall of Heroes will record the type and level of all units killed. If you change civilizations before the end of the Season, any special units in the Hall of Heroes will be converted into your new civilization's special units when they are returned to you.
5. If you immigrate before the end of the Season, a portion of your dead units in the Hall of Heroes will be returned to you via mail.

Megingj
We have ushered in a golden age for our civilization, yet humanity's thirst for new frontiers will never cease. Deciphering the records of our ancient ancestors, we have learned of a new land: a realm of mysterious Holy Sites, boundless resources, and unknown perils. We are preparing to embark upon an expedition unlike ever before; will you join us?
1. The "Eve of the Crusade" event is divided into three stages. During each stage, you can perform certain in-game actions to earn individual crusade points, kingdom crusade points, and camp crusade points.
2. The three stages are: Kill Marauders, Train Troops, and Attack Marauder Encampments.
3. Multiple kingdoms will participate together in this event, and all event rankings will be cross-kingdom rankings.
4. Camp crusade points will be calculated based on the total kingdom crusade points of all the kingdoms in the camp. The final camp rankings will determine the order in which each camp selects its starting province.
5. After the end of the Eve of the Crusade event, starting province selection will begin. Once the provinces have been selected, the path to the Lost Kingdom will be opened, allowing governors in participating kingdoms to teleport to their camp's starting province in Britain.

Rules
Overview
Combat
Other
Brave warriors, welcome to the Holmgang! The Holmgang is an ancient Viking tradition, a duel called to settle matters of land, property, or honor. Forget the politicking of the isles; here you will fight for glory!
Basic Rules
1. In this event, governors will be divided between Vikings and English rather than by camp. You'll be able to team up with any governors on your side.
- Viking Camps: Northumbria and East Anglia
- English Camps: Mercia and Wessex
2. Each team can have a maximum of three governors. Every governor must set a battle lineup to participate.
3. Winning teams will receive points, losing teams will lose points. Teams will be ranked by their final score.
4. This event will be divided into multiple rounds, with each round having its own ranking rewards.
5. At the end of each round, the side with more teams in the top 15 will be declared the winner of the round.
Competition Format
Warm-Up Phase
When the Lost Kingdom first opens, the Holmgang event will enter a warm-up phase, lasting for 2 days. You can use this time to familiarize yourself with the rules of the event and plan out your lineup.
Registration Phase
After the warm-up phase, there will be a 1-day registration phase.
1. During the registration phase:
- You can create or join a team, switch teams, and set your lineup.
- Team captains can edit their team roster and the order in which each member will fight.
2. Once the registration phase ends, team rosters, lineups, and battle orders will be locked in.
3. Each round will have its own registration phase. Your team info will be reset between rounds.
Duel Phase
The registration phase will be followed by the duel phase, lasting for 5 days. During this phase, your team captain will be able to choose opponents for your team to challenge.
1. You can challenge up to 3 groups per day. A group is comprised of 3 teams. Once you've challenged all teams in a group, you'll move onto the next group.
2. You can refresh the enemies in the group you're currently challenging. Teams you've already challenged and your duel history will not be refreshed. You can refresh up to 5 times per day.
Announcement Phase
The duel phase will be followed by the announcement phase, lasting for 1 day. The final team rankings will be displayed and all participants will receive Stratagem Points based on their ranking.
Team and Combat Rules
1. To compete in the Holmgang event, you'll need to form or join a team. Each team can have up to 3 members.
2. The captain of your team is the one who will choose which opponents to challenge. Duels will consist of multiple one-on-one battles between the members of both teams. If a member of your team is defeated, the next member in your battle order will be slotted in.
3. If you win a battle, you will stay in to fight the next member in the enemy team's battle order. Your troops' remaining HP will carry over between battles, though their Rage and position on the battlefield will be reset. The duel will end when all members of one of the teams have been defeated.
4. This event uses a points system. If you win a duel, you will earn points. If you lose, you will lose points.
Team Buffs
1. The Unit Capacity of troops fighting in this event will be based on the Unit Capacity provided by a level 25 City Hall. Buffs from <color=#d55500>commander levels, talents, equipment, Museum Relics, formations, armaments, VIP Unit Capacity bonuses, and Crystal technologies</color> will take effect in this event. All other buffs will not.
2. Each governor can deploy up to 5 troops. All troops will use <color=#d55500>Tier 5 units provided by the system</color>.
Overtime
If a battle goes on for 2 minutes without a winner, the damage dealt by all troops on the battlefield will increase by 10% every 10 seconds until somebody wins.

RAID
LOOT
Burhs are fortified settlements, part stronghold and part town. As centers of population and wealth, they make for a prime raiding target for those gutsy enough to challenge their formidable defenses. By defeating the defenders of an enemy Burh, you can loot a hoard of valuable supplies. By defending your own Burhs, you can stop an enemy advance in its tracks.
1. The Lost Kingdom is home to both English and Viking Burhs. You can only raid and loot enemy Burhs.
2. Burhs can be in one of two phases:
- The "Open" phase, lasting for {p1} hours, during which time they can be entered and raided by enemy camps.
- The "Protected" phase, lasting for {p2} hours. After a Burh's Open phase ends, it will enter the Protected phase. If the Burh was successfully looted, it will enter the Rebuilding phase before entering the Protected phase (though the overall duration will be the same).
3. During the Open phase, you can defeat an enemy Burh's defending troops or destroy its buildings to increase the Burh's Loot Value. Once a Burh reaches {p3} Loot Value, its status will be changed to "Looting", and governors in your camp will be able to loot its central building to claim a reward.
4. If a troop from any other camp enters the Burh, its status will change from Looting to Contending. You will not be able to claim a reward until you remove all troops from other camps from the Burh and the Burh's status reverts to Looting. Governors from other camps <b>will not be able to</b> loot a Burh that your camp has successfully raided.
5. Burhs in the Rebuilding or Protected phase cannot be entered, even by friendly troops.
6. During each Open phase, you can claim up to <b>2 rewards from looting Burhs, and only 1 reward per Burh</b>.
7. Troops from Viking camps (Northumbria and East Anglia) will receive a {p4}% bonus to Attack while in a Burh.
Troops from English camps (Mercia and Wessex) will receive a {p5}% bonus to Defense while in a Burh.
Viking Fyrd
English Fyrd
Loot Value: <b>+{p1}</b>;
inn! Grant us victory, or a seat in your hallowed hall!(
In Anglo-Saxon England, the Fyrd was a regional militia army, levied primarily to defend Burhs from enemy raids. Well-armed by their feudal lords and seasoned from constant battles, the warriors of this Fyrd are highly-motivated to defend their home from invaders.
1. Fyrds will actively attack enemy troops that enter their Burh. English Fyrds will attack troops from Northumbria and East Anglia; Viking Fyrds will attack troops from Wessex and Mercia.
2. Attacking Fyrds will not cost you action points. Your units can only be slightly wounded when fighting Fyrds.
3. You will not earn any rewards for defeating a Fyrd, but it will increase the Loot Value of the Burh. Once a Burh's Loot Value reaches 100 and there are no troops from other camps within it, the Burh's status will be changed to "Looting".
Viking Sentry
English Sentry&
A true Viking warrior fears no battle!
Day and night, these Sentries patrol the streets of the Burh, chasing down any intruders they come across.
1. Sentries will patrol fixed routes within their Burh, actively attacking any enemy troops they come across. English Sentries will attack troops from Northumbria and East Anglia; Viking Sentries will attack troops from Wessex and Mercia.
2. Attacking Sentries will not cost you action points. Your units can only be slightly wounded when fighting Sentries.
3. You will not earn any rewards for defeating a Sentry, but it will increase the Loot Value of the Burh. Once a Burh's Loot Value reaches 100 and there are no troops from other camps within it, the Burh's status will be changed to "Looting".,

KING OF ALL BRITAIN

King of All Britain
King of All BritainD
Bretwalda. Rex Totius Britanniae. King of All Britain. A title held by those mighty few who held overlordship over all of Albion. For centuries, Saxon, Angle, Gael, and Briton have fought savagely for the honor, but now a new contender arrives from the unforgiving north...
Welcome to Britain, friends from afar. A land of boundless opportunity, now engulfed in a war between the English and Vikings. Four great kingdoms are locked in a delicate balance of power, but your arrival threatens to tip the scales. Who will you support, and who will be crowned the King of All Britain?
Starting Province Selection'
Once the Eve of the Crusade event ends, each camp will be given a limited time to select a starting province. The Eve of the Crusade camp rankings will determine the selection order. Each starting province comes with a unique buff, so getting to choose first can put you at a tactical advantage.
Holmgang)
In this Vikings vs English PvP mode, you can form a team with other governors on your side (not just in your camp). Each team can have up to 3 members, and you'll need to set a battle lineup to compete. The event is divided into multiple independent rounds, each with its own rankings and rewards.
Stratagems
In this story, you can use Stratagem Points to purchase powerful persistent buffs called Stratagems. New Stratagems will be added to the Stratagem Shop as the Lost Kingdom Chronicles progress. The order in which new Stratagems are added will vary between different Lost Kingdoms.
Burh Raiding
Scattered across the map of this Lost Kingdom are both English and Viking Burhs. You can raid enemy Burhs to earn Stratagem Points and other valuable rewards. English troops will gain a Defense bonus while in a Burh, while Viking troops will gain an Attack bonus.
Prime Ragnar Lodbrok's Artifact
At a certain point in the Lost Kingdom Chronicles, all governors will receive an artifact designed exclusively for Prime Ragnar Lodbrok. If Prime Ragnar has this artifact equipped and is serving as a secondary commander, all of his skill will apply to all unit types.
Bastions are spread throughout the Lost Kingdom, each of them led by a commander and their units. There are 14 commanders in total for you to find. 
Why bring in a "Pioneer" system?<
What are the sorts of things we'll get to see in this story?'
What is the schedule of this new story?)
Where can I give suggestions or feedback?0
Will I receive a special reward for taking part?M
Though the core content of this story is now complete, as part of a new Season there may be some areas that require urgent improvement. We are hoping to use this as an opportunity for governors to get a sneak peek, to collect feedback, and provide a more enjoyable story. This Pioneer experience is planned to be an infrequent event.
In the King of All Britain story, you'll get to experience the new starting province selection feature, the Holmgang PvP event, a reworked Stratagem system, Burh raiding, and a unique artifact for Prime Ragnar Lodbrok.
Starting Province Selection: Based on the camp rankings in the Eve of the Crusade event, each camp will take turns selecting a starting province (Mercia, Wessex, East Anglia, or Northumbria).
Holmgang: Form a team with other English or Viking governors and duel other teams for Stratagem Points, unique titles, buffs for your entire camp, and other rewards.
Stratagems: In the reworked Stratagem system, you can use Stratagem Points to purchase new Stratagems and build a combination that enhances your unique playstyle. New Stratagems will be added to the Stratagem Shop as the Lost Kingdom Chronicles progress.
Burh Raiding: Scattered across the Lost Kingdom are both Viking and English Burhs. Raid Burhs belonging to the enemy to loot valuable rewards.
Prime Ragnar Lodbrok Artifact: At a certain point in the Lost Kingdom Chronicles, you'll receive the unique artifact "Megingj
", designed exclusively for Prime Ragnar Lodbrok. When equipped, all of Prime Ragnar Lodbrok's skills will apply to all unit types.G
In the King of All Britain story, the battle schedule will be roughly as follows (the specific days are subject to change and are only for reference):
After the Eve of the Crusade event, camps will select their starting provinces. On Day 3, the Holmgang event will begin. On Day 9, governors from different camps will meet for the first time, and Burhs will be opened for raiding. On Day 19, the camps will be able to fight for control over the Altars of Darkness. On Day 29, all the camps will be connected to one another. On Day 32, the final battle for the King's Land will begin.
You can share any thoughts or issues with us by going to Settings -> Customer Support. We pay close attention to all feedback we receive and will send you an in-game reward.
Governors who take part in the newest story will be eligible for basic Pioneer rewards and participation rewards, in addition to ordinary story rewards.

Greetings, governor, and welcome to the Holmgang.
In this event, governors will be divided between English and Viking rather than by camp. You can form a team with any governors on your side, regardless of camp.`
This event will be divided into multiple rounds, with each round having its own ranking rewards.
Based on your team's ranking, you'll receive a large sum of Stratagem points. The side that has more teams ranked in the top 15 will be considered the "winning" side for the round.
All governors on the winning side will receive a temporary buff. The highest-ranked governors on the winning side will receive a unique title they can display.
Stratagem Points can be used in the Stratagem Shop to purchase Stratagems, which will grant you powerful boons within the Lost Kingdom.0

Castrum TempestatisT
A legacy of those who once ruled these isles, Britain is replete with old Castra, stone-brick fortresses built using techniques long-lost to this land. Though many of them lie in ruins, just as many have been refurbished by the island's later residents to serve as unassailable strongholds, especially as the Viking menace draws near...
1. Castra will be opened for contention once every 2 days. During this period, all alliances will be able to attack the Castra. An alliance that can capture and hold a Castrum for 4 hours will gain control over it.
2. The first commander (captain of the rallied army) to occupy a Castrum will become the defense captain, responsible for repelling enemy attacks. If this commander is withdrawn, the commander garrisoned at the Castrum with the highest power will automatically succeed them.
3. Buffs from the same types of Castra will not stack.
4. Half of the units who are severely wounded during Castrum battles will be sent to the Hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with barbarians, all severely wounded units will be sent to the Hospital).
Castrum Victoris
Field Troop Attack
Monastery of the Holy Sword
Monastery of the Divine Mercy
Monastery of the Sacred Shield
The countless abbeys and priories of Britain have long been places of peace, isolation, and prayer, largely left alone by the island's inhabitants in their internecine conflicts. Yet this same seclusion, along with the incredible wealth they hold, have made them the ideal prey of Viking raiders. With the arrival of a conquering Viking army, no place is safe from the fires of war.
1. Monasteries will be opened for contention once every 2 days. During this period, all alliances will be able to attack the Monasteries. An alliance that can capture and hold a Monastery for 4 hours will gain control over it.
2. The first commander (captain of the rallied army) to occupy a Monastery will become the defense captain, responsible for repelling enemy attacks. If this commander is withdrawn, the commander garrisoned at the Monastery with the highest power will automatically succeed them.
3. Buffs from the same types of Monasteries will not stack.
4. Half of the units who are severely wounded during Monastery battles will be sent to the Hospital, while the rest will die immediately (during battles with barbarians, all severely wounded units will be sent to the Hospital).
Southwell Minster
Situated at the heart of England, the Cathedral and Parish Church of the Blessed Virgin Mary (also known as "Southwell Minster") is a sacred site for the English and a mighty-fine prize for the Vikings. To control it would symbolize control over Nottinghamshire and the River Trent, the vital waterway that divides Northern and Southern England
1. Southwell Minster will open for contention every 3 days. During this period, all coalitions can attack the Minster. Any coalition that can capture and hold Southwell Minster for 8 hours will gain control of it.
2. The first commander (captain of the rallied army) to occupy Southwell Minster will become the defense captain, responsible for repelling enemy attacks. If this commander is withdrawn, the commander garrisoned at Southwell Minster with the highest power will automatically succeed them.
3. All units who are severely wounded during Southwell Minster battles will die immediately (during battles with barbarians, all severely wounded units will be sent to the Hospital).'

Though the core content of this story is now complete, as a version upgrade there may be some areas that require urgent improvement. We are hoping to use this as an opportunity for governors to get a sneak peek, to collect feedback, and provide a more enjoyable story. This Pioneer experience is planned to be an infrequent event.
You can share any thoughts or issues with us by going to Settings -> Customer Support. We pay close attention to all feedback we receive and will send you an in-game reward.
Governors who take part in the newest story will be eligible for basic Pioneer rewards and participation rewards, in addition to ordinary story rewards.

While this Directive is active, a selected Alliance Flag cannot be attacked. During this time, the Flag cannot be used as a basis for building new Flags or for attacking neighboring Flags or Fortresses. Cannot be used on Flags adjacent to Passes.,
In the King of All Britain story, kingdoms will be divided into camps. Each camp must contain at least 1 kingdom. To ensure a level playing field, kingdoms will be assigned to camps automatically. Each camp can choose its starting province, with the camp rankings of the Eve of the Crusade event determining the order in which the camps get to choose. Different starting provinces come with different bonuses, so getting to choose first can put you at a strategic advantage.
When attacking another governor in your camp, you cannot plunder their resources.
Can be earned by defeating barbarians, gathering resources, completing Bastion Quests, and defeating enemy camp units. Tech Tokens you earn in your current coalition can only be used to upgrade your current coalition's technologies.
Can be acquired by researching certain coalition technologies. Used to unlock elite unit enhancement and upgrade mastery skills.
If you remove this alliance from the coalition, <color=#48ff30>its members will be unable to use mastery skills until it joins another coalition</color>. Are you sure you want to remove this alliance?T
All of your alliance's troops currently in coalition buildings or rallies will be immediately recalled, and any rallies initiated by your alliance's members will be canceled. In addition, <color=#48ff30>your members will be unable to use mastery skills until you join another coalition</color>. Are you sure you want to leave the coalition?

Resources Returned/
At the end of the season, {p1}% of the resources you spent on healing units in the Lost Kingdom will be returned to you.
If you do not receive an Autarch Testimony this season (or if you acquired one by fusing Autarch Testimony Fragments), you will receive an extra {p2}% of the resources ({p3}% total).2
A percentage of units lost and resources spent on healing units during the Season of Conquest will be returned to you at the end of the season.
1. During the Season of Conquest, your units killed in battles with other governors will be recorded in the Hall of Heroes. Units lost in battles outside of the Lost Kingdom or after the season is settled will not be recorded. 
2. During the Season of Conquest, resources you spend on healing units in the Lost Kingdom will also be recorded in the Hall of Heroes. Resources you spend on healing units outside of the Lost Kingdom or after the season ends will not be counted. 
3. At the end of the season, a portion of your dead units in the Hall of Heroes will be resurrected and returned to you. In addition, your kingdom will receive extra special immigration points based on how many Tier 4+ units it lost. 
4. Units awaiting resurrection will still count towards your kingdom's power total. 
5. The Hall of Heroes will record the type and level of all units killed. If you change civilizations before the end of the season, any special units in the Hall of Heroes will be converted into your new civilization's special units when they are returned to you. 
6. At the end of the season, a percentage of the healing resources recorded in the Hall of Heroes will be returned to you. If you did not receive an Autarch Testimony, you will receive a higher percentage of the resources. 
7. If you immigrate before the end of the season, a portion of your dead units and healing resources in the Hall of Heroes will be returned to you via mail.@

The unit and resource return feature will be enabled when the Lost Kingdom ends.4
A percentage of units lost and resources spent on healing this season will be returned to you at the end of the season. 
1. In this season's Lost Kingdom, your units killed in battles with other governors and resources spent on healing will be recorded in the Hall of Heroes. Units lost or resources spent outside of the Lost Kingdom or after the season is settled will not be recorded. 
2. At the end of the season, 30% of your dead units and {p1}% of your healing resources will be returned to you. 
3. This return ratio is fixed and cannot be increased. 
4. The Hall of Heroes will record the type and level of all units killed. If you change civilizations before the end of the season, any special units in the Hall of Heroes will be converted into your new civilization's special units when they are returned to you. 
5. In addition to the 30% of units returned to you, you can use Troop Return Scrolls to receive up to 10% extra (each Troop Return Scroll adds 1%).
6. You can use Troop Return Scrolls on yourself, or to help an ally. 
7. If you use a Troop Return Scroll on an ally, but the ally leaves the alliance before claiming their units, your Troop Return Scroll will be returned to you via mail. 
8. If you immigrate before the end of the season, you can still receive 30% of your dead units and {p1}% of your healing resources. If you used a Troop Return Scroll, however, you will not receive the extra units. 
9. Troop Return Scrolls can only be used during their corresponding season. At the end of the season, unused Return Scrolls will be converted into 500 Gems each.0
