Announcement Stage
Kingdom Activity Level: {p1}
Manage Kingdom
Barbarian legends tell that the Great Ziggurat was once a stairway leading directly to the realm of the gods. The Ziggurat is the meeting place of the most powerful barbarian chieftains in the entire Lost Kingdom, and is guarded by their most elite warriors. For thousands of years, all those who have thought to conquer the Great Ziggurat have perished beneath its walls.
1. The Great Ziggurat will open for contention every 3 days. During this period, all coalitions can attack the Ziggurat. Any coalition that can capture and hold the Ziggurat for 8 hours will gain control of it.
2. The first commander (captain of the rallied army) to occupy the Great Ziggurat will become the defense captain responsible for repelling enemy attacks. If this commander is withdrawn, the commander with the highest power will automatically succeed the position.
3. All units who are severely wounded during Great Ziggurat battles will die immediately (during battles with barbarians, all severely wounded units will be sent to the Hospital).
4. The Great Ziggurat will be opened for contention at the same time as its four Citadels. If a Citadel and the Great Ziggurat are occupied by different coalitions, the troops garrisoned in the Great Ziggurat will take massive damage every 5 minutes.
Situated at the heart of England, the Cathedral and Parish Church of the Blessed Virgin Mary (also known as "Southwell Minster") is a sacred site for the English and a mighty-fine prize for the Vikings. To control it would symbolize control over Nottinghamshire and the River Trent, the vital waterway that divides Northern and Southern England
1. Southwell Minster will open for contention every 3 days. During this period, all coalitions can attack the Minster. Any coalition that can capture and hold Southwell Minster for 8 hours will gain control of it.
2. The first commander (captain of the rallied army) to occupy Southwell Minster will become the defense captain, responsible for repelling enemy attacks. If this commander is withdrawn, the commander garrisoned at Southwell Minster with the highest power will automatically succeed them.
3. All units who are severely wounded during Southwell Minster battles will die immediately (during battles with barbarians, all severely wounded units will be sent to the Hospital).S

1. Each Strategy Tree has 4 levels: <b>Basic</b>, <b>Intermediate</b>, <b>Advanced</b>, and <b>Ultimate</b>. Before you can adopt more advanced Strategies, you must adopt a Strategy from the level before it.
2. You can only choose <b>one Strategy</b> from each level of a Strategy Tree.
3. Different Strategies cost different amounts of Strategy Points to adopt. Basic Strategies cost <b>1</b>, Intermediate Strategies cost <b>2</b>, Advanced Strategies cost <b>3</b>, and Ultimate Strategies cost <b>4</b> Strategy Points.
4. You <b>cannot undo</b> a Seasonal Strategy selection once developed, so choose wisely!
5. You can earn up to <b>17</b> Strategy Points: 12 from the Lost Kingdom Chronicles and 5 from Crusader Achievements and the Midseason Review event.M
1. After the Team-Up Phase begins, kingdoms can start forming teams with each other. Each team can have up to 4 kingdoms.
2. Kings can choose to create their own team or join an existing one.
3. Kingdoms will be categorized into one of four classes: A, B, C, or D. Teams must be made up of a certain combination of kingdom classes.
4. Kings can change teams or create their own at any time before the end of the Team-up Phase.
5. After the Team-Up Phase ends, kingdoms that do not yet belong to a team will be assigned randomly to a team they meet the requirements for.
6. After the Team-Up Phase ends, teams of the same type will be matched against each other based on various factors.
7. Matched teams will be randomly assigned to either Camp Solaria or Camp Lunaria.
8. You cannot immigrate to a kingdom if its team is undergoing matchmaking.]
In the Heroic Anthem story, Crystal technologies are getting a major upgrade:
- Technologies now unlock in a more efficient order. Unit Capacity will be unlocked earlier, allowing governors to bring their full force to bear in early battles.
- The new Crystal technologies system splits unit-specific buffs into separate branches. Focus on your favorite unit types and skip the ones you don't need
no more wasting resources just to unlock the next technology.
- Some QoL technologies will be introduced, such as the ability to summon a rune of your choice or quickly return defeated troops to your city.

Though the core content of this story update is now complete, the number of changes means there may be some areas that require urgent improvement. We are hoping to use this as an opportunity to provide you with a sneak peek, collect feedback, and craft a more enjoyable story. This Pioneer experience is planned to be an infrequent event.
You can share any thoughts or issues with us by going to Settings -> Customer Support. We look carefully at all our governors' suggestions, and will be sure to issue a reward afterwards.
In addition to ordinary story rewards, governors who take part in the newest story will be eligible for basic Pioneer rewards and participation rewards.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), all Level 8 Passes throughout the Lost Kingdom will enter a countdown. After {p1} hours, they will become <b>semi-protected</b>. A semi-protected Pass can only be attacked by alliances from its controlling camp. Alliances from other camps cannot attack it, nor can they use it to enter the controlling camp's provinces.
<b>Inscription Shop</b>
You can use an Autarch Testimony to unlock the whole shop and Conquest Coins to redeem rewards. Unused Autarch Testimonies will be lost at the end of the season.
1. All participating kingdoms and domains will be divided into classes based on their overall strength.
2. S-class kingdoms and domains are so powerful that they will not have any teammates.
3. A and B-class kingdoms and domains can form teams of <b>4</b>, which must consist of 2 A and 2 B-class kingdoms or domains. C and D-class kingdoms and domains can form teams of <b>6</b>, which must consist of 3 C and 3 D-class kingdoms or domains.
4. For the sake of fairness, each Lost Kingdom will consist of either only S, A, and B-class kingdoms/domains, or only C and D-class kingdoms/domains.
5. Once the team-up phase ends, teams that do not have enough members will be automatically combined with each other.
6. When combining teams, the system will adhere to team composition requirements in a way that covers missing class types. For example, if you have a team of 1 A-class and 1 B-class kingdom/domain, you will not be paired with a team of 2 A-class kingdoms/domains; however, a team of 2 A-class and 1 B-class kingdoms/domains will be combined with a single B-class kingdom/domain.
7. Before entering the Lost Kingdom, 4 teams of similar strength will be matched against each other and divided into 4 camps.
1. Kings and domain regents can either choose to create their own team, or join an existing team.
2. Kingdoms and domains will be divided into S, A, B, C, or D-class. Teams must consist of either 1 S-class; 2 A-class + 2 B-class; or 3 C-class + 3 D-class. Each Lost Kingdom will consist of either S, A, and B-class kingdoms/domains, or C and D-class kingdoms/domains.
3. Until the end of the team-up phase, kings and domain regents will be free to create teams, invite other kingdoms/domains to join their team, approve applications from other kingdoms/domains to join their team, remove team members, edit the team announcement, or disband their team.
4. After the team-up phase ends, the team list will be locked and the above actions will be unavailable. Teams that lack the required members will be automatically combined. The system will adhere to team composition requirements in a way that covers missing class types. For example, if you have a team of 1 A-class and 1 B-class kingdom/domain, you will not be paired with a team of 2 A-class kingdoms/domains; however, a team of 2 A-class and 1 B-class kingdoms/domains will be combined with a single B-class kingdom/domain.
5. After the team-up phase, teams of the same type will be matched against each other based on overall strength.
6. In each Lost Kingdom, 4 teams of similar strength will be matched against each other and divided into 4 camps.
7. After registration, this kingdom will not be able to accept any new immigrants.

In this story, Crystal technologies are getting a major upgrade:
- Technologies now unlock in a more sensible order. Troop capacity techs will be unlocked earlier, allowing governors to bring their full force to bear in early battles.
- The new Crystal technologies system splits unit-specific buffs into separate branches. Focus on your favorite unit types and skip the ones you don't need
no more wasting resources just to unlock the next technology.
- Some QoL technologies will be introduced, such as the ability to summon a rune of your choice or quickly return defeated troops to your city.

In the King of All Britain story, governors and their kingdoms will be grouped into different camps. Each camp must contain at least 1 kingdom. In order to guarantee fair matchmaking, the total power of each camp will be automatically balanced by the system. When attacking another governor in your camp, you cannot plunder their resources.
1. You can send your troops to join a rallied army or garrison as a "standby troop".
2. During combat, whenever a rallied army or garrison loses an active troop, it will be automatically replaced by a standby troop in order (starting with the first standby troop to join the rally).

SONG OF TROY
Song of Troyj
Sing, O Goddess! Sing not only of the rage of Achilles, but of Agamemnon's vanity and Hector's duty. For when foolish Paris made off with the bride of Menelaus, it was proud Agamemnon who called the Hellenes to war.
From the crags and coasts of Greece sails a mighty host, oathbound to avenge this insult and return Queen Helen to her husband. Mycenaean, Spartan, Thessalian, and Ithacan alike have set aside their differences in the name of Achaean brotherhood, but there are some divisions that even a common enemy cannot mend...
Across the Aegean, their foes are not content to sit and wait. Mighty Troy has called the banners. Allies, cousins, and even strangers flock to their side, knowing that a united front is all that stands between their glory and oblivion.
High above, the gods watch on, embroiled in their own game beyond mortal ken. Who shall the scales favor?
Pantheon
You can choose a Greek deity as your Patron. Your chosen Patron will provide you with unique buffs and special skills. Patron skills require sufficient Faith to cast.
As the Chronicles progress, you'll have a chance to change your Patron.
Trojan Horses
After your camp occupies its Crusader Fortress, it will automatically start building Trojan Horses. These can be allocated to the alliances in your camp. Trojan Horses can move to any accessible location in the Lost Kingdom (except for Altar of Darkness provinces) and be turned into a Horse Fort (except for in the King's Land). The Horse Fort's territory will be counted as valid alliance territory. While a Horse Fort is under construction, it acts as "temporary" alliance territory. Members of your coalition can teleport into its territory, but their cities won't be protected from attack. Your camp can have up to 5 Trojan Horses/Horse Forts.
Olympian Arena#
As the Chronicles progress, the Olympian Arena will be opened periodically. During the duel period, you can choose a side to fight for and dispatch troops to the Arena. Your dispatched troops will be controlled by the computer and will automatically fight other troops in one-on-one battles.
Aeolia
In the northern plains of Greece lies the region of Aeolia
or Thessaly
the homeland of mighty Achilles. Under his command, the unfailingly loyal Myrmidons march fearlessly into battle.
Mycenae
The beating heart of Bronze Age Greek culture, Mycenae is the unquestioned hegemon of southern Greece. Rich in resources and brave men, the disciplined armies of Mycenae are commanded by King Agamemnon, supreme leader of the Greek coalition.
Dardania
Located north of Troy upon the Hellespont, Dardania is the ancient homeland of Troy's royal family. Known for their valor, the Dardanians are led by brave Prince Aeneas, son of Aphrodite and a distant cousin of the Trojan princes.
Lycia
Dotting the southwest coast of Asia Minor is a loose confederation of wealthy city-states. Led by the demigod hero Sarpedon, the Lycians are some of Troy's staunchest allies.O
Though the core content of this story update is now complete, the amount of new content means there may be some areas that require improvement. We are hoping to use this as an opportunity to provide you with a sneak peek, collect feedback, and craft a more enjoyable story. This Pioneer experience is planned to be an infrequent event.I
In the Song of Troy story, you can experience new game mechanics including the Pantheon, Trojan Horses, the Olympian Arena, and exclusive Artifacts.
Pantheon: Select a Greek deity as your Patron to receive their blessings. Your chosen Patron will provide you with unique buffs and special skills.
Trojan Horses: After your camp occupies its Crusader Fortress, it will automatically build Trojan Horses that can be allocated to alliances in your camp. Trojan Horses can move across the Lost Kingdom map and be turned into a Horse Fort. The Horse Fort's territory will be counted as valid alliance territory.
Olympian Arena: As the Chronicles progress, the Olympian Arena will be opened periodically. During the duel period, you can choose a side to fight for and dispatch troops to the Arena. Your dispatched troops will be controlled by the computer and will automatically fight other troops in one-on-one battles.
Exclusive Artifacts: As the Chronicles progress, you will unlock exclusive Artifacts for Achilles and Hector. When equipped, the effects of their skills will apply to all unit types.&
In the Song of Troy story, the battle schedule will be roughly as follows (specific days are subject to change): 
On Day 8, governors from different camps will meet for the first time. On Day 15, the first Olympian Arena round will begin. On Day 19, camps will be able to fight for control over the Altars of Darkness. On Day 30, the second Olympian Arena round will begin. On Day 32, all the camps will be connected to one another. On Day 36, the final battle for the city of Troy will commence. On Day 44, the final Olympian Arena round will begin.
You can share any thoughts or issues with us by going to Settings -> Customer Support. We pay close attention to all feedback we receive and will send you an in-game reward.

Leadership of the alliance occupying your camp's Crusader Fortress can allocate Trojan Horses to other alliances in your camp.

Poseidon's patronage grants you the following benefits:
1. A number of free Barbarian Troop kill rewards every day.
2. Ability to inflict debilitating Slow effects on enemy troops.
3. Enhanced overall survivability of your troops.
Athena's patronage grants you the following benefits:
1. Obtain Wisdom Scrolls that provide unique buffs and can be shared with allies.
2. Aura skills that can be activated on the battlefield to provide AoE buffs.
Artemis's patronage grants you the following benefits:
1. High battlefield mobility.
2. Instant removal of resource points and Barbarian Forts from the Lost Kingdom map.
3. Teleportation to another location in the same province for you and your allies' troops.
Apollo's patronage grants you the following benefits:
1. Enhanced strength and tactical flexibility for your ranged troops.
2. Unique combat modes for your ranged troops.

Reward Preview
Olympian Arenam
<b><size=30>Olympian Arena</size></b>
<color=#997336>A clash of heroes, a battle for glory! Who shall win the favor of Lady Nike?</color>
 <b>Overview</b>
After the Olympian Arena opens, periodic competitions will be held. You can dispatch troops to fight for either Ares or Hades. Your troops will be controlled by the computer, fighting against other troops in one-on-one battles by order.
 <b>How to Play</b>
1. The Olympian Arena will be unlocked as the Chronicles progress. Each round will have its own rewards.
2. At any time during the preparation and duel phase, you can join a side's duel queue by dispatching a troop to that side's Olympian Teleporter. You can only switch sides if you have no troops in your current side's duel queue.
3. The troops you dispatch can only use units currently in your city. Your dispatched troops will instantly teleport to your chosen Olympian Teleporter.
4. Troops in the Olympian Arena will fight each other in one-on-one duels. The winning troop will recover all of its slightly wounded units and remain in the Arena.
 <b>Important Notes</b>
1. A troop must consist of 200,000+ Tier 4/5 units to fight in the Olympian Arena.
2. Troops that fight in the Olympian Arena can be severely wounded, though at a slightly lower rate than normal. While the Olympian Arena is open, your Hospital Capacity in Lost Kingdom will increase by {p1}%.
Preparation Phase: {p1}+
Earn {p1} kill points in the Olympian Arena
Duel Phase: {p1}
Event Ends In: {p1}
If you rank in the top 100 of kill point rankings, you'll receive a rank reward. The higher your rank, the better your rewards. Rewards will be sent by mail at the end of the event.

Other Effects
Trojan Horses and Horse Forts
Trojan Horse
Trojan Horses can move to any accessible location in the Lost Kingdom and be converted into a Horse Fort, granting them immense strategic value.
Horse Fort
A territory building created by a Trojan Horse. Horse Forts can act as a starting point for alliance territory, but other alliances can attack it even if they don't have adjacent territory.
If your camp has occupied its Crusader Fortress, the leader/Counselor of the alliance occupying it can assign Trojan Horses to your camp's alliances.

1. After your camp occupies its Crusader Fortress, it will build a Trojan Horse every {p2} hours (up to a maximum of {p1} Horses).
2. If your camp has the maximum amount of Trojan Horses, your Crusader Fortress will only start building a new one if one of your Trojan Horses or Horse Forts is destroyed or dismantled.
3. The leader and Counselor of the alliance occupying the Crusader Fortress can assign idle Trojan Horses to alliances in your camp that have unlocked the Trojan Horse alliance skill.
4. If there are no troops garrisoned in an assigned Trojan Horse, the leader or Counselor of the alliance occupying the Crusader Fortress can recall it.

1. If your Trojan Horse has garrisoned troops and its status is set to Mobile, the garrison captain can order it to move. Trojan Horses cannot enter Altar of Darkness provinces.
2. The status of your Trojan Horse can be controlled by the leader or Counselor of the alliance occupying your camp's Crusader Fortress.

4. Trojan Horses can move through Passes belonging to your coalition.
5. Trojan Horses cannot enter strongholds.

CONVERT
A territory building created by a Trojan Horse. Horse Forts can act as a starting point for alliance territory, but other alliances can attack it even if they don't have adjacent territory. Horse Forts can't be built in the King's Land.
1. While a Horse Fort is under construction, it acts as "temporary" alliance territory. Members of your coalition can teleport into its territory, but their cities won't be protected from attack.
2. If the Horse Fort is set on fire while still under construction, it will immediately be destroyed.
3. A completed Horse Fort will protect all allied cities in its territory from attack and can act as a starting point for other alliance territory buildings. If it is bordering another alliance's stronghold, you can attack that stronghold.
4. Other alliances can attack your Horse Forts even if they don't have territory adjacent to it.
5. Horse Forts can't be built in the King's Land.
Moves your city to the temporary territory of an incomplete Horse Fort. This temporary territory provides no protection, leaving your city vulnerable to attack. Proceed with caution!

Troy
The magnificent city of Troy, also known as Ilios, sits on the shores of the Dardanelles. Legend has it that Apollo and Poseidon themselves built its towering walls, making it easy to defend and daunting to attack. Many of the finest heroes now lay beneath the dirt, having given their lives to claim this prize.
1. The city of Troy will be open for contention every 3 days. During this period, all coalitions can attack the city. Any coalition that can capture and hold Troy for 8 hours will gain control of it. 
2. Whenever a troop captures Troy, its commander (or captain if it's a rallied army) will become the city's garrison captain, responsible for repelling enemy attacks. If this commander is withdrawn, the commander with the highest power in the garrison will automatically take over the position. 
3. All units that are severely wounded in Troy battles will die immediately. If severely wounded by barbarians, however, units will be sent to the Hospital instead.+
Hecatomb Coins: Recover 1 coin every {p1}m.

Welcome to the Pantheon, governor. In this hallowed temple, you can select a <color=#ffa800>Patron deity</color>.
Your Patron will grant you unique <color=#ffa800>active skills</color> and other <color=#ffa800>blessings</color>. Tap here to preview the effects of each skill.
After choosing a Patron, you <color=#ffa800>won't be able to change it for some time</color>. As the Chronicles progress, you'll have <color=#ffa800>one chance to change your Patron</color>.a
Choose carefully! The blessings of your Patron can mean the difference between doom and conquest!y
After choosing a Patron, you must earn <color=#ffa800>Patron EXP</color> to acquire <color=#ffa800>Patron Points</color>.{
Completing Bastion quests, fighting in the Olympian Arena, and taking part in the Midseason Review can earn you Patron EXP.~
Earning Patron EXP will grant you <color=#ffa800>Patron Points</color>, which you can use to unlock and upgrade Patron skills.
To use active Patron skills, you'll also need <color=#ffa800>Hecatomb Coins</color>. You'll gain Hecatomb Coins naturally over time.b
Lastly, you'll need sufficient <color=#ffa800>Faith</color> to enjoy the blessings of your Patron.
The amount of Faith required will <color=#ffa800>increase daily</color>. You can earn it through a variety of daily activities.
In this Lost Kingdom, when your camp captures its <color=#ffa800>Crusader Fortress</color>, you will unlock an exciting new ability: <color=#ffa800>Trojan Horses</color>.f
Once your camp has occupied its Crusader Fortress, it will automatically start building Trojan Horses.
The <color=#ffa800>leader and Counselor</color> of the alliance occupying the Crusader Fortress can assign completed Trojan Horses to other alliances in your camp.{
Trojan Horses can be garrisoned with troops. The <color=#ffa800>garrison captain</color> can control the Horse's movements.
Melee troops can attack the Trojan Horse, dealing a <color=#ffa800>fixed amount of Durability damage</color>. The troops garrisoned in the Horse will not be harmed.
<color=#ffa800>The leader and Counselor</color> of the alliance occupying the Crusader Fortress can manage settings to prevent Trojan Horses from moving.

Rules
Intro
Stage Rewards6
 <b>Story</b>
In ancient Egrian legends, Heliamber was said to be the condensed essence of the Sun Above, imbued with incredible solar energy. When carried into battle, the gemstone greatly enhances a soldier's strength and courage.
Acquiring Heliamber is no easy feat, for it can only be found in the mysterious Daybreak Canyon. The barbarians occupying the canyon guard the Heliamber jealously, as they too draw power from its presence. Few have the strength to overcome these mighty warriors.
Those who do, however, will find themselves rewarded with the power of the sun itself.
 <b>Valor</b>
The level of your Heliamber Mines determines the Valor level of your troops.
In Daybreak Trials battles:
 - If your Valor is higher than your enemy's, your troops deal more damage and take less damage.
 - If the enemy's Valor is higher, enemy troops deal more damage and take less damage.
When attacking Barbarian Forts/Holy Sites:
 - If your Valor is higher than your enemy's, your troops deal more damage and take less damage.
 <b>Combat</b>
You can deploy up to 3 troops to the Daybreak Canyon.
Your battles in the Daybreak Trials will not affect your units outside of the event.
 <b>Rewards</b>
You'll earn a reward the first time you clear each stage. You can also claim daily rewards for every stage you've cleared.

Rules
Merger Rules
Merger Changes
Special Rewards
Fates Intertwined Event$
Once your kingdom has been merged, you'll gain a few temporary perks to help your alliance get back on its feet. Alliance Flags will have increased building speed, your alliance's member limit will be raised, and the Ark of Osiris Golden Battlefield will have fewer registration restrictions.
Stronghold Reset
After the merger, the kingdom map will be fully reset. Strongholds will be reverted to "Not Open" and must be unlocked again via the Kingdom Chronicles. Occupying a reset stronghold for the first time will earn you first occupation rewards.
Kingdom Info Adjustment#
Once the merger is complete, the new kingdom will undergo a few changes:
- The new kingdom will use the name and age of the captain kingdom. By default, the Kingdom Overview page will display the info of the captain kingdom, but you'll still be able to view the historical info of all member kingdoms.
- All kingdom titles (including king) will be reset.
- Many events (e.g. The Mightiest Governor, Hall of Fame, etc) will inherit their history from the captain kingdom, but you'll still be able to view the historical info of all member kingdoms.
Alliance Info Adjustment
After the merger, alliances in the merged kingdoms will be adjusted:
- Alliances from the merged kingdoms will carry over into the new kingdom.
- All of your alliance's Center Fortresses, Fortresses, Flags, and Resource Centers will be cleared from the map. The resources used to construct these buildings will be returned to your alliance.
- All alliance territory will be cleared and alliance resource points will be unoccupied.:

Though the core content of this story update is now complete, the amount of new content means there may be some areas that require urgent improvement. We are hoping to use this as an opportunity to provide you with a sneak peek, collect feedback, and craft a more enjoyable story. This Pioneer experience is planned to be an infrequent event.
Chronicles chapters cannot be skipped during this story. Passes will open 13 hours after their corresponding Chronicles chapter ends.
You can share any thoughts or issues with us by going to Settings -> Customer Support. We look carefully at all our governors' suggestions, and will be sure to issue a reward afterwards.
In addition to ordinary story rewards, governors who take part in the newest story will be eligible for basic Pioneer rewards and participation rewards.
1. After Crusader Fortresses have been occupied and the corresponding Chronicles chapter ends, voting will commence to determine the time that Level 4 passes will become semi-protected.
2. Alliance leaders who have occupied Crusader Fortresses have the right to vote. Each camp can vote once, and must select at least 3 time slots.
3. If the vote is a draw, then a time slot will be chosen at random from the time slots with the most votes.
4. After Level 4 passes are unlocked and the corresponding Chronicles chapter ends, they will become semi-protected the day after. The time slots that can be voted for are 02:00, 08:00, 12:00, 16:00 and 20:00 UTC.
5. After Crusader Fortresses are occupied and the corresponding Chronicles chapter ends, voting will take place over the next 24 hours. You can change your selected time slots at any time during this period.
6. Results will be published after the 24-hour period ends.
7. The Chronicles chapter cannot be skipped. Remaining passes will become semi-protected or open up 13 hours after the chapter ends.

1. In each voting session, 3 preferred time slots must be selected out of 5 available options.
2. The chosen time slots will be designated as first/second/third priority based on the selected order.
3. When there are multiple time slots preferred by both sides, the most suitable time slot will be chosen based on priority weighting. If the time slot cannot be determined by weighting, one will be selected randomly for the match.
4. The preferred time slots can be changed at any time before the end of the voting period.
5. Only alliance leaders who are occupying Crusader Fortresses have the right to vote.

Level 4 Pass Voting
After the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), all Level 4 passes in the Lost Kingdom will enter a voting phase. Alliance leaders who occupy Crusader Fortresses have the right to vote.
At the end of this chapter (whether or not the objective is achieved), all Level 4 passes throughout the Lost Kingdom will become semi-protected at a time determined by voting. Semi-protected passes can only be attacked by coalitions from the controlling camp. Governors from other camps cannot attack semi-protected passes, nor can they pass through to enter outer provinces corresponding to the controlling camp.

To ensure that season stories can be experienced in a fair and orderly environment, and to safeguard the quality of gameplay for all governors, we hereby request that all participating governors and alliance/kingdom officers acknowledge these key principles laid out below:
1. If the server or game encounters a <b>critical issue</b> that disrupts fairness, we reserve the right to terminate stories at any time. In such cases, holistic evaluations will be made prior to termination based on traceable technical data and backend logs to the best of our abilities.
2. If a critical issue occurs but ongoing stories are not terminated, we reserve the right to assess impacted parties and decide whether to <b>reload game data (to an earlier saved point)</b>.
- Critical issues may include but are not limited to: server instability, game-breaking bugs, serious abuse of loopholes or design flaws, cheating through illegal programs or private deals, etc.
- Reloadable game data includes but is not limited to: alliance building data, neutral map building data, location data of governor cities, etc.
- To ensure that governors' normal game experience remains as smooth as possible, any reloading that occurs will adopt the most minimal and precise scope when applied.
3. In addition to the critical issues mentioned above, when abnormal behavior is clearly detected in alliance/kingdom management (such as maliciously dismantling alliance/kingdom buildings on the map without any tactical purpose, or disbanding an alliance without warning) in such a way that severely affects other story participants, we reserve the right to decide whether to <b>reload game data (to an earlier saved point)</b> based on the extent of impact.
4. If any alliance/kingdom participating in stories believes there to be a critical issue disrupting fairness of the game, leaders and officers of relevant parties may reach out to Customer Support. We ask that you provide detailed reports and evidence to the best of your abilities when submitting feedback, so that we may quickly locate potential issues.
5. In cases of critical issues and abnormal behavior, we will send further instructions and decisions via in-game mail within 72 hours. If there is any conflict or ambiguity between communication channels, the content of the in-game mail will prevail.

<b><size=30>Olympian Arena</size></b>
<color=#997336>A clash of heroes, a battle for glory! Who shall win the favor of Lady Nike?</color>
 <b>Event Overview</b>
After the Olympian Arena opens, periodic competitions will be held. You can dispatch troops to fight for either Ares or Hades. Your troops will be controlled by the computer, fighting against other troops in one-on-one battles.
 <b>Event Rules</b>
1. The Olympian Arena will be unlocked as the Chronicles progress. Each round will have its own rewards.
2. At any time during the preparation and duel phase, you can join a side's duel queue by dispatching a troop to that side's Olympian Teleporter. You can only switch sides if you have no troops in your current side's duel queue.
3. The troops you dispatch can only use units currently in your city. Your dispatched troops will instantly teleport to your chosen Olympian Teleporter.
4. Troops in the Olympian Arena will fight each other in one-on-one duels. The winning troop will recover 85% of its slightly wounded units and remain in the Arena.
 <b>Important Notes</b>
1. A troop must consist of 200,000+ Tier 4/5 units to fight in the Olympian Arena.
2. Troops that fight in the Olympian Arena can be severely wounded, though at a slightly lower rate than normal. While the Olympian Arena is open, your Hospital Capacity in the Lost Kingdom will increase by {p1}%.-

Greetings Governor,
In this round of registration for the Lost Kingdom story, your kingdom's {p1} domain was too powerful for a suitable opponent to be found. Please rearrange domain members for a more balanced distribution among the kingdom. Otherwise, this domain is at risk of failing to find a match.x
1. Your kingdom's king must appoint regents to manage each Lost Kingdom domain. Regents will help the king create a battle roster for their respective domains, which includes all the governors and alliances that will be allowed to fully take part in the Lost Kingdom.
2. Each domain has up to <b>{p1} slots</b> it can allocate. A governor must receive a slot to fully take part in the Lost Kingdom. The king or their regents must first allocate slots to alliances. Alliance leaders can then assign these slots to their individual members.
3. At the end of the story by-election phase, if there are any open slots left, they will be automatically assigned to the governors with the<b> highest activity points and power</b> in the kingdom that do not already have a slot. When the system automatically assigns slots, <b>no kingdom can have more than {p2} slots in a single Lost Kingdom</b>.

When you choose to teleport to a new location while already in the Lost Kingdom, you can only choose locations within your kingdom's territory. When your city is in alliance territory, it cannot be attacked (troops on the map but not in your city do not benefit from this protection). Cities located in territory taken over by another camp will be forcibly teleported to their starting province. This will not affect you if you are under the effect of the Imprison king skill, and actively teleporting to another kingdom's territory (e.g. through a Random Teleport) will not trigger this effect.
1. The order in which each camp selects its starting province is based on the camp rankings in the Eve of the Crusade event. The highest-ranked camp gets to select first.
2. During the Eve of the Crusade event, the king of the kingdom with the highest kingdom score in each camp will get to select their camp's starting province.
3. When selecting a starting province for your camp, you can change your choice of province at any time before your selection time ends.

<b>Registration Rules</b>
1. Kings can choose their kingdom's story. Once the king of a kingdom has chosen a story and registered, all governors of that kingdom will be considered registered.
2. Once initial registration has ended, if the story your kingdom registered for does not meet the requirements to begin, your kingdom will enter a by-election phase and your registration will be canceled. Your kingdom can register for another story during the by-election phase. By-elections only happen once, so choose carefully!
3. There will be multiple camps in each story. Camps may be comprised of multiple kingdoms.
4. Kingdoms that do not register this time will be automatically registered for a random story during the next registration period.
5. All kingdoms entering the Season of Conquest for the first time will be automatically placed in the Heroic Anthem story. Once all kingdoms are ready, matchmaking will begin.
6. Kingdoms that have endured intense battles and suffered significant losses will be granted additional time to recover and regroup, along with an extra chance to skip story registration.
